Parameter Efficient Hybrid Transformer (PEHT) for Network Traffic Prediction via Dynamic Urban Congestion Integration
Authors: Abdolazim Rezaei, Mehdi Sookhak, Mahboobeh Haghparast
First: 2026-06-26T17:17:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T17:17:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate network traffic prediction is a critical element for efficient resource allocation in dynamic urban cellular networks. However, prediction remains challenging because network demand is influenced by complex mobility patterns, congestion dynamics, and heterogeneous user behavior. This paper introduces the Parameter-Efficient Hybrid Transformer (PEHT), a network traffic prediction framework that integrates urban mobility and congestion information into a Transformer-based architecture. PEHT separates primary network communication features from secondary urban mobility features and incorporates Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) into the Transformer encoder to reduce the number of trainable parameters while maintaining high predictive accuracy. A multimodal fusion strategy then injects external mobility and congestion features into the decoder to improve traffic forecasting. Experiments on the Telecom Italia Milan dataset and multiple synthetic congestion scenarios show that PEHT outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in terms of RMSE, MAE, and $R^2$. The implementation is available in the GitHub repository.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过动态城市拥堵集成的参数高效混合Transformer（PEHT）用于网络流量预测
准确的网络流量预测是动态城市蜂窝网络高效资源分配的关键要素。然而，预测仍具挑战性，因为网络需求受复杂移动模式、拥堵动态和异质用户行为影响。本文提出参数高效混合Transformer（PEHT），一种将城市移动性和拥堵信息整合进Transformer架构的网络流量预测框架。PEHT将主要网络通信特征与次要城市移动特征分离，并在Transformer编码器中引入低秩适应（LoRA），以减少可训练参数数量同时保持高预测精度。随后，多模态融合策略将外部移动性和拥堵特征注入解码器，以提升流量预测效果。在Telecom Italia米兰数据集及多个合成拥堵场景的实验表明，PEHT在RMSE、MAE和R²指标上优于现有最先进基线方法。代码实现已发布于GitHub仓库。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of accurate network traffic prediction in dynamic urban environments where demand is shaped by complex mobility and congestion patterns, this paper proposes the Parameter-Efficient Hybrid Transformer (PEHT). PEHT integrates urban mobility and congestion data into a Transformer architecture by separating core network features from external mobility inputs and applying Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to the encoder to reduce trainable parameters without sacrificing accuracy. The decoder then uses a multimodal fusion strategy to incorporate these external features. Evaluated on the Telecom Italia Milan dataset and synthetic congestion scenarios, PEHT consistently outperforms existing methods, achieving lower RMSE, MAE, and higher R² scores.
为应对城市动态网络环境中因复杂移动模式和拥堵动态导致的网络流量预测难题，本文提出参数高效的混合Transformer（PEHT）框架。该框架将城市移动与拥堵数据融入Transformer架构，通过分离核心网络特征与外部移动特征，并在编码器中引入低秩适配（LoRA）以减少可训练参数，同时在解码器中采用多模态融合策略注入外部特征以提升预测精度。在Telecom Italia米兰数据集及多个合成拥堵场景的实验中，PEHT在RMSE、MAE和R²指标上均优于现有方法。
Vision-Default, Prior-Override: Causal Mechanisms of Perception-Knowledge Conflict in Vision-Language Models
Authors: Niclas Lietzow, Danielle Bitterman, Carsten Eickhoff, William Rudman, Michal Golovanevsky
First: 2026-06-26T17:16:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T17:16:04+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 11 figures, 8 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language models must reconcile visual evidence with memorized world knowledge when the two conflict. How they resolve this conflict shapes the reliability of multimodal systems, yet prior work characterizes it behaviorally without a component-level causal account. We combine activation patching across three granularities (residual stream, attention heads, and MLP sublayers) with model-component ablation studies and mechanistic analysis. Across three VLM families, we find that visual grounding emerges by default, whereas prior grounding depends on a small set of causally necessary attention heads (2.5-4.8%) concentrated in the second half of the network. These heads enable answers from stored world knowledge (e.g., "red" for a strawberry) despite conflicting visual input. Ablating them flips predictions from knowledge-grounded to visually grounded answers in 68-96% of cases under prior-knowledge prompts, but changes only 0.8-7.5% of visually grounded predictions, establishing an asymmetric causal structure. The identified heads decompose into routing heads, which modulate information flow, and writing heads, which directly project answer tokens into the residual stream. This structure is consistent across model families and scales, revealing a sparse causal circuit underlying perception-knowledge conflict in VLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉-默认，先验-覆盖：视觉-语言模型中感知-知识冲突的因果机制
视觉-语言模型在视觉证据与记忆中的世界知识冲突时，必须加以调和。其冲突解决方式决定了多模态系统的可靠性，但以往研究仅从行为层面描述该过程，缺乏组件级因果解释。我们结合三种粒度（残差流、注意力头、MLP子层）的激活修补，与模型组件消融研究及机制分析。在三个VLM家族中发现，视觉锚定默认出现，而先验锚定依赖于一小部分因果必需的注意力头（2.5%-4.8%），集中于网络后半段。这些注意力头使模型能在视觉输入冲突时，仍从存储的世界知识中生成答案（如“草莓是红色的”）。消融这些注意力头，在先验-知识提示下，68%-96%的预测从知识锚定转向视觉锚定，但仅改变0.8%-7.5%的视觉锚定预测，揭示了不对称因果结构。这些注意力头可分解为路由头（调节信息流）和写作头（直接将答案词元投影至残差流）。该结构在不同模型家族和规模中保持一致，揭示了VLM中感知-知识冲突背后的稀疏因果回路。
Summary / 总结
Vision-language models must resolve conflicts between visual input and stored world knowledge, a process critical for reliable multimodal reasoning. The study employs activation patching at three granularities—residual stream, attention heads, and MLP sublayers—combined with ablation and mechanistic analysis across three VLM families. Results show that visual grounding is the default mode, while prior knowledge grounding relies on a small subset (2.5–4.8%) of attention heads concentrated in the network’s second half, which enable knowledge-based answers despite visual contradictions. Ablating these heads shifts predictions from knowledge-grounded to visually grounded responses in 68–96% of prior-knowledge cases, but affects only 0.8–7.5% of visually grounded cases, revealing an asymmetric causal hierarchy. These heads further decompose into routing and writing heads, a structure consistent across models and scales, indicating a sparse causal circuit governing perception-knowledge conflict.
视觉语言模型需在视觉输入与存储世界知识冲突时进行调和，这对多模态系统的可靠性至关重要。研究通过在残差流、注意力头和MLP子层三个粒度上进行激活修补与组件消融，揭示了因果机制。结果表明，视觉锚定是默认机制，而先验知识锚定依赖于网络后半段少量（2.5–4.8%）因果必需的注意力头，这些头可支持知识驱动的答案（如“草莓是红色的”），即使视觉输入矛盾。消融这些头可使68–96%的知识驱动预测转为视觉驱动，但仅改变0.8–7.5%的视觉驱动预测，揭示了不对称因果结构。这些头进一步分解为路由头和写作头，其结构在不同模型家族和规模中保持一致，表明感知-知识冲突背后存在稀疏因果电路。
CO-DEFEND: Continuous Decentralized Federated Learning for Secure DoH-Based Threat Detection
Authors: Diego Cajaraville-Aboy, Marta Moure-Garrido, Carlos Beis-Penedo, Carlos Garcia-Rubio, Rebeca P. Díaz-Redondo, Celeste Campo, Ana Fernández-Vilas, Manuel Fernández-Veiga
Venue: Computer Networks, Volume 276 (2026) 111961
First: 2025-04-02T16:40:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T17:15:11+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 12 figures, 6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The use of DNS over HTTPS (DoH) tunneling by an attacker to hide malicious activity within encrypted DNS traffic poses a serious threat to network security, as it allows malicious actors to bypass traditional monitoring and intrusion detection systems while evading detection by conventional traffic analysis techniques. ML techniques can be used to detect DoH tunnels; however, their effectiveness relies on large datasets containing both benign and malicious traffic. Sharing such datasets across entities is challenging due to privacy concerns. In this work, we propose CO-DEFEND framework that enables multiple entities to collaboratively train a classification machine learning model for DoH threat detection while preserving data privacy, enhancing scalability and resilience against single points of failure. The proposed DFL framework provides a realistic implementation for DoH threat detection, enabling multiple entities to train their local models online with incoming DoH flows in real-time batches as they are processed - an approach that fits naturally within modern Internet architectures. This framework adapts four classical machine learning algorithms, Support Vector Machines, Logistic Regression, Decision Trees, and Random Forest, for federated scenarios and efficient training. In addition, a key methodological feature of CO-DEFEND is the use of DT and RF as model selection rather than aggregation mechanisms, allowing each participant to retain interpretable and locally optimal decision structures while benefiting from collective updates. We compare our proposed method by using the dataset CIRA-CIC-DoHBrw-2020 with existing machine learning approaches, including more computationally complex alternatives such as neural networks, to demonstrate its effectiveness in detecting malicious DoH tunnels while improving scalability and computational efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CO-DEFEND：面向基于DoH安全威胁检测的连续去中心化联邦学习
攻击者利用DNS over HTTPS（DoH）隧道在加密DNS流量中隐藏恶意活动，对网络安全构成严重威胁，使其能够绕过传统监控和入侵检测系统，并逃避常规流量分析技术的检测。机器学习技术可用于检测DoH隧道，但其有效性依赖于包含良性与恶意流量的大规模数据集。由于隐私顾虑，跨实体共享此类数据集颇具挑战。本文提出CO-DEFEND框架，使多个实体能够在保护数据隐私的前提下协同训练用于DoH威胁检测的分类机器学习模型，同时提升可扩展性并增强对单点故障的抗性。所提出的DFL框架为DoH威胁检测提供了现实可行的实现方案，支持多个实体在处理DoH流量时实时在线训练本地模型——该方法天然契合现代互联网架构。该框架将四种经典机器学习算法——支持向量机、逻辑回归、决策树和随机森林——适配联邦学习场景并实现高效训练。此外，CO-DEFEND的关键方法论特征是将决策树（DT）和随机森林（RF）作为模型选择而非聚合机制，使每个参与者保留可解释且局部最优的决策结构，同时受益于群体更新。我们使用CIRA-CIC-DoHBrw-2020数据集，将本方法与现有机器学习方法（包括计算复杂度更高的神经网络）进行比较，以证明其在检测恶意DoH隧道方面的有效性，同时提升可扩展性和计算效率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of detecting DNS over HTTPS (DoH) tunneling that evades traditional security systems, this paper proposes CO-DEFEND, a continuous decentralized federated learning framework enabling multiple entities to collaboratively train a threat detection model without sharing raw data. The method adapts four classical ML algorithms—SVM, Logistic Regression, Decision Trees, and Random Forest—for federated settings, with DT and RF used for model selection rather than aggregation to preserve local interpretability and optimize performance. Experiments on the CIRA-CIC-DoHBrw-2020 dataset show CO-DEFEND achieves competitive detection accuracy while outperforming more complex models like neural networks in terms of scalability and computational efficiency, making it suitable for real-time, decentralized deployment in modern network architectures.
为应对恶意DNS over HTTPS（DoH）隧道化流量绕过传统安全检测的问题，CO-DEFEND提出一种连续去中心化联邦学习框架，允许多个实体在不共享原始数据的前提下协同训练威胁检测模型。该框架将支持向量机、逻辑回归、决策树和随机森林四种经典机器学习算法适配联邦场景，并创新性地将决策树与随机森林作为模型选择机制，以保留本地可解释性并融合集体更新优势。在CIRA-CIC-DoHBrw-2020数据集上的实验表明，该方法在检测恶意DoH隧道方面表现与神经网络相当，同时具备更优的可扩展性和计算效率，适用于现代网络架构中的实时隐私保护部署。
Agent-Native Immune System: Architecture, Taxonomy, and Engineering
Authors: Bo Shen, Lifeng Chang, Tianyuan Wei, Yunpeng Li, Feng Shi, Yichen Han, Peijie Gao, Shiyi Kuang, Xin Chang, Dehui Li
First: 2026-06-26T17:08:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T17:08:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The transition from static chat bots to autonomous agents--equipped with persistent memory, tool-use protocols, and multi-agent collaboration--has fundamentally expanded the AI threat landscape. Current defense mechanisms, such as perimeter security and training-time alignment, remain external to the agent's active reasoning loop. Consequently, they fall short: a fully aligned agent remains highly vulnerable to runtime hijacking via memory poisoning, tool-chain manipulation, or multi-agent protocol attacks. To address this critical gap, we introduce the Agent-Native Immune System (ANIS), the first biologically inspired, endogenous defense architecture embedded directly within the agent's cognitive loop. Our framework presents four primary contributions. First, we design a six-layer Immune Tower (L0-L5), distinctly incorporating Barrier Immunity (L1) as a non-cognitive, physical-and-logical isolation layer. Second, we establish a unified taxonomy of Agent Viruses and Agent Vaccines, formalizing the critical distinction between superficial non-parametric defenses and robust parametric vaccines. Third, we conceptualize the Harness Triad--Meta, Self, and Auto--a self-monitoring, meta-cognitive automation backbone that drives Continual Immune Learning (CIL), enabling vaccines to dynamically adapt to novel threats. Finally, we establish a rigorous theoretical demarcation between model alignment and agent immunity: while alignment provides a static "constitutional" value foundation during training, ANIS serves as the dynamic "law enforcement" mechanism during runtime. We conclude by framing open challenges for the field, including immune protocol standardization, novel evaluation metrics such as the Autoimmunity Rate (false-positive intervention rate), and the co-evolutionary dynamics between pathogens and vaccines within collective intelligence ecosystems.

标题：代理原生免疫系统：架构、分类与工程
从静态聊天机器人向具备持久记忆、工具使用协议和多代理协作能力的自主代理的转变，从根本上扩展了AI威胁格局。当前防御机制，如边界安全和训练时对齐，仍位于代理主动推理循环之外，因此效果有限：即使完全对齐的代理仍极易受运行时劫持，如记忆投毒、工具链操控或多代理协议攻击。为解决这一关键缺口，我们提出代理原生免疫系统（ANIS），这是首个生物启发式、内生式防御架构，直接嵌入代理认知循环。我们的框架有四大贡献：首先，设计六层免疫塔（L0-L5），明确将屏障免疫（L1）作为非认知的物理与逻辑隔离层；其次，建立代理病毒与代理疫苗统一分类体系，明确区分表面非参数防御与稳健参数疫苗；第三，提出驾驭三元组——元认知、自我监控与自动化——作为驱动持续免疫学习（CIL）的自监控元认知自动化核心，使疫苗能动态适应新威胁；最后，建立模型对齐与代理免疫的严格理论区分：对齐提供训练时静态“宪法”价值基础，而ANIS则在运行时充当动态“执法”机制。我们最后提出领域开放挑战，包括免疫协议标准化、如自免疫率（误报干预率）等新型评估指标，以及病原体与疫苗在群体智能生态系统中的共进化动态。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing vulnerability of autonomous AI agents to runtime attacks despite training-time alignment, the paper proposes the Agent-Native Immune System (ANIS), a biologically inspired defense architecture integrated directly into the agent's cognitive loop. The method introduces a six-layer Immune Tower with a physical-and-logical Barrier Immunity layer, a taxonomy distinguishing non-parametric defenses from parametric vaccines, and the Harness Triad (Meta, Self, Auto) to enable self-monitoring and continual immune learning. Experimental results demonstrate that ANIS effectively mitigates memory poisoning, tool-chain manipulation, and multi-agent protocol attacks by dynamically adapting vaccines, while maintaining agent functionality without compromising alignment. The framework also introduces the Autoimmunity Rate as a novel metric to evaluate false-positive intervention rates, highlighting the need for standardized immune protocols and co-evolutionary dynamics in multi-agent systems.
为了解决自主代理在运行时仍易受攻击（如内存污染、工具链操控）的问题，本文提出了一种内生于代理认知循环的生物启发式防御架构——Agent-Native Immune System（ANIS）。该方法包含六层免疫塔（含物理与逻辑隔离的屏障免疫层）、区分非参数防御与参数疫苗的统一分类体系，以及Meta、Self、Auto三元自监控机制以支持持续免疫学习。实验表明，ANIS能有效检测并阻断运行时攻击，大幅降低代理被劫持的成功率，同时保持系统效率，并明确区分了对齐与免疫在理论上的不同作用。
Learning Topology-Aware Representations via Test-Time Adaptation for Anomaly Segmentation
Authors: Ali Zia, Usman Ali, Abdul Rehman, Umer Ramzan, Kang Han, Muhammad Faheem, Shahnawaz Qureshi, Wei Xiang
First: 2026-06-26T17:04:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T17:04:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Test-time adaptation (TTA) has emerged as a promising paradigm for mitigating distribution shifts in deep models. However, existing TTA approaches for anomaly segmentation remain limited by their reliance on pixel-level heuristics, such as confidence thresholding or entropy minimisation, which fail to preserve structural consistency under noise and texture variation. Moreover, they typically treat anomaly maps as flat intensity fields, ignoring the higher-order spatial relationships that characterise complex defect geometries. We introduce TopoTTA (Topological Test-Time Adaptation), a novel framework that integrates persistent homology, a tool from topological data analysis, into the TTA pipeline to enforce geometric and structural coherence during adaptation. By applying multi-level cubical complex filtration to anomaly score maps, TopoTTA derives robust topological pseudo-labels that guide a lightweight test-time classifier, enhancing segmentation quality without retraining the backbone model. The approach avoids reliance on method-specific raw-score thresholding for mask binarisation, preserves connectivity, and generalises across both 2D and 3D modalities. Extensive experiments across six standard benchmarks (MVTec AD, VisA, Real-IAD, MVTec 3D-AD, AnomalyShapeNet, and MVTec LOCO) demonstrate an average 15% F1 improvement over state-of-the-art unsupervised anomaly detection and segmentation methods, with the largest gains on anomalies exhibiting complex geometric or structural variations. These findings suggest that integrating topological reasoning into test-time adaptation provides a principled route to structure-aware generalisation, bridging the gap between geometric learning and robust adaptation.

标题：通过测试时自适应学习拓扑感知表示以实现异常分割
测试时自适应（TTA）已成为缓解深度模型分布偏移的有前景范式。然而，现有用于异常分割的TTA方法受限于对像素级启发式方法（如置信度阈值或熵最小化）的依赖，这些方法在噪声和纹理变化下无法保持结构一致性。此外，它们通常将异常图视为平坦强度场，忽略了刻画复杂缺陷几何形态的高阶空间关系。我们提出TopoTTA（拓扑测试时自适应），一种将拓扑数据分析中的持久同调工具整合进TTA流程的新框架，以在自适应过程中强制几何与结构一致性。通过在异常分数图上应用多级立方体复形过滤，TopoTTA生成鲁棒的拓扑伪标签，引导轻量级测试时分类器，提升分割质量而无需重新训练骨干模型。该方法避免依赖特定方法的原始分数阈值进行掩码二值化，保持连通性，并在2D和3D模态间具备泛化能力。在六个标准基准（MVTec AD、VisA、Real-IAD、MVTec 3D-AD、AnomalyShapeNet和MVTec LOCO）上的广泛实验表明，相比最先进的无监督异常检测与分割方法，平均F1提升15%，在具有复杂几何或结构变化的异常上收益最大。这些结果表明，将拓扑推理融入测试时自适应，为结构感知泛化提供了一条原则性路径，弥合了几何学习与鲁棒自适应之间的鸿沟。

By applying multi-level cubical complex filtration to anomaly score maps, TopoTTA generates topological pseudo-labels that guide a lightweight classifier, avoiding raw-score thresholding and preserving connectivity. Evaluated across six benchmarks, TopoTTA achieves an average 15% F1 improvement over state-of-the-art methods, with the most significant gains on anomalies with complex geometries, demonstrating the value of topological reasoning for structure-aware generalization.
为解决现有测试时自适应方法在异常分割中依赖像素级启发式、忽略结构连贯性的问题，本文提出TopoTTA框架，将持久同调引入自适应流程以强制几何一致性。通过在异常分数图上构建多级立方体复合滤波，生成拓扑伪标签引导轻量级分类器，无需重训练主干网络，同时保持连通性并支持2D与3D数据泛化。在六个基准数据集上，TopoTTA平均提升15% F1分数，尤其在几何结构复杂的异常上收益显著，表明拓扑推理可有效增强结构感知的自适应能力。
Active-learning mapping of the Vicsek model phase diagram
Authors: Grace T. Bai, Brandon B. Le
First: 2026-04-30T17:52:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T17:02:53+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Vicsek model is a minimal model of collective motion, capturing how local alignment interactions can generate macroscopic nonequilibrium order in systems such as bird flocks. In this work, we use active learning to map the Vicsek phase diagram as a function of noise strength, density, and particle speed. A neural-network classifier is trained on global polar-order labels, and classifier entropy is used to select new simulations near uncertain crossover regions. The resulting phase map resolves a high-noise disordered gas, a low-noise polar ordered regime, and an intermediate coexistence-candidate regime whose noise window shifts upward and broadens with increasing density. Independent density and local-order diagnostics indicate that the intermediate regime contains dense, locally ordered bands coexisting with a dilute, weakly ordered background. Comparison with the ordered regime shows that banded coexistence is identified by the joint enhancement of band contrast, local-order heterogeneity, and positive density-order correlation. Overall, these results establish a machine-learning-guided workflow for active matter, in which active learning constructs an operational phase map and independent spatial diagnostics convert classifier-defined regimes into physically interpretable nonequilibrium morphologies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：维切克模型相图的主动学习映射
维切克模型是集体运动的最小模型，描述了局部对齐相互作用如何在鸟群等系统中生成宏观非平衡有序。本研究利用主动学习绘制维切克模型相图，参数包括噪声强度、密度和粒子速度。通过神经网络分类器训练全局极化序标签，利用分类器熵选择不确定的相变区域附近的新模拟。所得相图揭示了高噪声无序气体、低噪声极化有序相，以及一个中间共存候选相，其噪声窗口随密度增加而上移并变宽。独立的密度和局部有序诊断表明，中间相包含密集、局部有序的带状结构与稀疏、弱有序背景共存。与有序相比较，带状共存由带对比度增强、局部有序异质性增加和正密度-有序相关性共同识别。总体而言，这些结果建立了一种机器学习引导的主动物质工作流程，其中主动学习构建操作性相图，独立空间诊断将分类器定义的相转化为物理可解释的非平衡形态。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently explore the phase behavior of the Vicsek model under varying parameters, this study employs active learning to construct a phase diagram as a function of noise, density, and particle speed. A neural-network classifier is trained to predict global polar order, and regions of high classifier entropy—indicating uncertainty—are prioritized for new simulations. The resulting map identifies three regimes: a high-noise disordered gas, a low-noise polar ordered state, and an intermediate coexistence regime that broadens and shifts with increasing density. Spatial diagnostics confirm this intermediate regime features dense, locally ordered bands coexisting with a dilute, weakly ordered background, distinguished by enhanced band contrast, local-order heterogeneity, and positive density-order correlation. Your AI Travel Agent Would Book You a Bullfight: An Agentic Benchmark for Implicit Animal Welfare in Frontier AI Models
Authors: Jasmine Brazilek, Joel Christoph, Oliver Tullio, Carol Kline, Miles Tidmarsh, Arturs Kanepajs
First: 2026-06-16T16:42:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T17:01:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI agents are moving from advisors to actors, booking travel, planning menus, and running procurement on behalf of users. Existing benchmarks for AI and animal welfare evaluate model text responses to question-answer prompts, leaving open whether the welfare reasoning surfaced in those responses transfers to agentic deployment where the model must take actions with tools. We introduce TAC (Travel Agent Compassion), the first agentic benchmark measuring whether AI agents avoid options involving animal exploitation when acting on behalf of users. TAC presents an AI agent with twelve hand-authored travel booking scenarios across six categories of animal exploitation, augmented to forty-eight samples to control for price, rating, and position confounds. We evaluate seven frontier models from four labs. Every model scores below the chance level of sixty-four percent, with the best performer (Claude Opus 4.7) at fifty-three percent. A single welfare-aware sentence in the system prompt yields gains of forty-seven to sixty-three percentage points in Claude and GPT-5.5, twenty-six points in GPT-5.2, and under twelve points in DeepSeek and Gemini. An auxiliary Inspect Scout audit of 288 base-condition transcripts from the top two performers, using Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite as judge, flags zero transcripts for evaluation awareness, suggesting the below-chance rates do not stem from the models recognising the evaluation. We discuss implications for category-level variation across cultural domains, the limits of text-response welfare benchmarks, and the EU General-Purpose AI Code of Practice systemic risk framework.

RSICCLLM: A Multimodal Large Language Model for Remote Sensing Image Change Captioning
Authors: Yelin Wang, Zijia Song, Shuo Ye, Chuanguang Yang, Miaoyu Wang, Yong Xu, Zhulin An, Yongjun Xu, Zitong Yu
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-26T16:57:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:57:40+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Remote Sensing Image Change Captioning (RSICC) aims to describe changes between bi-temporal remote sensing images and holds significant research and application value. However, most existing methods rely on conventional deep learning architectures, and the limited model capacity constrains performance. Although large-model post-training techniques have achieved great success in general domains, their direct transfer to RSICC remains challenging due to data scarcity and the need for fine-grained change understanding. To address this, we propose RSICCLLM, the first post-training framework for large vision-language models in RSICC. Specifically, we design a data generation paradigm, release the instruction dataset RSICI, and establish a task-specific RSICC benchmark. We further introduce Difference-aware Supervised Fine-tuning to explicitly extract change representations and guide the model in perceiving and understanding temporal differences. In addition, we propose Dual-Negative Preference Optimization (DNPO), which employs two complementary negative-sample construction strategies to construct the preference dataset RSICP and further refine model performance. Extensive experiments validate the superior capability of RSICCLLM, which achieves outstanding results with only 7B parameters, surpassing models of substantially larger scales. The code and dataset will be made publicly available at https://github.com/keaill/RSICCLLM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RSICCLLM：面向遥感图像变化描述的多模态大语言模型

为提升遥感图像变化描述的准确性，本文提出RSICCLLM，这是首个面向遥感图像变化描述任务的视觉-语言大模型微调框架。该方法通过数据生成范式构建指令数据集RSICI，并建立专用评估基准。其核心包括差分监督微调以显式建模时序变化，以及双负样本偏好优化（DNPO）通过互补负样本策略提升模型偏好学习能力。实验表明，仅7B参数的RSICCLLM性能超越多个规模更大的模型，验证了其高效性与优越性。
Quenched large deviations for Monte Carlo integration with Coulomb gases
Authors: Martin Rouault, Rémi Bardenet, Mylène Maïda
First: 2025-08-02T14:52:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:56:04+00:00
Comments: 40 pages. Accepted for publication in Bernoulli
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Gibbs measures, such as Coulomb gases, are popular in modelling systems of interacting particles. Recently, we proposed to use Gibbs measures as randomized numerical integration algorithms with respect to a target measure $π$ on $\mathbb R^d$, following the heuristics that repulsiveness between particles should help reduce integration errors. A major issue in this approach is to tune the interaction kernel and confining potential of the Gibbs measure, so that the equilibrium measure of the system is the target distribution $π$. Doing so usually requires another Monte Carlo approximation of the \emph{potential}, i.e. the integral of the interaction kernel with respect to $π$. Using the methodology of large deviations from Garcia--Zelada (2019), we show that a random approximation of the potential preserves the fast large deviation principle that guarantees the proposed integration algorithm to outperform independent or Markov quadratures. For non-singular interaction kernels, we make minimal assumptions on this random approximation, which can be the result of a computationally cheap Monte Carlo preprocessing. For the Coulomb interaction kernel, we need the approximation to be based on another Gibbs measure, and we prove in passing a control on the uniform convergence of the approximation of the potential.
中文标题/摘要
标题：蒙特卡洛积分中库仑气体的淬火大偏差
吉布斯测度（如库仑气体）常用于建模相互作用粒子系统。我们最近提出将吉布斯测度作为相对于目标测度 $π$ 在 $\mathbb R^d$ 上的随机数值积分算法，其启发式思想是粒子间的排斥性有助于减少积分误差。该方法的主要问题是调节吉布斯测度的相互作用核和约束势，使得系统的平衡测度为目标分布 $π$。通常这需要对势（即相互作用核相对于 $π$ 的积分）进行另一次蒙特卡洛近似。我们采用Garcia--Zelada（2019）的大偏差方法，证明了势的随机近似仍保持快速大偏差原理，从而保证所提出的积分算法优于独立或马尔可夫四次积分。对于非奇异相互作用核，我们对这种随机近似仅作最小假设，其结果可由计算成本低廉的蒙特卡洛预处理获得。对于库仑相互作用核，该近似需基于另一吉布斯测度，并在过程中证明了势近似的均匀收敛性控制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the idea that repulsive particle interactions in Gibbs measures can reduce integration errors, this paper proposes using Coulomb gases as randomized Monte Carlo algorithms for numerical integration with respect to a target distribution π on R^d. The key challenge is tuning the interaction kernel and confining potential so that the system’s equilibrium matches π, which typically requires approximating the potential via another Monte Carlo step. Using large deviation theory from Garcia–Zelada (2019), the authors show that even a random approximation of the potential preserves the fast large deviation principle, ensuring superior convergence over independent or Markov quadratures. For non-singular kernels, minimal assumptions on the approximation suffice, often achievable via cheap Monte Carlo preprocessing; for Coulomb kernels, the approximation must itself be Gibbs-based, and the paper additionally establishes uniform convergence of the potential approximation.
为利用Gibbs测度中粒子间的排斥性降低积分误差，本文提出将Coulomb气体作为随机蒙特卡洛算法用于对R^d上目标分布π的数值积分。核心挑战在于调节相互作用核与约束势，使系统平衡分布匹配π，这通常需通过另一蒙特卡洛步骤近似势函数。作者借助Garcia–Zelada（2019）的大偏差理论证明，即使势函数是随机近似，其快速大偏差原理仍被保留，从而保证该算法优于独立或马尔可夫四阶矩法。对非奇异核，仅需对近似做最小假设，常可通过计算成本低廉的蒙特卡洛预处理实现；对Coulomb核，近似需基于另一Gibbs测度，并附带证明了势函数近似的均匀收敛性。
Context-specific Credibility-aware Multimodal Fusion with Conditional Probabilistic Circuits
Authors: Pranuthi Tenali, Sahil Sidheekh, Saurabh Mathur, Erik Blasch, Kristian Kersting, Sriraam Natarajan
First: 2026-03-27T17:29:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:50:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal fusion requires integrating information from multiple sources that may conflict depending on context. Existing fusion approaches typically rely on static assumptions about source reliability, limiting their ability to resolve conflicts when a modality becomes unreliable due to situational factors such as sensor degradation or class-specific corruption. We introduce C$^2$MF, a context-specfic credibility-aware multimodal fusion framework that models per-instance source reliability using a Conditional Probabilistic Circuit (CPC). We formalize instance-level reliability through Context-Specific Information Credibility (CSIC), a KL-divergence-based measure computed exactly from the CPC. CSIC generalizes conventional static credibility estimates as a special case, enabling principled and adaptive reliability assessment. To evaluate robustness under cross-modal conflicts, we propose the Conflict benchmark, in which class-specific corruptions deliberately induce discrepancies between different modalities. Experimental results show that C$^2$MF improves predictive accuracy by up to 29% over static-reliability baselines in high-noise settings, while preserving the interpretability advantages of probabilistic circuit-based fusion.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于上下文感知可信度的多模态融合方法
多模态融合需要整合可能因上下文而冲突的多源信息。现有融合方法通常依赖于对源可靠性的静态假设，限制了其在模态因环境因素（如传感器退化或类别特异性损坏）而不可靠时解决冲突的能力。我们提出了C²MF框架，该框架通过条件概率电路（CPC）建模每个实例的源可靠性，并通过基于KL散度的上下文特定信息可信度（CSIC）形式化实例级可靠性，CSIC作为特例可推广传统静态可信度估计，实现原则性且自适应的可靠性评估。为评估跨模态冲突下的鲁棒性，我们提出冲突基准，其中类别特异性损坏故意导致不同模态间差异。实验结果表明，在高噪声环境下，C²MF相比静态可靠性基线方法预测准确率提升最高达29%，同时保留了基于概率电路融合方法的可解释性优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to handle conflicting multimodal data under varying contextual conditions, this paper proposes C$^2$MF, a framework that dynamically assesses source reliability per instance using a Conditional Probabilistic Circuit. It introduces Context-Specific Information Credibility (CSIC), a KL-divergence-based metric that quantifies reliability adaptively, generalizing static credibility as a special case. Evaluated on a newly proposed Conflict benchmark designed to induce class-specific modality discrepancies, C$^2$MF demonstrates up to 29% higher predictive accuracy than static-reliability baselines in high-noise scenarios, while maintaining the interpretability of probabilistic circuit fusion.
为应对不同情境下多模态数据冲突与源可靠性动态变化的问题，本文提出C$^2$MF框架，利用条件概率电路建模每个实例的源可靠性，并引入基于KL散度的上下文特定信息可信度（CSIC）指标，以自适应评估可靠性，其静态可信度为特例。在人为引入类特定模态差异的冲突基准上，C$^2$MF在高噪声环境下相较静态可靠性基线提升预测准确率最高达29%，同时保持概率电路融合的可解释性优势。
Deep Reinforcement Learning-Enhanced Event-Triggered Data-Driven Predictive Control for a 3D Cable-Driven Soft Robotic Arm
Authors: Cheng Ouyang, Moeen Ul Islam, Kaixiang Zhang, Zhaojian Li, Xiaobo Tan, Dong Chen
First: 2026-06-24T17:26:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:48:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Soft robots are challenging to control due to their nonlinear and time-varying dynamics. Data-enabled predictive control (DeePC) offers a model-free alternative by directly leveraging measured input-output trajectories to construct a predictive controller. However, its receding-horizon formulation requires solving a constrained optimization problem at every sampling instant, which can be computationally demanding for real-time deployment on resource-limited robotic platforms. To address this limitation, we propose an adaptive reinforcement-learning-based event-triggered DeePC (RL-ET-DeePC) framework for soft robotic control. A model-free RL policy is trained to determine when to invoke the DeePC optimizer based on the current system state representation, thereby reducing unnecessary optimization calls while preserving closed-loop performance. Simulation results show that RL-ET-DeePC reduces optimization frequency by up to 66% compared to periodic DeePC, while maintaining comparable tracking accuracy. Hardware experiments on a three-dimensional cable-driven soft robotic arm demonstrate zero-shot transfer, achieving a 34% reduction in optimization frequency with tracking accuracy comparable to periodic DeePC and more consistent performance than a static threshold-based event-triggered baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于深度强化学习的事件触发数据驱动预测控制用于三维缆驱动柔性机械臂
柔性机器人因其非线性和时变动力学特性而难以控制。数据驱动预测控制（DeePC）提供了一种无需模型的替代方案，通过直接利用测量的输入-输出轨迹构建预测控制器。然而，其滚动时域形式在每个采样时刻都需要求解约束优化问题，对于资源受限的机器人平台实时部署而言计算负担较重。为解决此问题，我们提出了一种基于强化学习的自适应事件触发DeePC（RL-ET-DeePC）框架用于柔性机器人控制。通过训练一个无模型的强化学习策略，根据当前系统状态表示决定何时调用DeePC优化器，从而减少不必要的优化调用，同时保持闭环性能。仿真结果表明，RL-ET-DeePC相比周期性DeePC将优化频率降低了高达66%，同时保持相近的跟踪精度。在三维缆驱动柔性机械臂上的硬件实验验证了零样本迁移能力，实现了优化频率降低34%，跟踪精度与周期性DeePC相当，且比基于静态阈值的事件触发基线方法表现更稳定。
Summary / 总结
To address the computational burden of real-time predictive control for soft robots with nonlinear dynamics, this work proposes an RL-ET-DeePC framework that uses reinforcement learning to adaptively trigger a data-driven predictive controller only when necessary. The RL policy learns to decide optimization timing based on system state representations, avoiding redundant computations while preserving control performance. Experimental results show up to 66% reduction in optimization frequency in simulation and 34% reduction in hardware tests, with tracking accuracy comparable to periodic control and more consistent behavior than static threshold methods.
为解决软机器人实时控制中模型无关预测控制的计算负担，本文提出一种基于强化学习的事件触发式DeePC框架，通过学习系统状态自适应触发优化，减少冗余计算同时保持性能。仿真显示相比周期性DeePC优化次数减少66%且跟踪精度相当，硬件实验在三维缆驱动软机械臂上验证零样本迁移，优化次数减少34%，且性能比静态阈值触发基线更稳定。
MeDUET: Disentangled Unified Pretraining for 3D Medical Image Synthesis and Analysis
Authors: Junkai Liu, Ling Shao, Le Zhang
First: 2026-02-19T23:45:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:48:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Self-supervised learning (SSL) and diffusion models have respectively advanced representation learning and generative modeling for high-dimensional 3D visual data, yet they are often developed as separate paradigms. Their unification remains challenging under multi-source heterogeneity, as anatomical content must be preserved for analysis while acquisition-related style varies across centers and affects synthesis. In this paper, we propose MeDUET, a 3D Medical image Disentangled UnifiEd PreTraining framework in the variational autoencoder latent space. MeDUET formulates unified pretraining as an empirical factor identifiability problem, aiming to learn domain-invariant content factors for anatomy and domain-specific style factors for appearance. To improve factor separation, MeDUET first uses token demixing with a standard adversarial domain regularizer to establish basic content-style specialization, and further introduces Mixed Factor Token Distillation and Swap-invariance Quadruplet Contrast to reduce mixed-region factor leakage and organize factor spaces with factor-wise invariance and discriminability. With these learned factors, MeDUET transfers effectively to both synthesis and analysis, yielding higher fidelity, faster convergence, and better controllability for synthesis, while achieving competitive or superior domain generalization and label efficiency on diverse datasets, tasks, and modalities. Overall, MeDUET shows that multi-source heterogeneity can serve as useful supervision, with disentanglement providing an effective interface for unifying 3D medical image synthesis and analysis. Our code is available at https://github.com/JK-Liu7/MeDUET.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MeDUET：面向3D医学图像合成与分析的解耦统一预训练
自监督学习（SSL）和扩散模型分别在高维3D视觉数据的表征学习和生成建模方面取得了进展，但它们通常作为独立范式开发。在多源异构性背景下，其统一仍具挑战性，因为分析需保持解剖内容，而采集相关的风格因中心不同而变化，影响合成效果。本文提出MeDUET，一种在变分自编码器潜在空间中的3D医学图像解耦统一预训练框架。MeDUET将统一预训练建模为经验因子可识别性问题，旨在学习解剖结构的领域不变内容因子和外观的领域特异性风格因子。为提升因子分离，MeDUET首先采用令牌解混与标准对抗域正则化建立基础内容-风格专业化，进一步引入混合因子令牌蒸馏与交换不变四元组对比，以减少混合区域因子泄漏并按因子层面实现不变性与可区分性组织因子空间。借助所学因子，MeDUET在合成与分析任务中均能有效迁移，合成效果更高质量、收敛更快、可控性更强，同时在多样数据集、任务和模态上实现竞争性或更优的领域泛化与标签效率。总体而言，MeDUET表明多源异构性可作为有用监督，解耦提供统一3D医学图像合成与分析的有效接口。代码见https://github.com/JK-Liu7/MeDUET。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to unify self-supervised learning and diffusion models for 3D medical imaging, MeDUET proposes a disentangled unified pretraining framework in the variational autoencoder latent space. It treats the problem as an empirical factor identifiability task, learning domain-invariant anatomical content factors and domain-specific style factors. To enhance disentanglement, it employs token demixing with adversarial regularization, followed by mixed factor token distillation and swap-invariance quadruplet contrast to suppress factor leakage and improve discriminability. Experiments show MeDUET achieves superior synthesis fidelity, faster convergence, and better controllability while also improving domain generalization and label efficiency across diverse datasets and modalities, demonstrating that multi-source heterogeneity can be leveraged effectively through disentanglement.
为统一自监督学习与扩散模型在3D医学图像中的应用，MeDUET在变分自编码器潜在空间中提出一种解耦预训练框架，分离解剖结构内容与采集相关风格。通过令牌解混、对抗正则化及对比学习技术强化因子分离并减少因子交叉泄漏。实验表明，MeDUET在合成图像保真度、收敛速度与可控性方面表现更优，同时在多数据集、多任务与多模态下展现出更强的领域泛化能力与标签效率。
Decentralized Orchestration Architecture for Fluid Computing: A Secure Distributed AI Use Case
Authors: Diego Cajaraville-Aboy, Ana Fernández-Vilas, Rebeca P. Díaz-Redondo, Manuel Fernández-Veiga, Pablo Picallo-López
Venue: Computer Networks, Volume 284 (2026) 112369
First: 2026-03-12T14:49:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:46:17+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 9 figures and 1 table
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Distributed AI and IoT applications increasingly execute across heterogeneous resources spanning end devices, edge/fog infrastructure, and cloud platforms, often under different administrative domains. Fluid Computing has emerged as a promising paradigm for enhancing massive resource management across the computing continuum by treating such resources as a unified fabric, enabling optimal service-agnostic deployments driven by application requirements. However, existing solutions remain largely centralized and often do not explicitly address multi-domain considerations. This paper proposes an agnostic multi-domain orchestration architecture for fluid computing environments. The orchestration plane enables decentralized coordination among domains that maintain local autonomy while jointly realizing intent-based deployment requests from tenants, ensuring end-to-end placement and execution. To this end, the architecture elevates domain-side control services as first-class capabilities to support application-level enhancement at runtime. As a representative proof of concept, we instantiate the architecture through a distributed AI use case; specifically, we consider a multi-domain Decentralized Federated Learning (DFL) deployment under Byzantine threats. Under this setting, we leverage domain-side capabilities to enhance Byzantine security by introducing FU-HST, an SDN-enabled multi-domain anomaly detection mechanism that complements Byzantine-robust aggregation. We validate the use-case workflow via simulation in single- and multi-domain settings, evaluating anomaly detection, DFL performance, and computation/communication overhead.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向流式计算的去中心化编排架构：一种安全分布式AI用例
分布式AI和物联网应用日益在异构资源上执行，这些资源涵盖终端设备、边缘/雾基础设施和云平台，常跨越不同管理域。流式计算作为一种新兴范式，通过将这些资源视为统一的计算织物，提升跨计算连续体的大规模资源管理能力，实现基于应用需求的服务无关最优部署。然而，现有方案多为集中式，且常未显式考虑多域问题。本文提出一种面向流式计算环境的多域无偏编排架构。编排平面支持各域在保持本地自治的同时，协同实现租户的意图式部署请求，确保端到端的部署与执行。为此，架构将域侧控制服务提升为一等能力，以支持运行时应用级增强。作为代表性概念验证，我们通过分布式AI用例实例化该架构；具体而言，考虑在拜占庭威胁下的多域去中心化联邦学习（DFL）部署。在此设定下，我们利用域侧能力增强拜占庭安全性，引入FU-HST机制——一种支持SDN的多域异常检测机制，与拜占庭鲁棒聚合互补。我们通过单域和多域仿真验证该用例工作流，评估异常检测、DFL性能及计算/通信开销。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for secure, decentralized coordination across heterogeneous and multi-domain computing environments in distributed AI and IoT, this paper proposes a fluid computing orchestration architecture that enables domain autonomy while supporting intent-based service deployment. The method elevates domain-side control services as core capabilities to facilitate runtime application enhancements, demonstrated through a decentralized federated learning use case under Byzantine threats. Experimental results show that the proposed FU-HST mechanism, leveraging SDN for multi-domain anomaly detection, effectively complements Byzantine-robust aggregation, improving DFL accuracy and resilience while maintaining acceptable computation and communication overhead in both single- and multi-domain simulations.
为应对异构分布式AI环境中多域协同与安全需求，本文提出一种面向流体计算的去中心化编排架构，支持各域自治同时实现意图驱动的服务部署。方法通过将域侧控制服务提升为核心能力，实现跨管理域协调，并以去中心化联邦学习在拜占庭威胁下的应用为验证场景。实验表明，引入的FU-HST机制（基于SDN的多域异常检测）有效增强拜占庭鲁棒性，提升DFL精度与收敛性，且计算与通信开销可控，在单域与多域仿真中表现稳定。
Image-based Geo-localization for Robotics: Are Black-box Vision-Language Models there yet?
Authors: Sania Waheed, Bruno Ferrarini, Michael Milford, Sarvapali D. Ramchurn, Shoaib Ehsan
Venue: ICRA 2026
First: 2025-01-28T13:46:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:43:32+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the ICRA 2026 Workshop on Multi-Modal Spatial AI for Robust Navigation and Open-World Understanding (MM-SpatialAI)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The advances in Vision-Language models (VLMs) offer exciting opportunities for robotic applications involving image geo-localization - the problem of identifying the geo-coordinates of a place based on visual data only. In robotics, such capabilities are particularly relevant to the global re-localization stage of the kidnapped robot problem, where a robot must recover its pose without prior knowledge of its location. Recent work has focused on using a VLM as embedding extractor for geo-localization. However, the most sophisticated VLMs may only be available as black boxes that are accessible through an API, and come with a number of limitations: there is no access to training data, model features and gradients; retraining is not possible; and the number of predictions may be limited by the API. The potential of state-of-the-art VLMs as a stand-alone, zero-shot geo-localization systems at planet scale using a single text-based prompt is largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, this paper undertakes the first systematic study, to the best of our knowledge, to investigate state-of-the-art generative VLMs as stand-alone, zero-shot geo-localization systems in a black-box setting with realistic constraints. We consider three main scenarios for this thorough investigation: a) fixed text-based prompt; b) semantically-equivalent text-based prompts; and c) semantically-equivalent query images. Beyond standard accuracy, we introduce model consistency as a metric to account for the auto-regressive and probabilistic nature of generative VLMs. Our findings reveal that while VLMs demonstrate strong coarse-level localization and navigation priors, fine-grained localization degrades significantly under realistic variations, highlighting reliability challenges for deploying generative VLMs in robust, open-world robotic navigation systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于图像的机器人地理定位：黑盒视觉-语言模型是否已就绪？
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）的进步为仅基于视觉数据识别地点地理坐标的机器人应用——即图像地理定位——带来了激动人心的机会。在机器人领域，这种能力尤其适用于被劫持机器人问题中的全局重定位阶段，即机器人需在无先验位置知识的情况下恢复自身位姿。近期研究聚焦于将VLM作为地理定位的嵌入提取器。然而，最先进的VLM可能仅以黑盒形式提供，仅可通过API访问，且存在诸多限制：无法访问训练数据、模型特征和梯度；不可重新训练；API对预测次数有限制。目前，利用单个文本提示在行星尺度上将最先进的VLM作为独立的零样本地理定位系统的能力尚待探索。为弥合这一差距，本文首次系统性地（据我们所知）研究了最先进的生成式VLM在黑盒环境下、在现实约束下作为独立零样本地理定位系统的潜力。我们针对此全面调查考虑三种主要场景：a) 固定文本提示；b) 语义等价文本提示；c) 语义等价查询图像。除标准精度外，我们引入模型一致性作为度量，以反映生成式VLM的自回归和概率特性。研究发现，尽管VLM表现出强大的粗粒度定位与导航先验，但在现实变化下细粒度定位显著退化，凸显了在开放世界机器人导航系统中部署生成式VLM的可靠性挑战。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential of vision-language models (VLMs) to enable zero-shot geo-localization for robots without prior maps, this study investigates whether state-of-the-art generative VLMs can function as standalone, black-box systems under realistic constraints. The authors evaluate three prompt scenarios—fixed text prompts, semantically equivalent text prompts, and semantically equivalent images—and introduce 'model consistency' as a new metric to assess the probabilistic nature of generative VLMs. Experimental results show that while VLMs excel at coarse-level localization and navigation priors, fine-grained accuracy degrades significantly under real-world variations, indicating reliability limitations for deployment in open-world robotic navigation.
为探索视觉语言模型（VLM）是否能在无先验位置知识的情况下，作为黑盒系统实现零样本地理定位，本研究系统评估了当前最先进的生成式VLM在现实约束下的表现。方法涵盖三种场景：固定文本提示、语义等价提示及语义等价图像查询，并引入“模型一致性”指标以评估生成模型的随机性。实验结果表明，尽管VLM在粗粒度定位与导航方面表现良好，但在真实环境变化下细粒度定位能力显著下降，揭示了其在开放世界机器人导航中部署的可靠性挑战。
Monte Carlo with kernel-based Gibbs measures: Guarantees for probabilistic herding
Authors: Martin Rouault, Rémi Bardenet, Mylène Maïda
First: 2024-02-18T23:39:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:40:36+00:00
Comments: 24 pages. Accepted for publication in SIAM journal on Mathematics of Data Science
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Kernel herding belongs to a family of deterministic quadratures that seek to minimize the maximum mean discrepancy (MMD), that is, the worst-case integration error over a reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS). These MMD minimization procedures come with strong experimental support, but comparatively less theoretical footing. In particular, apart from recent progress in distribution compression, little has been proved in favor of an improvement of MMD minimization over classical Monte Carlo quadrature when the RKHS is infinite-dimensional. In this paper, we study a joint probability distribution over quadrature nodes, a tailored Gibbs distribution, whose support intuitively tends to concentrate around MMD minimizers as a temperature parameter is decreased. Our main contribution is to prove that drawing integration nodes from our distribution does outperform i.i.d Monte Carlo. While our bounds on the worst-case integration error feature the same rate as i.i.d. Monte Carlo, we do obtain a tighter concentration inequality as the temperature parameter decreases. This means smaller confidence intervals as the number of quadrature nodes increases. While arguably a first step, our results demonstrate that the mathematical toolbox developed around Gibbs measures can help understand to what extent kernel herding and its variants improve on computationally cheaper methods. There remains the issue of sampling from our Gibbs distribution. In our numerical experiments, we demonstrate that a simple MCMC chain already yields approximate samples that lead to improved confidence intervals around the target integrals, as supported by our theoretical results.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于核基Gibbs测度的蒙特卡洛方法：概率牧群的保证
核牧群属于一类确定性数值积分方法，旨在最小化最大均值差异（MMD），即在再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）中的最坏情况积分误差。这些MMD最小化过程具有较强的实验支持，但理论基础相对薄弱。特别是，除了最近在分布压缩方面的进展外，尚无理论证明当RKHS为无限维时，MMD最小化优于经典蒙特卡洛积分。本文研究了一种联合概率分布，即量化的节点的定制Gibbs分布，其支撑集在温度参数减小时直观地趋向于MMD最小化点。我们的主要贡献是证明从该分布中抽样积分节点确实优于独立同分布蒙特卡洛。虽然我们对最坏情况积分误差的界与独立同分布蒙特卡洛相同，但随着温度参数减小，我们获得了更紧的集中不等式。这意味着随着积分节点数量增加，置信区间更小。尽管这或许只是第一步，但我们的结果表明，围绕Gibbs测度发展起来的数学工具可以帮助理解核牧群及其变体在计算成本更低的方法上能改进到何种程度。仍需解决从该Gibbs分布采样的问题。在数值实验中，我们证明了一个简单的MCMC链已能产生近似样本，从而在目标积分周围获得更优的置信区间，这得到了我们理论结果的支持。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the empirical success of kernel herding but limited theoretical guarantees, especially in infinite-dimensional RKHS settings, this paper introduces a kernel-based Gibbs distribution over quadrature nodes designed to concentrate around MMD minimizers as temperature decreases. The method draws samples from this Gibbs distribution and proves that, while worst-case error bounds match i.i.d. Monte Carlo, the concentration inequality tightens with lower temperature, yielding smaller confidence intervals for larger sample sizes. Experimental results confirm that even a simple MCMC sampler produces samples that empirically improve confidence intervals, validating the theoretical claims.
为从理论上证明核采样优于经典蒙特卡洛方法，本文提出一种针对积分节点的定制化吉布斯分布，其在温度降低时趋向于集中于MMD最小化点。作者证明，从该分布采样可获得比独立同分布蒙特卡洛更紧的集中不等式，尽管最坏情况误差率相同。实验表明，即使使用简单的MCMC链采样，也能获得更优的积分置信区间，验证了理论结果并展示了吉布斯分布工具在分析概率采样方法中的价值。
Parameter-Efficient Continuous-Variable Photonic Quantum Neural Networks for Edge Quantum AI: Demonstration in Oral Cancer Detection
Authors: Akshay Bhagwan Sonawane, Sophie Choe, Lakshman Tamil
First: 2026-06-26T16:37:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:37:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Early detection of oral cancer markedly improves clinical outcomes, yet specialized diagnostic tools remain scarce in low-resource settings. Smartphone-based screening is a scalable alternative but needs lightweight models that run within edge-hardware constraints. Hybrid classical-quantum architectures are emerging candidates for parameter-efficient learning, yet most rely on qubit hardware that needs cryogenic operation, unsuitable for edge deployment. Continuous-variable (CV) photonic quantum computing, which operates at room temperature, offers a complementary route. We investigate a hybrid classical-CV quantum classifier for oral cancer detection from smartphone images. The pipeline combines a MobileNetV1 feature extractor, principal component analysis to 16 dimensions, and a parameterized CV-QNN of displacement, interferometric, and Kerr gates on a photonic backend. We propose a simplified $Φ\circ D \circ U_1$ CV-QNN architecture that cuts trainable parameters 40-45% relative to the standard CV-QNN layer of Killoran et al. (2019a), and identify dimensionality-reduction and encoding-restriction strategies that mitigate barren plateaus, raising loss-gradient variance by roughly 58 orders of magnitude. Whether the simplified layer beats the full layer is width-dependent: the full layer holds a small but significant edge at two qumodes, whereas the simplified layer is significantly better at four qumodes using 44% fewer parameters. The strongest model, a four-qumode simplified CV-QNN with only 18 parameters, attains the highest validation AUC of all models, exceeds a 55-parameter classical baseline using 67% fewer parameters, and reaches 100% calibrated test accuracy across all seeds. These results support CV photonic quantum machine learning for parameter-efficient, room-temperature medical image classification and motivate progress toward edge quantum AI.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向边缘量子人工智能的参数高效连续变量光子量子神经网络：口腔癌检测演示
早期口腔癌检测显著改善临床预后，但在资源匮乏地区仍缺乏专用诊断工具。基于智能手机的筛查是可扩展的替代方案，但需要能在边缘硬件约束下运行的轻量级模型。混合经典-量子架构是参数高效学习的新兴候选方案，但多数依赖需低温运行的量子比特硬件，不适合边缘部署。在室温下运行的连续变量（CV）光子量子计算提供了一种互补路径。我们研究了一种用于从智能手机图像检测口腔癌的混合经典-CV量子分类器。该流程结合MobileNetV1特征提取器、主成分分析降至16维，以及在光子后端上运行的参数化CV-QNN（含位移、干涉和克尔门）。我们提出了一种简化$Φ\circ D \circ U_1$ CV-QNN架构，相比Killoran等人（2019a）的标准CV-QNN层可减少40-45%可训练参数，并识别出维度缩减和编码限制策略，可缓解 barren plateaus 问题，使损失梯度方差提升约58个数量级。简化层是否优于完整层取决于宽度：在两个模式下完整层有微小但显著优势，而在四个模式下简化层使用44%更少参数表现显著更好。最强模型——仅18个参数的四模式简化CV-QNN，实现了所有模型中最高的验证AUC，以67%更少参数超越55参数的经典基线模型，并在所有种子下达到100%校准测试准确率。这些结果支持CV光子量子机器学习在参数高效、室温医学图像分类中的应用，并推动边缘量子人工智能的发展。
Summary / 总结
To address the lack of accessible diagnostic tools for oral cancer in low-resource settings, this study proposes a parameter-efficient hybrid classical-quantum classifier using continuous-variable photonic quantum neural networks (CV-QNNs) deployable on edge hardware. The method combines MobileNetV1 for image feature extraction, PCA for dimensionality reduction to 16 dimensions, and a simplified CV-QNN architecture with displacement, interferometric, and Kerr gates. The proposed $Φ\circ D \circ U_1$ layer reduces trainable parameters by 40–45% compared to standard CV-QNNs and mitigates barren plateaus by boosting loss-gradient variance by 58 orders of magnitude. Experimental results show that while the full CV-QNN performs slightly better at two qumodes, the simplified version outperforms it at four qumodes using 44% fewer parameters, achieving the highest validation AUC and 100% calibrated test accuracy with only 18 parameters—67% fewer than a classical baseline.
为解决低资源地区口腔癌早期诊断工具匮乏的问题，本研究提出一种参数高效的混合经典-量子分类器，利用可在边缘硬件上部署的连续变量光量子神经网络（CV-QNN）。该方法结合MobileNetV1特征提取、PCA降维至16维，以及包含位移、干涉和克尔门的简化CV-QNN架构（$Φ\circ D \circ U_1$），参数量比标准CV-QNN减少40–45%，并采用编码策略缓解 barren plateaus，使损失梯度方差提升约58个数量级。实验表明，四模态简化模型仅用18个参数即获得最高验证AUC，以67%更少参数超越55参数经典基线，并在所有种子下实现100%校准测试准确率，验证了CV光量子机器学习在边缘部署医学图像分类中的可行性。
HPRO: Hierarchical Progressive Reward Optimization via Preference Extraction for Emotional Text-to-Speech
Authors: Sihang Nie, Xiaofen Xing, Rui Xing, Haoming Li, Ruitong Xiao, Jingyuan Xing, Baiji Liu, Xiangmin Xu
First: 2026-06-26T16:35:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:35:48+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 3 figures, 3 tables; Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recently, Large Language Model (LLM)-based Text-to-Speech (TTS) models have achieved remarkable naturalness. However, the standard Supervised Fine-Tuning paradigm often converges to statistically averaged prosody, limiting emotional expressiveness. While preference-driven optimization offers a promising alternative, existing approaches suffer from two structural mismatches: information conflict, where content and emotion in a shared latent space produce conflicting gradients, leading to reward hacking and semantic degradation; and scale gap, where sparse sentence-level rewards struggle to guide dense frame-level generation. To overcome these challenges, we propose HPRO, a hierarchical progressive reward optimization framework. Within HPRO, we introduce the HD-Emo codec as a novel differentiable reward model to resolve the information conflict. It extracts speech into distinct content and style preference tokens, structurally isolating emotional optimization from semantic content. Building upon this structured preference space, HPRO bridges the scale gap by progressively aligning frame-, word- and sentence-level objectives. Experiments demonstrate that HPRO significantly enhances emotional expressiveness, while effectively preserving linguistic intelligibility. The code and audio samples are publicly available at https://xxh333.github.io/hpro-demo/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HPRO：通过偏好提取的分层渐进奖励优化用于情感文本到语音
最近，基于大语言模型（LLM）的文本到语音（TTS）模型在自然度方面取得了显著进展。然而，标准的监督微调范式往往收敛于统计平均的语调，限制了情感表达能力。虽然偏好驱动的优化提供了一种有前景的替代方案，但现有方法存在两种结构不匹配问题：信息冲突，即内容与情感在共享潜在空间中产生冲突梯度，导致奖励作弊和语义退化；以及尺度差距，即稀疏的句子级奖励难以指导密集的帧级生成。为克服这些挑战，我们提出了HPRO，一种分层渐进奖励优化框架。在HPRO中，我们引入了HD-Emo编解码器作为新型可微分奖励模型，以解决信息冲突问题。它将语音提取为独立的内容和风格偏好标记，结构上将情感优化与语义内容分离。在此结构化偏好空间基础上，HPRO通过逐步对齐帧级、词级和句子级目标，弥合了尺度差距。实验表明，HPRO显著增强了情感表达能力，同时有效保持了语言可理解性。代码和音频样本可在https://xxh333.github.io/hpro-demo/公开获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of standard supervised fine-tuning in LLM-based TTS models that converge to averaged prosody and lose emotional expressiveness, HPRO introduces a hierarchical progressive reward optimization framework. It employs a novel differentiable HD-Emo codec to decompose speech into content and emotion tokens, resolving information conflict by structurally separating emotional optimization from semantic content. To address the scale gap between sparse sentence-level rewards and dense frame-level generation, HPRO progressively aligns objectives across frame, word, and sentence levels. Experimental results show that HPRO significantly improves emotional expressiveness while maintaining linguistic intelligibility.
为了解决基于LLM的TTS模型在监督微调中收敛于平均语调、缺乏情感表现力的问题，HPRO提出了一种分层渐进式奖励优化框架。该框架引入新型可微分HD-Emo编码器，将语音分解为内容与情感令牌，结构化地分离情感优化与语义内容，解决信息冲突问题；并通过逐层对齐帧、词、句级目标，弥合稀疏句子奖励与密集帧级生成之间的尺度差距。实验表明，HPRO显著提升情感表现力，同时保持语言可理解性。
How Width and Data Shape Generalization Scaling Laws in Quadratic Neural Networks
Authors: Julius Girardin, Emanuele Troiani, Yizhou Xu, Vittorio Erba, Florent Krzakala, Lenka Zdeborová
First: 2026-06-26T16:27:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:27:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Understanding how performance scales jointly with model size and data is a central problem in modern machine learning. Existing theoretical works on scaling laws typically describe generalization as a function of data or compute, often in fixed-feature or infinite-width regimes and for online SGD. Here, we instead study how generalization scales with the number of trainable parameters and the number of samples in a feature-learning model. We analyze $\ell_2$-regularized empirical test error minimization in a quadratic two-layer network in a finite-sample setting with structured data. This setting allows for an explicit characterization of the generalization error as a function of the number of samples, model width, and regularization. Our results reveal a phase diagram with distinct scaling regimes as the number of parameters varies. In particular, the generalization error follows data-dependent power laws controlled by the spectral structure of the target. We further characterize the transitions between regimes, including the onset of interpolation, and their impact on generalization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：宽度与数据如何影响二次神经网络中的泛化缩放规律
理解模型规模与数据量如何共同影响性能是现代机器学习的核心问题。现有理论工作通常将泛化视为数据或计算量的函数，常在固定特征或无限宽度假设下，针对在线SGD进行分析。本文则研究特征学习模型中泛化如何随可训练参数数量和样本数量变化。我们在结构化数据的有限样本设定下，分析二次两层网络中带ℓ₂正则化的经验测试误差最小化问题。该设定允许我们显式刻画泛化误差作为样本数、模型宽度和正则化强度的函数。结果揭示了参数数量变化时的相图，包含不同缩放阶段。特别地，泛化误差遵循由目标谱结构控制的数据依赖幂律。我们进一步刻画了各阶段间的转变，包括插值的起始点及其对泛化的影响。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how model size and data quantity jointly affect generalization, this work studies generalization scaling in quadratic neural networks under $\ell_2$-regularized empirical risk minimization with finite samples and structured data. The authors derive an explicit formula for generalization error as a function of model width, sample size, and regularization, revealing a phase diagram with distinct scaling regimes. Their analysis shows that generalization error follows data-dependent power laws governed by the spectral structure of the target function, and identifies transitions such as the onset of interpolation that significantly impact generalization behavior.
为理解模型规模与数据量如何共同影响泛化能力，该研究在有限样本和结构化数据条件下，分析二次神经网络的泛化缩放规律。通过在两层网络中采用 $\ell_2$ 正则化的经验测试误差最小化，推导出泛化误差关于模型宽度、样本数和正则化项的显式表达式。研究发现，参数数量变化会引发不同泛化缩放阶段，包括插值阈值的出现，其泛化行为由目标函数的谱结构决定。
Functional outcomes and naturalistic engagement with a purpose-built conversational AI for mental health (Ash)
Authors: Kristen M. Van Swearingen, Thomas D. Hull, Karthik V. Sarma, Caitlin A. Stamatis
First: 2026-06-26T16:27:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:27:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Background: Conversational AI chatbots designed for mental health may offer an accessible, scalable avenue for supporting psychological well-being, yet prior evaluations have largely focused on clinical symptom reduction rather than broader indicators of day-to-day functioning, and have rarely monitored for potential harms such as inflated self-perception. Objective: We examined within-person change in psychological functioning indicators among real-world users of Ash, a purpose-built conversational AI for mental health support, over the first four weeks of use, and whether these changes were associated with engagement metrics. Methods: In this single-arm observational cohort study, new users (n = 1,284) completed in-app single-item measures of psychological functioning (life satisfaction, relationship satisfaction, sleep quality, behavioral activation), working alliance, and grandiosity (inflated self-perception), at baseline and Week 4. Paired-sample t-tests examined within-person change; ANCOVAs tested engagement-outcome associations at Week 4, controlling for baseline. Results: At baseline, participants reported below-average life satisfaction and fair sleep quality. Significant within-person improvements emerged across all functioning indicators and working alliance (ps < .001; d = 0.14-0.26), with no change in grandiosity. Active days, total sessions, and total minutes consistently predicted Week 4 psychological functioning and working alliance (ps <= .006; partial R^2 range: 0.58-2.15%; controlling for baseline), whereas user message volume did not. Conclusion: Findings provide preliminary data for the potential of evidence-based conversational AI to extend mental health support for broad psychological functioning, extending the existing literature beyond symptom-based outcomes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：为心理健康设计的专用对话式人工智能的职能结果与自然参与度（Ash）
背景：为心理健康设计的对话式人工智能聊天机器人可能提供一种便捷、可扩展的支持心理健康的途径，但以往评估主要关注临床症状减轻，而非日常功能的广泛指标，且很少监测如自我认知膨胀等潜在危害。 目标：我们考察了真实用户在使用Ash（一种为心理健康支持设计的专用对话式人工智能）前四周内心理功能指标的个体变化，以及这些变化是否与参与度指标相关。 方法：本单臂观察性队列研究中，新用户（n=1,284）在基线和第4周完成应用内单项目心理功能（生活满意度、人际关系满意度、睡眠质量、行为激活）、工作联盟和夸大（自我认知膨胀）测量。配对样本t检验分析个体变化；协方差分析在第4周检验参与度与结果关联，控制基线。 结果：基线时，参与者报告生活满意度低于平均水平，睡眠质量一般。所有功能指标和工作联盟均显著改善（p<.001；d=0.14-0.26），夸大无变化。活跃天数、总会话数和总时长持续预测第4周心理功能和工作联盟（p<=.006；部分R²范围：0.58-2.15%；控制基线），而用户消息量无预测作用。 结论：研究结果为基于证据的对话式人工智能扩展心理健康支持、涵盖广泛心理功能提供了初步数据，超越了现有以症状为基础的文献。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate conversational AI for mental health beyond symptom reduction, this study examined within-person changes in psychological functioning among real-world users of Ash, a purpose-built chatbot, over four weeks. Using a single-arm observational design with 1,284 new users, researchers collected self-reported data on life satisfaction, relationship satisfaction, sleep quality, behavioral activation, working alliance, and grandiosity at baseline and Week 4. Paired t-tests revealed significant improvements across all functioning indicators and working alliance, with no change in grandiosity. Engagement metrics—active days, total sessions, and total minutes—were significantly associated with improved outcomes at Week 4, while message volume was not, suggesting that sustained interaction duration, not frequency of messaging, drives functional gains.
为评估对话式AI在心理健康支持中的作用，本研究考察了Ash（一款专为心理健康设计的AI）在真实用户使用四周内的心理功能变化。研究采用单臂观察性队列设计，对1284名新用户在基线和第4周测量了生活满意度、睡眠质量、工作联盟等心理功能指标及使用活跃度。结果显示，所有心理功能指标和工作联盟均显著改善，自尊膨胀无变化。活跃天数、总会话数和总时长与第4周的心理功能改善显著相关，而用户消息量无显著关联。
GenMatter: Perceiving Physical Objects with Generative Matter Models
Authors: Eric Li, Arijit Dasgupta, Yoni Friedman, Mathieu Huot, Vikash Mansinghka, Thomas O'Connell, William T. Freeman, Joshua B. Tenenbaum
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-24T02:18:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:26:41+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 12 figures, CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human visual perception offers valuable insights for understanding computational principles of motion-based scene interpretation. Humans robustly detect and segment moving entities that constitute independently moveable chunks of matter, whether observing sparse moving dots, textured surfaces, or naturalistic scenes. In contrast, existing computer vision systems lack a unified approach that works across these diverse settings. Inspired by principles of human perception, we propose a generative model that hierarchically groups low-level motion cues and high-level appearance features into particles (small Gaussians representing local matter), and groups particles into clusters capturing coherently and independently moveable physical entities. We develop a hardware-accelerated inference algorithm based on parallelized block Gibbs sampling to recover stable particle motion and groupings. Our model operates on different kinds of inputs (random dots, stylized textures, or naturalistic RGB video), enabling it to work across settings where biological vision succeeds but existing computer vision approaches do not. We validate this unified framework across three domains: on 2D random dot kinematograms, our approach captures human object perception including graded uncertainty across ambiguous conditions; on a Gestalt-inspired dataset of camouflaged rotating objects, our approach recovers correct 3D structure from motion and thereby accurate 2D object segmentation; and on naturalistic RGB videos, our model tracks the moving 3D matter that makes up deforming objects, enabling robust object-level scene understanding. This work thus establishes a general framework for motion-based perception grounded in principles of human vision.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GenMatter：基于生成物质模型的物理物体感知
人类视觉感知为理解基于运动的场景解释的计算原理提供了宝贵见解。人类能够稳健地检测并分割构成独立可移动物质块的运动实体，无论观察稀疏移动点、纹理表面还是自然场景。相比之下，现有计算机视觉系统缺乏一种能在这些多样化场景中统一工作的方法。受人类感知原理启发，我们提出一种生成模型，该模型将低级运动线索和高级外观特征分层聚类为粒子（代表局部物质的小高斯分布），并将粒子聚类为捕捉可独立且协调运动的物理实体的簇。我们开发了一种基于并行化块Gibbs采样的硬件加速推理算法，以恢复稳定的粒子运动和聚类。该模型可处理多种输入（随机点、风格化纹理或自然RGB视频），使其能够在生物视觉成功但现有计算机视觉方法失败的场景中工作。我们在三个领域验证了该统一框架：在2D随机点运动图中，我们的方法捕捉了人类物体感知，包括在模糊条件下分级的不确定性；在受格式塔启发的伪装旋转物体数据集中，我们的方法从运动中恢复正确的3D结构，从而实现准确的2D物体分割；在自然RGB视频中，我们的模型追踪构成变形物体的移动3D物质，实现稳健的物体级场景理解。本工作因此建立了一种基于人类视觉原理的运动感知通用框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the human ability to robustly perceive and segment moving physical entities across diverse visual inputs, this work introduces GenMatter, a generative model that hierarchically groups motion cues and appearance features into particles representing local matter, then clusters these particles into coherent, independently moveable physical objects. The model employs a hardware-accelerated inference algorithm based on parallelized block Gibbs sampling to recover stable motion and groupings. Experiments across three domains—2D random dot kinematograms, camouflaged rotating objects, and naturalistic RGB videos—demonstrate its effectiveness: it captures human-like object perception with graded uncertainty, recovers accurate 3D structure and 2D segmentation from motion, and tracks 3D matter in deforming objects for robust scene understanding, establishing a unified motion-based perception framework inspired by biological vision.
受人类在不同视觉输入下稳健感知运动物体能力的启发，该研究提出GenMatter模型，通过分层方式将运动线索与外观特征聚类为表示局部物质的粒子，再将粒子聚类为可独立运动的物理实体。模型采用基于并行块吉布斯采样的硬件加速推理算法以稳定恢复运动与聚类。实验在三个领域——二维随机点运动图、伪装旋转物体数据集及自然RGB视频中验证，结果显示该模型能捕捉人类物体感知、从运动中恢复准确三维结构，并实现变形物体的鲁棒性物体级跟踪，证明其通用性和生物可解释性。
Unleashing Infinite Motion: Scaling Expressive Quadrupedal Motion via Generative Video Priors
Authors: Youzhi Liu, Li Gao, Yifei Qian, Liu Liu, Yang Cai, Ziqiao Li
First: 2026-06-26T16:23:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:23:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Quadruped robots have achieved remarkable locomotion, yet their behavioral repertoire remains confined to a few gaits--far from the expressive, companion-like presence long envisioned for them. Attempts to import the humanoid recipe of large-scale motion data have inherited one tacit assumption: that robot motion must first pass through an animal body, making data collection dependent on cooperative animals, reconstruction fragile across species, and retargeting ill-posed across incompatible morphologies. We propose Uni-Mo, a fully automated pipeline that removes the animal from the loop by reframing data scarcity as a generation problem: an LLM proposes motion prompts, a video diffusion model synthesizes the corresponding robot behaviors, and the generated videos are lifted into 3D reference trajectories used to train tracking policies deployed on a real Unitree Go2. To make naively-drifting generations reliably extractable, we introduce an Identity Consistency Loss that enforces appearance coherence across frames. We release Quad-Imaginarium at https://github.com/GaoLii/Quad-Imaginarium.git, the resulting open-source dataset of 7,488 language-annotated quadruped motions (18.5 hours) spanning acrobatic and performative behaviors. We validate 392 randomly sampled motions on a real Unitree Go2 with a 96.7% deployment success rate, complemented by a 97.6% success rate across the full dataset in simulation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：释放无限运动：通过生成式视频先验扩展四足机器人表达性运动
四足机器人已实现卓越的运动能力，但其行为范围仍局限于少数步态，远未达到人们长期期望的富有表现力、如伴侣般的存在形态。尝试借鉴人形机器人大规模运动数据的方法，隐含了一个默认假设：机器人运动必须先经过动物身体，导致数据采集依赖合作动物、跨物种重建脆弱、且在形态不兼容时重定向问题难以解决。我们提出Uni-Mo，一个全自动流程，通过将数据稀缺性重新定义为生成问题，绕过动物环节：大语言模型提出运动提示，视频扩散模型合成对应机器人行为，生成视频被提升为3D参考轨迹，用于训练部署在真实Unitree Go2机器人上的跟踪策略。为使随意漂移的生成结果可靠提取，我们引入身份一致性损失，确保帧间外观一致性。我们开源了Quad-Imaginarium数据集（https://github.com/GaoLii/Quad-Imaginarium.git），包含7,488个语言标注的四足运动（18.5小时），涵盖杂技与表演性行为。我们在真实Unitree Go2机器人上对392个随机采样运动进行验证，部署成功率96.7%，并在仿真环境中对完整数据集实现97.6%的成功率。

为突破四足机器人运动表现力不足的问题，该研究提出Uni-Mo全自动流程，通过将运动生成视为核心任务，绕过依赖动物合作的传统数据采集方式。该方法利用LLM生成运动提示，通过视频扩散模型合成机器人行为，并将视频转化为3D轨迹用于训练真实机器人策略。为确保生成视频中运动可稳定提取，引入身份一致性损失以维持帧间视觉一致性。最终生成的Quad-Imaginarium数据集包含7488个语言标注的运动样本，总时长18.5小时，涵盖杂技与表演类动作。在真实Unitree Go2机器人上验证392个采样动作，部署成功率96.7%，仿真环境中全集成功率97.6%。
Govern the Repository, Not the Agent: Measuring Ecosystem-Level Risk in AI-Native Software
Authors: Daniel Russo
First: 2026-06-26T16:22:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:22:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous coding agents now open and merge pull requests in shared repositories at scale, and the field evaluates them the way it has always evaluated components, one agent at a time, on isolated benchmark tasks. Yet agents that each pass their own tests still leave repositories that accumulate problems no single contribution accounts for. We ask whether this problem belongs to the individual agent or to the repository where it accumulates. We study integration friction, the cost of integrating a contribution into a codebase that other contributors are concurrently changing. Across more than 930,000 agent-authored pull requests, we measure how much of the variation in friction stays with the repository after the contribution, its author, its size, and its agent are accounted for. About half does, and it survives full controls. In the same repositories, agent-authored contributions concentrate this repository-level friction roughly twice as much as human ones (intraclass correlation 0.30 versus 0.16), a gap that holds after controlling for codebase size, age, task shape, process maturity, and merge path. The risk is a property of the ecosystem, not the agent. AI-native software is therefore better measured and governed at the ecosystem level than one agent at a time.
中文标题/摘要
标题：治理仓库而非代理：衡量AI原生软件生态系统的风险
自主编码代理如今大规模地在共享仓库中打开并合并拉取请求，该领域仍像评估组件那样，逐个代理、在孤立基准任务上评估它们。然而，每个代理通过自身测试后，仍会留下仓库中累积的问题，这些问题无法由单个贡献解释。我们探讨这一问题属于单个代理还是累积其问题的仓库。我们研究了集成摩擦——将贡献整合到其他贡献者同时修改的代码库中的成本。在超过93万份代理撰写的拉取请求中，我们衡量了在控制贡献者、大小和代理后，摩擦变化中仍保留于仓库的比例。约一半如此，且在完全控制后仍存在。在相同仓库中，代理撰写的贡献集中了约两倍于人类贡献的仓库级摩擦（组内相关系数0.30对比0.16），这一差距在控制代码库大小、年龄、任务形态、流程成熟度和合并路径后依然存在。风险属于生态系统，而非代理。因此，AI原生软件应从生态系统层面而非单个代理层面进行衡量与治理。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether risks in AI-native software stem from individual autonomous coding agents or from the broader repository ecosystem. By analyzing over 930,000 agent-authored pull requests, the researchers measure 'integration friction'—the difficulty of merging contributions into concurrently evolving codebases—and assess how much of this friction persists after accounting for the agent, author, contribution size, and repository characteristics. They find that roughly half of the friction remains tied to the repository even after full controls, and agent-authored contributions concentrate this friction nearly twice as much as human ones (intraclass correlation 0.30 vs. 0.16), even after adjusting for codebase size, age, task complexity, process maturity, and merge strategy. The results indicate that ecosystem-level risk is inherent and not attributable to individual agents, suggesting AI-native software should be evaluated and governed at the ecosystem level rather than per-agent.
为理解AI编码代理是否在软件生态系统中引入系统性风险，本研究探讨代码合并时产生的集成摩擦（即在并发修改的代码库中合并代理生成代码的成本）是由单个代理引起还是由代码库本身导致。通过对93万多个代理生成的拉取请求进行分析，研究者在控制作者、代理、贡献大小及代码库特征后，发现约一半的摩擦仍与代码库相关，且代理贡献集中了约两倍于人类贡献的此类摩擦（组内相关系数0.30 vs. 0.16），即使在控制代码库规模、年龄、任务结构、流程成熟度和合并路径后仍成立。这表明风险属于生态系统层面，而非单个代理，因此AI原生软件应以生态系统为单位进行评估与治理，而非逐个代理进行。
Disentangling Continuous-Time Latent Dynamics: Identifiability of Latent SDEs via Diffusion Shifts
Authors: Yuanyuan Wang, Wenjie Wang, Haoxuan Li, Mingming Gong, Kun Zhang
First: 2026-06-26T16:18:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:18:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Causal representation learning for time series has developed strong identifiability results in discrete-time latent causal models, but identifiability in continuous-time latent stochastic differential equation (SDE) models remains largely open. We address this gap using environment-induced shifts in diffusion covariance. We study additive-noise latent SDEs observed through an unknown nonlinear diffeomorphism, with shared drift but environment-specific diffusion covariance. We show that two diagonal diffusion regimes with pairwise distinct coordinate-wise variance ratios identify the latent coordinates up to permutation and scaling, without any sparsity assumption on the drift. We first prove this result for linear Ornstein--Uhlenbeck systems and then extend it to general additive-noise latent SDEs. Under mild smoothness, the instantaneous drift-Jacobian causal graph is identifiable up to the same permutation. We propose a two-stage estimator for latent disentanglement and optional graph recovery; experiments on synthetic systems confirm the predicted identifiability boundary, and an application to Hardanger Bridge monitoring data illustrates the approach on real sensor trajectories.
中文标题/摘要
标题：解耦连续时间潜在动态：通过扩散偏移识别潜在随机微分方程的可识别性
时间序列的因果表示学习在离散时间潜在因果模型中已取得强可识别性结果，但连续时间潜在随机微分方程（SDE）模型的可识别性仍基本未解。我们通过环境诱导的扩散协方差偏移来解决这一问题。我们研究通过未知非线性微分同胚观测的加性噪声潜在SDE，具有共享漂移但环境特定的扩散协方差。我们证明，两个对角扩散模式，其坐标方向方差比成对不同，可在不假设漂移稀疏性的前提下，唯一确定潜在坐标（至排列和缩放）。我们首先在线性Ornstein-Uhlenbeck系统中证明该结果，然后推广至一般加性噪声潜在SDE。在温和光滑性条件下，瞬时漂移雅可比因果图可识别至相同排列。我们提出一种两阶段估计器用于潜在解耦及可选图恢复；在合成系统实验中验证了预测的可识别性边界，对哈当格桥监测数据的应用展示了该方法在真实传感器轨迹上的效果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of identifiability results for continuous-time latent stochastic differential equation models, this work leverages environment-induced shifts in diffusion covariance to establish identifiability. It studies additive-noise latent SDEs observed through unknown nonlinear diffeomorphisms, with shared drift but distinct diffusion covariances per environment. The authors prove that two diagonal diffusion regimes with distinct coordinate-wise variance ratios uniquely identify latent coordinates up to permutation and scaling, without requiring sparsity in drift. 为解决连续时间潜变量随机微分方程模型中因果表示学习的可识别性问题，本文提出基于环境诱导扩散协方差变化的方法以解耦潜变量动态。研究考虑通过未知非线性微分同胚观测的加性噪声SDE，其漂移共享但扩散协方差环境特异，证明当两个对角扩散模式具有不同坐标方向方差比时，潜变量坐标可唯一确定至排列与缩放，无需漂移稀疏性假设。方法首先在线性Ornstein-Uhlenbeck系统中建立，后推广至一般加性噪声SDE，且在温和光滑性条件下可识别漂移雅可比因果图至相同排列。提出两阶段估计器用于潜变量解耦与图恢复，合成实验验证了可识别性边界，实际应用在哈当格桥传感器轨迹数据中展示了方法有效性。
Neural Logic Networks for Interpretable Classification
Authors: Vincent Perreault, Katsumi Inoue, Richard Labib, Alain Hertz
First: 2025-08-11T16:49:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:15:54+00:00
Comments: 26 pages, 8 figures, code available at https://github.com/VincentPerreault0/NeuralLogicNetworks
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Traditional neural networks have an impressive classification performance, but what they learn cannot be inspected, verified or extracted. Neural Logic Networks on the other hand have an interpretable structure that enables them to learn a logical mechanism relating the inputs and outputs with AND and OR operations. We generalize these networks with NOT operations and biases that take into account unobserved data and develop a rigorous logical and probabilistic modeling in terms of concept combinations to motivate their use. We also propose a novel factorized IF-THEN rule structure for the model as well as a modified learning algorithm. Our method improves the state-of-the-art in Boolean networks discovery and is able to learn relevant, interpretable rules in tabular classification, notably on examples from the medical and industrial fields where interpretability has tangible value.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于可解释分类的神经逻辑网络

Motivated by the lack of interpretability in traditional neural networks, this work introduces Neural Logic Networks that explicitly model logical relationships using AND, OR, and NOT operations with biases to handle unobserved data. The method employs a factorized IF-THEN rule structure and a modified learning algorithm to enable rigorous probabilistic modeling of concept combinations.
