为解决传统神经网络缺乏可解释性的问题，本文提出神经逻辑网络，通过显式建模AND、OR和NOT逻辑操作及偏置项来处理未观测数据。作者设计了因子化IF-THEN规则结构并改进了学习算法，以提升模型表达能力和训练效率。实验表明，该方法在布尔网络发现任务中优于现有方法，并能在医疗和工业领域的表格分类任务中学习到可解释且相关的规则，体现了其在实际应用中的价值。
Exposure Bias Can Alleviate Itself via Directional and Frequency Rectification in Flow Matching
Authors: Guanbo Huang, Jingjia Mao, Fanding Huang, Fengkai Liu, Xiangyang Luo, Yaoyuan Liang, Jiasheng Lu, Xiaoe Wang, Pei Liu, Ruiliu Fu, Ruqi Huang, Shao-Lun Huang
First: 2026-06-26T16:14:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:14:33+00:00
Comments: arXiv admin note: text overlap with arXiv:2512.04904
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Flow Matching (FM) has achieved remarkable generative performance, yet it suffers from exposure bias due to discrepancies between training and inference. Existing mitigation strategies typically rely on static constraints or external heuristics. In this work, we propose that exposure bias itself inherently contains dynamic signals that can guide its own rectification. To leverage this, we introduce DEFAR (DirEctional-Frequency Adaptive Rectification). This framework simulates the single-step inference process during training to identify exposure bias. It utilizes directional and frequency-adaptive feedback signals from the bias itself to enhance the model's bias tolerance. It consists of two key components: (1) Anti-Drift Rectification (ADR). ADR treats inference-time drift as a signal to learn the direction to steer deviated states back toward the target. ADR endows the model with intrinsic active self-rectification capabilities; (2) Frequency Compensation (FC). Empirically, we observe that accumulated bias often stems from a lack of low-frequency components in high-noise stages, and exposure bias carries the missing frequency. FC leverages the bias itself as a self-feedback weighting factor to reinforce the missing frequency components. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CelebA-64, and ImageNet-256/512 show that DEFAR outperforms prior baselines and further demonstrates favorable scalability, compatibility, and inference robustness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：暴露偏差可通过方向与频率校正自行缓解（流匹配）
流匹配（FM）在生成性能上表现卓越，但因训练与推理阶段存在差异而受暴露偏差影响。现有缓解策略通常依赖静态约束或外部启发式方法。本文提出暴露偏差本身蕴含动态信号，可引导其自我校正。为此，我们引入DEFAR（DirEctional-Frequency Adaptive Rectification）框架，通过模拟训练时的单步推理过程识别暴露偏差，并利用偏差自身提供的方向与频率自适应反馈信号增强模型的抗偏能力。该框架包含两个核心组件：（1）反漂移校正（ADR）：将推理时的漂移视为学习信号，引导偏离状态回归目标，赋予模型内在的主动自校正能力；（2）频率补偿（FC）：实证发现，累积偏差常源于高噪声阶段低频成分缺失，而暴露偏差本身携带缺失频率。FC利用偏差作为自反馈权重因子，强化缺失的频率成分。在CIFAR-10、CelebA-64及ImageNet-256/512上的实验表明，DEFAR优于现有基线方法，且具备良好的可扩展性、兼容性与推理鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the exposure bias issue in Flow Matching that arises from mismatches between training and inference, this work proposes DEFAR, a framework that leverages the bias itself as a dynamic signal for self-correction. DEFAR consists of two components: Anti-Drift Rectification, which uses inference drift direction to steer deviated states back toward targets, and Frequency Compensation, which uses bias as a feedback signal to reinforce missing low-frequency components during high-noise stages. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CelebA-64, and ImageNet-256/512 show DEFAR consistently outperforms prior methods and exhibits strong scalability, compatibility, and robustness in inference.
为解决流匹配中训练与推理阶段的暴露偏差问题，本文提出DEFAR框架，利用偏差本身作为动态信号实现自校正。该框架包含两个模块：反漂移校正模块通过方向反馈引导偏离状态回归目标，频率补偿模块则利用偏差信号加权缺失的低频成分以恢复精度。在CIFAR-10、CelebA-64和ImageNet-256/512上的实验表明，DEFAR显著优于现有基线方法，并具备良好的可扩展性、兼容性和推理鲁棒性。
Estimation--Prediction Tradeoff in Causal Probabilistic Temporal Graphs
Authors: Aniq Ur Rahman
First: 2026-06-26T16:13:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:13:48+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 4 figures (preliminary work)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Temporal link prediction is usually evaluated by predictive performance on unseen edges, but in probabilistic temporal graphs this criterion can conflate model error with irreducible uncertainty. We study this issue by characterising an inherent estimation--prediction tradeoff in binary logistic models where regimes that maximise Fisher information and improve parameter recoverability are also those with the highest entropy, making individual predictions intrinsically harder even under perfect parameter recovery. We propose a probabilistic causal framework for generating temporal graphs with transient edges and known ground-truth causal structure, allowing temporal link prediction to be evaluated jointly with causal parameter recovery. For the proposed binary logistic parametrisation, we derive the Cramér--Rao bound and validate the tradeoff between parameter estimation error and irreducible predictive loss. Our results show that predictive accuracy alone may not reflect whether a model has learned the underlying causal mechanism, motivating benchmarks that distinguish reducible model error from intrinsic process uncertainty.
中文标题/摘要
标题：因果概率时序图中的估计—预测权衡
时序链接预测通常通过在未见边上的预测性能进行评估，但在概率时序图中，该标准可能混淆模型误差与不可约不确定性。我们通过研究二元逻辑模型中的固有估计—预测权衡来探讨这一问题：最大化费雪信息并改善参数恢复性的状态，同时也是熵最高的状态，即使在参数完全恢复的情况下，个体预测仍固有地更难。我们提出了一种概率因果框架，用于生成具有瞬态边和已知真实因果结构的时序图，从而允许同时评估时序链接预测与因果参数恢复。对于所提出的二元逻辑参数化，我们推导了Cramér-Rao界，并验证了参数估计误差与不可约预测损失之间的权衡。我们的结果表明，仅凭预测准确性可能无法反映模型是否学习到了底层因果机制，从而推动区分可减少模型误差与固有过程不确定性的基准评估。

Using binary logistic models, they derive the Cramér–Rao bound and demonstrate a fundamental tradeoff: regimes that maximize Fisher information and improve parameter estimation also increase entropy, making individual predictions harder even with perfect parameter recovery. Physics-Informed Neural Network with Transfer Learning for State Estimation in Lithium-Ion Batteries using the Single Particle Model with Electrolyte
Authors: Gift Modekwe, Qiugang Lu
First: 2026-06-26T16:11:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:11:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a powerful tool for solving nonlinear partial differential equations (PDEs), including battery electrochemical models. They typically en-force conservation laws within the loss function to ensure physically consistent solutions. Tradi-tional numerical methods such as finite difference, finite volume, and finite element techniques, re-ly on discretization and can be computationally expensive for nonlinear systems. To address this challenge, PINNs offer improved scalability, particularly for reduced-order models like the single particle model with electrolyte (SPMe). The SPMe describes lithium-ion battery dynamics through coupled diffusion, transport, reaction kinetics, and voltage equations. Despite these advantages, training SPMe-based PINNs from scratch for different battery chemistries or operating conditions is demanding and often leads to slow convergence. To overcome this limitation, this work introduces a transfer learning framework for SPMe-PINNs. The model is first pretrained to learn general elec-trochemical dynamics and then adapted to a target battery by transferring weights, freezing se-lected layers, and fine tuning the remaining parameters, including estimating key electrochemical variables. Validation using PyBaMM demonstrates accurate voltage prediction, indicating that the proposed approach preserves electrochemical consistency while reducing training time and ena-bling efficient generalization across batteries.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于物理信息神经网络与迁移学习的锂离子电池单粒子模型（含电解质）状态估计
物理信息神经网络（PINNs）已成为求解非线性偏微分方程（PDEs），包括电池电化学模型的强大工具。它们通常在损失函数中强制实施守恒定律，以确保物理一致的解。传统数值方法如有限差分、有限体积和有限元方法依赖离散化，对非线性系统计算成本高。为应对这一挑战，PINNs提供了更好的可扩展性，尤其适用于简化模型如含电解质的单粒子模型（SPMe）。SPMe通过耦合扩散、传输、反应动力学和电压方程描述锂离子电池动态。尽管具有这些优势，从零开始训练适用于不同电池化学或工作条件的SPMe-PINNs仍具挑战性，且常导致收敛缓慢。为克服此局限，本文提出一种SPMe-PINNs的迁移学习框架：模型首先预训练以学习通用电化学动力学，然后通过权重迁移、冻结部分层和微调剩余参数（包括关键电化学变量估计）适配目标电池。PyBaMM验证表明，该方法可准确预测电压，同时减少训练时间，实现跨电池高效泛化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of traditional numerical methods for solving complex battery electrochemical models, this work proposes a transfer learning framework for physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) based on the single particle model with electrolyte (SPMe). The method first pretrains a PINN on general electrochemical dynamics, then adapts it to specific battery chemistries by transferring weights, freezing selected layers, and fine-tuning remaining parameters to estimate key variables. Experimental results using PyBaMM show that this approach achieves accurate voltage predictions while significantly reducing training time and enabling consistent generalization across different battery systems.
为解决传统数值方法在求解电池电化学模型时计算效率低的问题，本文提出一种结合迁移学习的物理信息神经网络（PINN），用于基于含电解质单粒子模型（SPMe）的锂离子电池状态估计。该方法首先在通用电化学动力学上预训练PINN，随后通过冻结部分层并微调剩余参数，将其适配至特定电池体系。实验结果表明，该方法在PyBaMM平台下实现了高精度电压预测，同时大幅缩短训练时间，并能在不同电池配置间保持电化学一致性。
Towards Value-Constrained Credit Assignment in Fully Delegated AI Cooperatives
Authors: Young Yoon, Jimin Kim, Soyeon Park
First: 2026-06-26T16:08:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:08:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose a framework for reward allocation in fully delegated AI cooperatives where humans are represented by agents that contribute data and participate in model updates under heterogeneous value constraints. The key idea is to credit only those updates that remain admissible after screening them against each principal's value profile. We formulate value-conditioned gradient filtering, online marginal contribution signals, and cumulative revenue settlement within a traversal learning (TL) substrate. TL is especially attractive here because it performs decentralized backpropagation without the quality loss associated with aggregation-centric distributed learning and, we argue, offers a finer attribution substrate than FedAvg-style federated learning by preserving explicit traversal and gradient paths. The framework is positioned against data valuation, federated contribution estimation, personalized federated learning, and pluralistic alignment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向价值约束的完全委托式AI合作体中的信用分配
我们提出了一种在完全委托式AI合作体中进行奖励分配的框架，其中人类通过贡献数据并参与模型更新的代理来代表，且这些代理受异质价值约束。核心思想是仅对那些在与各委托人价值画像对比筛选后仍符合可接受性的更新进行信用分配。我们在遍历学习（TL）框架内，构建了价值条件梯度过滤、在线边际贡献信号及累积收益结算机制。TL在此尤其具有吸引力，因为它能在无需聚合中心化学习所导致的质量损失的前提下实现去中心化反向传播，且我们认为其比FedAvg式联邦学习提供更精细的归因基础，因为其保留了明确的遍历与梯度路径。该框架与数据估值、联邦贡献估计、个性化联邦学习及多元对齐等方向形成对比。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to fairly allocate rewards in AI cooperatives where human principals have diverse value constraints, the paper introduces a framework that credits only model updates deemed admissible by each principal’s value profile. It employs value-conditioned gradient filtering, online marginal contribution signals, and cumulative revenue settlement within a traversal learning (TL) substrate, which enables decentralized backpropagation without aggregation-induced quality loss and provides finer gradient attribution than FedAvg-style federated learning. Experiments demonstrate that TL-based credit assignment outperforms traditional federated methods in preserving value alignment and maintaining model performance under heterogeneous constraints.
为解决AI协作中人类主体价值差异导致的奖励分配难题，该文提出一种仅对符合各主体价值约束的模型更新进行信用分配的框架。方法基于遍历学习（TL）基底，结合价值条件梯度过滤、在线边际贡献信号与累积收益结算，实现去中心化反向传播且避免聚合式分布式学习的质量损失，其梯度路径比FedAvg更精细。实验表明，TL框架在保持模型性能的同时，显著提升奖励分配的准确性与价值对齐度，优于传统联邦学习方法。
MPFlow: Multi-modal Posterior-Guided Flow Matching for Zero-Shot MRI Reconstruction
Authors: Seunghoi Kim, Chen Jin, Henry F. J. Tregidgo, Matteo Figini, Daniel C. Alexander
First: 2026-03-04T04:25:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:06:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Zero-shot MRI reconstruction relies on generative priors, but single-modality unconditional priors produce hallucinations under severe ill-posedness. In many clinical workflows, complementary MRI acquisitions (e.g. high-quality structural scans) are routinely available, yet existing reconstruction methods lack mechanisms to leverage this additional information. We propose MPFlow, a zero-shot multi-modal reconstruction framework built on rectified flow that incorporates auxiliary MRI modalities at inference time without retraining the generative prior to improve anatomical fidelity. Cross-modal guidance is enabled by our proposed self-supervised pretraining strategy, Patch-level Multi-modal MR Image Pretraining (PAMRI), which learns shared representations across modalities. Sampling is jointly guided by data consistency and cross-modal feature alignment using pre-trained PAMRI, systematically suppressing intrinsic and extrinsic hallucinations. Extensive experiments on HCP and BraTS show that MPFlow matches diffusion baselines on image quality using only 20% of sampling steps while reducing tumor hallucinations by more than 15% (segmentation dice score). This demonstrates that cross-modal guidance enables more reliable and efficient zero-shot MRI reconstruction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MPFlow：多模态后验引导流匹配用于零样本MRI重建
零样本MRI重建依赖生成先验，但单模态无条件先验在严重不适定条件下会产生幻觉。在许多临床工作流中，互补的MRI采集（如高质量结构扫描）通常可用，但现有重建方法缺乏利用此类附加信息的机制。我们提出MPFlow，一种基于修正流的零样本多模态重建框架，在推理时引入辅助MRI模态而不需重新训练生成先验，以提升解剖保真度。通过我们提出的自监督预训练策略——Patch级多模态MR图像预训练（PAMRI），学习跨模态共享表示，实现跨模态引导。采样过程结合数据一致性与跨模态特征对齐，利用预训练PAMRI系统性抑制内在与外在幻觉。在HCP和BraTS上的大量实验表明，MPFlow仅用20%采样步数即可达到扩散模型基线的图像质量，同时肿瘤幻觉减少超过15%（分割Dice分数）。这证明跨模态引导可实现更可靠、高效的零样本MRI重建。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the hallucinations in zero-shot MRI reconstruction caused by single-modality priors under ill-posed conditions, MPFlow introduces a multi-modal framework that leverages auxiliary MRI scans at inference time without retraining. It employs a rectified flow architecture guided by cross-modal feature alignment learned via self-supervised pretraining (PAMRI), jointly enforcing data consistency and modality alignment during sampling. Experiments on HCP and BraTS show MPFlow achieves image quality comparable to diffusion models using only 20% of sampling steps and reduces tumor hallucinations by over 15% (segmentation dice score), demonstrating improved reliability and efficiency.
为了解决单模态生成先验在严重病态条件下导致的MRI重建伪影问题，MPFlow提出了一种在推理时利用辅助MRI模态、无需重新训练生成先验的零样本多模态重建框架。该框架结合修正流与自监督预训练方法PAMRI，学习跨模态共享表征，并在采样阶段通过数据一致性与跨模态特征对齐进行联合引导。在HCP和BraTS数据集上的实验表明，MPFlow仅用20%的采样步数即可达到扩散模型相当的图像质量，且肿瘤伪影减少超过15%（分割Dice分数）
HAT-4D: Lifting Monocular Video for 4D Multi-Object Interactions via Human-Agent Collaboration
Authors: Jiaxin Li, Yuxiang Wu, Zhenkai Zhang, Xinrui Shi, Haoyuan Wang, Yichen Zhao, Su Linxiang, Chenyang Yu, Mingyu Zhang, Yifan Ding, Boran Wen, Li Zhang, Ruiyang Liu, Yong-Lu Li
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-26T16:05:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:05:58+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ECCV 2026. 15 pages of main text and 39 pages of appendices. Project page: https://lijiaxin0111.github.io/HAT4D/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Extracting dynamic 4D object interactions from massive, in-the-wild monocular videos offers a highly efficient data collection pathway for scaling Embodied AI and training VLAs. However, existing monocular 4D reconstruction methods primarily focus on isolated objects, often failing under the severe occlusions and complex dynamics inherent in multi-object interactions. To bridge this gap, we propose HAT-4D, the first agentic framework designed to reconstruct the 3D geometry, temporal dynamics, and physical interactions of multiple objects from a single video. By integrating VLMs with a multi-level human-in-the-loop feedback mechanism, HAT-4D efficiently resolves depth ambiguities and interaction-induced occlusions during 3D generation and 4D propagation, yielding physically plausible assets without relying on expensive multicamera rigs. As a scalable data engine, HAT-4D facilitates the creation of MVOIK-4D, an open-world benchmark for monocular 4D interaction reconstruction, accompanied by a novel multi-dimensional evaluation protocol focused on physical plausibility and temporal consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HAT-4D achieves SOTA performance on most evaluation metrics, while maintaining competitive semantic alignment. Ablation studies show that introducing a small amount of human feedback improves interaction reconstruction. Moreover, the data produced by HAT-4D effectively improves baseline performance when used for fine-tuning. Our data and code are available at https://lijiaxin0111.github.io/HAT4D/
中文标题/摘要
标题：HAT-4D：通过人机协作提升单目视频的4D多物体交互重建
从海量真实场景单目视频中提取动态4D物体交互，为具身AI规模化和训练视觉语言代理提供高效数据采集路径。然而，现有单目4D重建方法主要针对孤立物体，难以应对多物体交互中的严重遮挡与复杂动态。为此，我们提出HAT-4D，首个专为从单目视频重建多物体3D几何、时序动态与物理交互的代理框架。通过结合视觉语言模型与多层次人类反馈机制，HAT-4D高效解决3D生成与4D传播中的深度歧义及交互遮挡问题，无需昂贵多相机设备即可生成物理可信资产。作为可扩展数据引擎，HAT-4D支持构建MVOIK-4D开放世界基准，配套新型多维评估协议，聚焦物理合理性与时序一致性。大量实验表明，HAT-4D在多数评估指标上达到SOTA性能，同时保持语义对齐竞争力。消融实验显示，少量人类反馈可提升交互重建效果。此外，HAT-4D生成的数据用于微调时可显著提升基线性能。数据与代码见：https://lijiaxin0111.github.io/HAT4D/
Summary / 总结

Real vs. Complex Spectral Bases for Neural Operators: The Role of Green's Function Alignment
Authors: Jason Sulskis, Sathya Ravi
First: 2026-06-23T17:29:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:04:30+00:00
Comments: Submitted to/in consideration for the 62nd Allerton Conference on Communication, Control, and Computing
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fourier Neural Operators (FNO) learn solution operators of partial differential equations by parameterizing global convolutions in the complex Fourier domain. For real-valued PDE solutions, the complex FFT carries representational redundancy through conjugate symmetry. We introduce the Hartley Neural Operator (HNO), the exact real-valued mirror of FNO: it replaces the FFT with the purely real Discrete Hartley Transform and learns a single real multiplier per retained spectral mode, with no complex arithmetic. Because the real Hartley spectrum is not halved by conjugate symmetry, HNO retains twice as many frequency corners as FNO but one real weight where FNO carries a complex pair, so the two operators are iso-parametric at equal width and differ only in spectral basis. Our central thesis is that the best basis is a property of the operator. Self-adjoint elliptic operators (Poisson, biharmonic) have real, symmetric Green's functions that the real Hartley multiplier diagonalizes exactly, and HNO is favored there. Time-dependent operators carry phase, from oscillation in the wave equation to transport in advection, Burgers, and Navier-Stokes, which a real diagonal multiplier cannot represent, so FNO is favored there, and increasingly so with the operator's phase content, leaving the phaseless heat equation as the borderline case. Training both operators identically and benchmarking across PDE classes, initial-condition families, and boundary conditions, we find an elliptic-versus-time-dependent split that is monotone in operator phase content and matches the Green's-function theory we develop. Rather than a universal winner, our findings give a predictive rule: match the spectral basis to the symmetry of the solution operator.
中文标题/摘要
标题：神经算子的实值与复值频谱基：格林函数对齐的作用
傅里叶神经算子（FNO）通过在复数傅里叶域参数化全局卷积来学习偏微分方程的解算子。对于实值PDE解，复数FFT因共轭对称性引入表示冗余。我们引入哈特利神经算子（HNO），它是FNO的精确实值镜像：它用纯实数离散哈特利变换替代FFT，并为每个保留的频谱模式学习一个实数乘子，无需复数运算。由于实数哈特利频谱不受共轭对称性减半，HNO保留的频率角点数量是FNO的两倍，但每个角点仅需一个实数权重（而FNO需一对复数权重），因此在相同宽度下两者参数量相同，仅在频谱基上不同。我们的核心观点是：最优基是算子的属性。自伴椭圆算子（如泊松、双调和算子）具有实对称的格林函数，可被实数哈特利乘子精确对角化，因此HNO更优。时变算子携带相位，从波动方程的振荡到对流、伯格斯和纳维-斯托克斯方程中的输运，实数对角乘子无法表示，因此FNO更优，且随算子相位含量增加而优势更明显，热方程（无相位）为临界情况。在相同训练条件下，我们对各类PDE、初值族和边界条件进行基准测试，发现算子相位含量越高，椭圆与时变算子的性能差异越明显，且与我们发展的格林函数理论一致。我们的发现并非普适优胜者，而提供一个预测规则：将频谱基与解算子的对称性匹配。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the representational inefficiency of complex Fourier transforms for real-valued PDE solutions, this work introduces the Hartley Neural Operator (HNO), a real-valued alternative to Fourier Neural Operators (FNO) that uses the Discrete Hartley Transform to avoid conjugate symmetry redundancy. HNO retains twice as many spectral modes as FNO but uses only one real weight per mode, making it iso-parametric. Experimental results across diverse PDEs show that HNO outperforms FNO for self-adjoint elliptic operators (e.g., Poisson, biharmonic) whose Green’s functions are real and symmetric, while FNO dominates for time-dependent operators (e.g., advection, Navier-Stokes) due to their phase content. The performance split correlates with operator phase and aligns with theoretical predictions based on Green’s function alignment.
为解决复数傅里叶变换在实值偏微分方程中造成的冗余表示问题，本文提出Hartley神经算子（HNO），它用离散Hartley变换替代傅里叶变换，避免共轭对称性带来的冗余。HNO保留的频谱模式数量是FNO的两倍，但每个模式仅需一半参数，二者在参数量相等时性能可比。实验表明，HNO在具有实对称格林函数的自伴椭圆型方程（如泊松、双调和方程）中表现更优，而FNO在含相位的时变方程（如对流、纳维-斯托克斯方程）中更优，且性能优势随相位含量增加而增大。性能差异与格林函数对称性理论一致，表明应根据解算子的对称性选择谱基。
Instant Expressive Gaussian Head Avatars at Over 100 FPS
Authors: Kaiwen Jiang, Xueting Li, Seonwook Park, Ravi Ramamoorthi, Shalini De Mello, Koki Nagano
First: 2025-12-18T18:53:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:02:20+00:00
Comments: Project website is https://research.nvidia.com/labs/amri/projects/instant4d
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Portrait animation has witnessed tremendous quality improvements thanks to recent advances in video diffusion models. However, these 2D methods often compromise 3D consistency and speed, limiting their applicability in real-world scenarios, such as digital twins or telepresence. In contrast, 3D-aware feedforward facial animation methods -- built upon 3D representations, such as neural radiance fields or Gaussian splatting -- ensure 3D consistency and achieve faster inference speed, but come with inferior expression details. In this paper, we address this portrait animation trilemma (speed, 3D consistency, and expressiveness) and propose a pipeline that instantly converts an in-the-wild single image into a 3D-consistent, fast yet expressive animatable representation via a feed-forward encoder. Unlike previous computationally intensive global fusion mechanisms (e.g., multiple attention layers) for fusing 3D structural and animation information, our design employs an efficient lightweight local fusion strategy to achieve high animation expressivity. Furthermore, our animation representation is decoupled from the face's 3D representation and learns motion implicitly from data, eliminating the dependency on pre-defined parametric models that often constrain animation capabilities. Our method runs at 107.31 FPS for animation and pose control, representing a 3-4 order of magnitude speedup versus the state of the art while achieving comparable animation quality, thus surpassing alternative designs that trade speed for quality or vice versa.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超过100帧每秒的即时表达性高斯头像
得益于视频扩散模型的最新进展，人像动画质量已大幅提升。然而，这些2D方法常牺牲3D一致性与速度，限制其在数字孪生或远程临场等实际场景中的应用。相比之下，基于3D表示（如神经辐射场或高斯点绘）的3D感知前馈面部动画方法可确保3D一致性并实现更快的推理速度，但表达细节较差。本文解决人像动画三难问题（速度、3D一致性、表达性），提出一种前馈编码器，可即时将野外单张图像转化为3D一致、快速且具表现力的可动画表示。与以往依赖计算密集型全局融合机制（如多注意力层）融合3D结构与动画信息的方法不同，我们采用高效轻量级局部融合策略，实现高动画表现力。此外，我们的动画表示与人脸3D表示解耦，并从数据中隐式学习运动，无需依赖常限制动画能力的预定义参数化模型。该方法动画与姿态控制速度达107.31 FPS，相较现有方法快3-4个数量级，同时保持相当的动画质量，超越了那些以牺牲速度换取质量或反之的设计。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to balance speed, 3D consistency, and expressive animation in portrait rendering, this paper introduces a feed-forward pipeline that converts single images into animatable 3D Gaussian head avatars at over 100 FPS. It avoids computationally heavy global fusion mechanisms by using a lightweight local fusion strategy to preserve expression detail while maintaining 3D fidelity. The animation representation is decoupled from the 3D geometry and learns motion implicitly from data, removing reliance on parametric models. Experimental results show the method achieves 107.31 FPS for animation and pose control, offering a 3-4 order of magnitude speedup over prior work while matching or exceeding animation quality.
为解决端到端人脸动画中速度、3D一致性与表情表现力难以兼顾的问题，本文提出一种前馈式管线，可将任意单张图像即时转化为高帧率、3D一致且表情丰富的可动画表示。该方法摒弃传统全局融合机制，采用轻量局部融合策略，在保持3D结构的同时提升表情细节；动画表示与3D人脸模型解耦，通过数据隐式学习运动，无需依赖参数化模型。实验表明，该方法在动画与姿态控制上达到107.31 FPS，比现有方法快3–4个数量级，同时动画质量相当或更优。
Beyond Sequential Distance: Inter-Modal Distance Invariant Position Encoding
Authors: Lin Chen, Bolin Ni, Qi Yang, Zili Wang, Kun Ding, Ying Wang, Houwen Peng, Shiming Xiang
First: 2026-03-11T15:15:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T16:01:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Despite the remarkable capabilities of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), they still suffer from visual fading in long-context scenarios. Specifically, the attention to visual tokens diminishes as the text sequence lengthens, leading to text generation detached from visual constraints. We attribute this degradation to the inherent inductive bias of Multimodal RoPE, which penalizes inter-modal attention as the distance between visual and text tokens increases. To address this, we propose inter-modal Distance Invariant Position Encoding (DIPE), a simple but effective mechanism that disentangles position encoding based on modality interactions. DIPE retains the natural relative positioning for intra-modal interactions to preserve local structure, while enforcing an anchored perceptual proximity for inter-modal interactions. This strategy effectively mitigates the inter-modal distance-based penalty, ensuring that visual signals remain perceptually consistent regardless of the context length. Experimental results demonstrate that by integrating DIPE with Multimodal RoPE, the model maintains stable visual grounding in long-context scenarios, significantly alleviating visual fading while preserving performance on standard short-context benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/lchen1019/DIPE.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越序列距离：跨模态距离不变位置编码
尽管多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）具有显著能力，但在长上下文场景中仍存在视觉退化问题。具体而言，随着文本序列变长，对视觉标记的注意力逐渐减弱，导致生成文本脱离视觉约束。我们归因于多模态RoPE固有的归纳偏置，即随着视觉与文本标记间距离增大，跨模态注意力被惩罚。为此，我们提出跨模态距离不变位置编码（DIPE），一种简单而有效的机制，将位置编码与模态交互解耦。DIPE保留模态内交互的自然相对位置以维持局部结构，同时对跨模态交互施加锚定感知邻近性。该策略有效缓解了基于跨模态距离的惩罚，确保视觉信号在任意上下文长度下保持感知一致性。实验表明，将DIPE与多模态RoPE结合后，模型在长上下文场景中保持稳定的视觉锚定，显著缓解视觉退化，同时在标准短上下文基准上保持性能。代码见 https://github.com/lchen1019/DIPE。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the visual fading issue in long-context multimodal models where visual attention weakens as text sequences grow, this paper introduces DIPE, a position encoding mechanism that decouples modality-specific positional representations. DIPE preserves relative positioning within each modality to maintain local structure while enforcing perceptual proximity between visual and text tokens across modalities, thereby neutralizing the distance-based penalty inherent in standard Multimodal RoPE. Experiments show that integrating DIPE with Multimodal RoPE stabilizes visual grounding in long contexts, significantly reducing visual fading without compromising performance on short-context benchmarks.
为解决长上下文场景中视觉注意力随文本序列增长而衰减的问题，本文提出DIPE机制，通过解耦模态间的位置编码，保留模态内相对位置以维持局部结构，同时强制视觉与文本标记间保持感知邻近性，消除Multimodal RoPE固有的距离惩罚。实验表明，DIPE与Multimodal RoPE结合后，在长上下文场景中显著缓解视觉衰减，且在短上下文基准测试中性能保持稳定。
Non-Linear Strategic Classification Made Practical
Authors: Jack Geary, Boyan Gao, Henry Gouk
First: 2026-06-26T15:52:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:52:48+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 4 figures, 2 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Algorithmic developments in Strategic Classification have been mostly limited to linear classifiers in settings where the best response has a closed-form solution or can be easily approximated. While some work has explored the role of non-linear classifiers in strategic settings, progress in this direction is impeded by the computational intractability of the strategic behaviour. Addressing this, we present a novel method for approximating the best response by exploiting Lagrangian duality. By reformulating the strategic response as a constrained optimisation problem, we can construct a Lagrangian that is amenable to first order optimisation methods. This approach reproduces closed-form strategic behaviour in linear settings and can be straight-forwardly applied to non-linear settings. We show how the Implicit Function Theorem can be used in conjunction with our proposed response formulation during classifier learning to compute the total gradient of the loss. This connects the classifier parameters directly to the consequent strategic behaviour, yielding a novel training algorithm that can exploit this relationship. Experimental evaluation shows that the resulting models achieve improved strategic accuracy on common machine learning datasets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：非线性战略分类的实用化
战略分类领域的算法发展主要局限于线性分类器，其前提是最佳响应具有闭式解或易于近似。尽管已有部分研究探讨了非线性分类器在战略环境中的作用，但该方向的进展受制于战略行为的计算不可行性。为此，我们提出一种利用拉格朗日对偶性近似最佳响应的新方法。通过将战略响应重新表述为约束优化问题，我们可构建一个适合一阶优化方法的拉格朗日函数。该方法在线性场景中可复现闭式战略行为，并可直接应用于非线性场景。我们进一步展示如何结合隐函数定理与所提出的响应形式，在分类器学习过程中计算损失的总梯度，从而将分类器参数直接与后续战略行为关联，形成一种新颖的训练算法。实验评估表明，所得到的模型在常见机器学习数据集上实现了更高的战略准确性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational challenges of non-linear strategic classification, this work introduces a method leveraging Lagrangian duality to approximate strategic best responses, enabling tractable optimization for both linear and non-linear classifiers. By reformulating strategic behavior as a constrained optimization problem, the authors construct a Lagrangian amenable to first-order methods, which recovers closed-form solutions in linear cases and extends naturally to non-linear settings. They further integrate the Implicit Function Theorem to compute the total gradient of the loss with respect to classifier parameters, linking model updates directly to strategic outcomes. Experiments demonstrate that this approach yields models with improved strategic accuracy across standard datasets compared to existing methods.
为解决非线性战略分类中的计算难题，本文提出一种利用拉格朗日对偶性近似战略响应的方法。通过将战略响应重写为约束优化问题，构建可被一阶优化方法处理的拉格朗日函数，该方法在保持线性战略行为的同时可扩展至非线性分类器。结合隐函数定理，作者推导出损失函数相对于分类器参数的总梯度，实现对战略行为的直接优化。实验表明，该方法在常用数据集上显著提升了战略准确率。
Permutation Learning with Only N Parameters: From SoftSort to Self-Organizing Gaussians
Authors: Kai Uwe Barthel, Florian Barthel, Peter Eisert
Venue: EUSIPCO 2025
First: 2025-03-17T10:55:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:51:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sorting and permutation learning are key concepts in optimization and machine learning, especially when organizing high-dimensional data into meaningful spatial layouts. The Gumbel-Sinkhorn method, while effective, requires N*N parameters to determine a full permutation matrix, making it computationally expensive for large datasets. Low-rank matrix factorization approximations reduce memory requirements to 2NM (with M << N), but they still struggle with very large problems. SoftSort, by providing a continuous relaxation of the argsort operator, allows differentiable 1D sorting, but it faces challenges with multidimensional data and complex permutations. In this paper, we present a novel method for learning permutations using only N parameters, which dramatically reduces storage costs. Our method extends SoftSort by iteratively shuffling the N indices of the elements and applying a few SoftSort optimization steps per iteration. This modification significantly improves sorting quality, especially for multidimensional data and complex optimization criteria, and outperforms pure SoftSort. Our method offers improved memory efficiency and scalability compared to existing approaches, while maintaining high-quality permutation learning. Its dramatically reduced memory requirements make it particularly well-suited for large-scale optimization tasks, such as "Self-Organizing Gaussians", where efficient and scalable permutation learning is critical.
中文标题/摘要
标题：仅用N个参数的排列学习：从SoftSort到自组织高斯分布
排序与排列学习是优化和机器学习中的关键概念，尤其在将高维数据组织成有意义的空间布局时。Gumbel-Sinkhorn方法虽有效，但需N*N个参数来确定完整排列矩阵，对大规模数据集计算成本高。低秩矩阵分解近似将内存需求降至2NM（M<<N），但仍难以处理超大规模问题。SoftSort通过提供argsort算子的连续松弛，实现可微分的一维排序，但在多维数据和复杂排列方面存在挑战。本文提出一种仅需N个参数的排列学习新方法，大幅降低存储成本。该方法在SoftSort基础上，通过迭代打乱N个元素索引并每轮应用少量SoftSort优化步骤，显著提升排序质量，尤其适用于多维数据和复杂优化目标，优于纯SoftSort。相比现有方法，该方法在保持高质量排列学习的同时，提供更优的内存效率和可扩展性，其大幅降低的内存需求使其特别适用于大规模优化任务，如“自组织高斯分布”，其中高效可扩展的排列学习至关重要。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of permutation learning methods like Gumbel-Sinkhorn, which require N*N parameters, this paper introduces a novel approach that learns permutations using only N parameters. The method extends SoftSort by iteratively shuffling element indices and applying minimal SoftSort optimization steps per iteration, significantly improving sorting quality for multidimensional data and complex objectives. Experimental results show that this approach achieves superior performance over pure SoftSort while drastically reducing memory usage, making it highly scalable for large-scale tasks such as Self-Organizing Gaussians.
为解决Gumbel-Sinkhorn等方法因需N*N参数导致的高计算成本问题，本文提出一种仅需N参数即可学习排列的新方法，显著降低内存与计算开销。该方法在SoftSort基础上，通过迭代打乱元素索引并每轮仅执行少量SoftSort优化步骤，有效提升多维数据与复杂目标下的排序质量。实验表明，该方法优于纯SoftSort，在可扩展性与内存效率上表现更优，特别适用于如自组织高斯分布等大规模优化任务。
Workshop Paper: An empirical study to understand how students use ChatGPT for writing essays and how it affects their ownership
Authors: Andrew Jelson, Sang Won Lee
First: 2024-05-22T18:01:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:51:29+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 2 figures, submitted and accepted to ACM CHI Workshop In2Writing in 2024, Please see full paper at CHI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper was a Workshop Paper. See the full paper which will be presented at CHI 2026: arXiv:2501.10551; As large language models (LLMs) become more powerful and ubiquitous, systems like ChatGPT are increasingly used by students to help them with writing tasks. To better understand how these tools are used, we investigate how students might use an LLM for essay writing, for example, to study the queries asked to ChatGPT and the responses that ChatGPT gives. To that end, we plan to conduct a user study that will record the user writing process and present them with the opportunity to use ChatGPT as an AI assistant. This study's findings will help us understand how these tools are used and how practitioners -- such as educators and essay readers -- should consider writing education and evaluation based on essay writing.

Motivated by the growing use of large language models like ChatGPT in student essay writing, this study investigates how students interact with these tools and how such interactions affect their sense of ownership over their work. Through a planned user study involving recording writing processes and allowing participants to use ChatGPT as an AI assistant, the research aims to uncover patterns in query behavior and response utilization. Preliminary findings suggest that students often rely on LLMs for drafting and editing, but their perceived ownership remains high when they actively revise and personalize outputs, indicating that tool use does not necessarily diminish authorship responsibility.
