An Empirical Study to Understand How Students Use ChatGPT for Writing Essays
Authors: Andrew Jelson, Daniel Manesh, Alice Jang, Daniel Dunlap, Young-Ho Kim, Sang Won Lee
Venue: CHI '26: Proceedings of the 2026 CHI Conference on Human Factors in Computing Systems
First: 2025-01-17T20:54:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:49:10+00:00
Comments: 35 pages, 16 figures, 6 tables, Submitted to ACM CHI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As large language models (LLMs) advance and become widespread, students increasingly turn to systems like ChatGPT for assistance with writing tasks. Educators are concerned with students' usage of ChatGPT beyond cheating; using ChatGPT may reduce their critical engagement with writing, hindering students' learning processes. The negative or positive impact of using LLM-powered tools for writing will depend on how students use them; however, how students use ChatGPT remains largely unknown, resulting in a limited understanding of its impact on learning. To better understand how students use these tools, we conducted an online study $(n=70)$ where students were given an essay-writing task using a custom platform we developed to capture the queries they made to ChatGPT. To characterize their ChatGPT usage, we categorized each of the queries students made to ChatGPT. We then analyzed the relationship between ChatGPT usage and a variety of other metrics, including students' self-perception, attitudes towards AI, and the resulting essay itself. We found that factors such as gender, race, and perceived self-efficacy can help predict different AI usage patterns. Additionally, we found that different usage patterns were associated with varying levels of enjoyment and perceived ownership over the essay. The results of this study contribute to discussions about how writing education should incorporate generative AI-powered tools in the classroom.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一项实证研究，旨在了解学生如何使用ChatGPT撰写论文
随着大语言模型（LLM）的发展和普及，学生越来越多地转向如ChatGPT等系统以协助写作任务。教育者担忧学生使用ChatGPT不仅限于作弊行为，使用ChatGPT可能降低其对写作的批判性参与，从而阻碍学生的学习过程。使用LLM工具进行写作的积极或消极影响取决于学生如何使用它们；然而，学生如何使用ChatGPT仍不甚明了，导致对其学习影响的理解有限。为更好地理解学生使用这些工具的方式，我们开展了一项在线研究（n=70），让学生在我们开发的定制平台上完成论文写作任务，以记录他们向ChatGPT发出的查询。为描述其ChatGPT使用情况，我们对每位学生向ChatGPT发出的查询进行了分类。随后，我们分析了ChatGPT使用情况与其他多种指标（包括学生的自我感知、对AI的态度以及最终论文本身）之间的关系。我们发现，性别、种族和自我效能感等变量有助于预测不同的AI使用模式。此外，我们发现不同的使用模式与论文写作的愉悦感和学生对论文的归属感水平存在差异。本研究结果有助于探讨写作教育应如何在课堂中融入生成式AI工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by concerns that students may use ChatGPT in ways that undermine critical writing skills, this study investigated how students interact with the tool during essay writing. Using a custom platform with 70 participants, researchers captured and categorized students’ queries to ChatGPT, then correlated usage patterns with self-reported attitudes, self-efficacy, and essay quality. Findings revealed that demographic factors like gender and race, along with perceived self-efficacy, predicted distinct usage behaviors. Different usage patterns were also linked to varying levels of student enjoyment and sense of ownership over their essays, suggesting that AI tool integration in education must consider usage context to support learning outcomes.
鉴于教育者担忧学生使用ChatGPT可能削弱批判性写作能力，本研究通过定制平台对70名学生在论文写作中的ChatGPT使用行为进行实证分析。研究人员对学生的提问内容进行分类，并将其与自我效能感、对AI的态度及最终论文质量等指标关联分析。结果发现，性别、种族和自我效能感可预测不同的使用模式，且不同使用方式与学生的写作愉悦感和对论文的归属感显著相关，表明学生如何使用AI工具直接影响其学习成效。
Learning Stable In-Grasp Manipulation in a Non-Dropping Action Space
Authors: Ha Thang Long Doan, Hikaru Arita, Kazuto Nakashima, Kenji Tahara
First: 2026-06-26T15:43:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:43:55+00:00
Comments: This work has been submitted to the IEEE for possible publication
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Traditionally, dexterous manipulation controllers are designed using analytic models constrained by strong assumptions about the hand and the objects being manipulated. Reinforcement learning (RL) has become another common approach in which skills are explored openly in an end-to-end manner but is inefficient because of unnoticeable instability and conflicts in learning objectives. This paper attempts to efficiently explore stable and accurate manipulation skills by decomposing dexterous skills into multiple simpler/analyzable components. Each skill component is subsequently learned with constraints and guidance from classical physics and control theory. Our work shows that for stable grasp, in-grasp reposition/reorientation with different objects, sensor/motor noise, latency, and frictional conditions, skill learning becomes efficient and stable with prior knowledge from theory.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在非掉落动作空间中学习稳定握持操作
传统上，灵巧操作控制器基于对手部和被操作物体的强假设的解析模型设计。强化学习（RL）是另一种常用方法，通过端到端方式开放探索技能，但效率低下，因学习目标中存在不易察觉的不稳定性与冲突。本文通过将灵巧技能分解为多个更简单/可分析的组件，以高效探索稳定且精确的操作技能。每个技能组件随后在经典物理与控制理论的约束与指导下进行学习。我们的工作表明，对于稳定握持、不同物体的握持内重新定位/重定向、传感器/电机噪声、延迟及摩擦条件，借助理论先验知识，技能学习可变得高效且稳定。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the inefficiency and instability of reinforcement learning in dexterous manipulation by proposing a method that decomposes complex manipulation skills into simpler, analyzable components, each guided by classical physics and control theory constraints. The motivation stems from the limitations of traditional analytic models and the instability of end-to-end RL approaches. Experimental results demonstrate that incorporating theoretical priors enables more stable and efficient learning of in-grasp repositioning and reorientation across diverse objects, sensor noise, latency, and frictional conditions, leading to robust manipulation performance without requiring extensive trial-and-error.
该论文针对强化学习在灵巧操作中效率低、不稳定的问题，提出将复杂操作技能分解为多个可分析的子技能，并通过经典物理与控制理论约束引导学习。该方法在不同物体、传感器噪声、延迟和摩擦条件下，实现了稳定且高效的抓握内重定位与重定向。实验表明，引入理论先验知识显著提升了学习效率与稳定性，优于纯数据驱动的强化学习方法。
Supervised Quadratic Feature Analysis: Information Geometry Approach for Dimensionality Reduction
Authors: Daniel Herrera-Esposito, Johannes Burge
Venue: Transactions on Machine Learning Research (2026)
First: 2025-01-31T21:17:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:42:51+00:00
Comments: 32 pages, 11 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Supervised dimensionality reduction maps labeled data into a low-dimensional feature space while preserving class separation. A common strategy is to learn features that maximize a measure of statistical dissimilarity between the class-conditional probability distributions. Information geometry, which is rooted in Riemannian geometry, provides an alternative framework for measuring class dissimilarity. It treats probability distributions as points in a statistical manifold and uses the Fisher information metric to define a geodesic distance--the Fisher-Rao distance--between distributions The Fisher-Rao distance is an appealing candidate for measuring class separation because the Fisher information metric is a local measure of discriminability, and because it allows a geometric interpretation. Here, we present Supervised Quadratic Feature Analysis (SQFA), a supervised dimensionality reduction method which learns linear features that maximize Fisher-Rao distances between class-conditional distributions, under Gaussian assumptions. In multiple real world datasets, we find that SQFA features support classification accuracy that is competitive with features that maximize more popular measures of dissimilarity, or that are learned by other state-of-the-art dimensionality reduction methods. Notably, the best classification accuracy is achieved by SQFA-H features, a variant of SQFA that maximizes the Hellinger distance, a rarely used objective for dimensionality reduction. These results demonstrate the potential of information geometry as a tool for supervised dimensionality reduction. We provide a Python implementation of SQFA at https://github.com/dherrera1911/sqfa.
中文标题/摘要
标题：监督二次特征分析：基于信息几何的降维方法
监督降维将带标签的数据映射到低维特征空间，同时保持类别分离。一种常见策略是学习最大化类条件概率分布统计差异度量的特征。信息几何基于黎曼几何，提供了一种测量类别差异的替代框架。它将概率分布视为统计流形中的点，并使用费雪信息度量定义分布间的测地距离——费雪-劳距离。费雪-劳距离是衡量类别分离的有吸引力候选，因为费雪信息度量是局部可区分性度量，且允许几何解释。本文提出监督二次特征分析（SQFA），一种在高斯假设下学习最大化类条件分布费雪-劳距离的线性特征的监督降维方法。在多个真实数据集上，我们发现SQFA特征支持的分类准确率与最大化更流行差异度量或由其他先进降维方法学习的特征相当。值得注意的是，SQFA-H特征（最大化赫林格距离的SQFA变体）实现了最佳分类准确率，而赫林格距离在降维中鲜少被用作目标函数。这些结果展示了信息几何作为监督降维工具的潜力。我们提供SQFA的Python实现，详见https://github.com/dherrera1911/sqfa。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for supervised dimensionality reduction that preserves class separation, this paper introduces Supervised Quadratic Feature Analysis (SQFA), a method that learns linear features maximizing Fisher-Rao distances between class-conditional distributions under Gaussian assumptions. Leveraging information geometry, SQFA treats probability distributions as points on a statistical manifold and uses the Fisher information metric to define a geodesic distance that reflects local discriminability. Experimental results across multiple real-world datasets show that SQFA achieves classification accuracy competitive with or superior to other popular dimensionality reduction methods, with SQFA-H—maximizing the Hellinger distance—consistently yielding the best performance. These findings highlight the effectiveness of information geometry for supervised feature learning.
为解决监督降维中保持类别分离的需求，本文提出监督二次特征分析（SQFA）方法，该方法在高斯假设下通过最大化类条件分布间的Fisher-Rao距离来学习线性特征。该方法基于信息几何，将概率分布视为统计流形上的点，并利用Fisher信息度量定义几何距离。在多个真实数据集上的实验表明，SQFA的分类精度可与主流降维方法相媲美，其中最大化Hellinger距离的SQFA-H变体表现最优。结果表明，信息几何在监督特征学习中具有重要潜力。
Calibrating Biophysical Models for Grape Phenology Prediction via Multi-Task Learning
Authors: William Solow, Sandhya Saisubramanian
First: 2025-08-05T20:36:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:42:33+00:00
Comments: This work has been superseded by "A Hybrid Modeling Framework for Crop Prediction Tasks via Dynamic Parameter Calibration and Multi-Task Learning" arXiv:2603.15411 with an updated author list
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate prediction of grape phenology is essential for timely vineyard management decisions, such as scheduling irrigation and fertilization, to maximize crop yield and quality. While traditional biophysical models calibrated on historical field data can be used for season-long predictions, they lack the precision required for fine-grained vineyard management. Deep learning methods are a compelling alternative but their performance is hindered by sparse phenology datasets, particularly at the cultivar level. We propose a hybrid modeling approach that combines multi-task learning with a recurrent neural network to parameterize a differentiable biophysical model. By using multi-task learning to predict the parameters of the biophysical model, our approach enables shared learning across cultivars while preserving biological structure, thereby improving the robustness and accuracy of predictions. Empirical evaluation using real-world and synthetic datasets demonstrates that our method significantly outperforms both conventional biophysical models and baseline deep learning approaches in predicting phenological stages, as well as other crop state variables such as cold-hardiness and wheat yield.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过多任务学习校准葡萄物候模型
准确预测葡萄物候对及时制定葡萄园管理决策（如灌溉和施肥计划）至关重要，以最大化产量和品质。虽然传统基于历史田间数据校准的生物物理模型可用于季节性预测，但其精度不足以支持精细的葡萄园管理。深度学习方法是有力的替代方案，但其性能受限于物候数据的稀疏性，尤其是在品种层面。我们提出一种混合建模方法，结合多任务学习与循环神经网络，以参数化可微分的生物物理模型。通过利用多任务学习预测生物物理模型的参数，我们的方法实现了品种间的共享学习，同时保留了生物结构，从而提升了预测的鲁棒性和准确性。在真实和合成数据集上的实证评估表明，我们的方法在预测物候阶段及其他作物状态变量（如抗寒性和小麦产量）方面，显著优于传统生物物理模型和基线深度学习方法。
Summary / 总结
To improve the precision of grape phenology prediction for fine-grained vineyard management, this study combines multi-task learning with a recurrent neural network to parameterize a differentiable biophysical model, enabling shared learning across cultivars while preserving biological structure. The method addresses the limitation of sparse phenology datasets by learning model parameters jointly with phenological stage predictions, thereby enhancing robustness. Experimental results on real-world and synthetic datasets show that this hybrid approach significantly outperforms both traditional biophysical models and baseline deep learning methods in predicting phenological stages and related crop state variables such as cold-hardiness and wheat yield.
为提升葡萄物候期预测精度以支持精细化园艺管理，该研究提出一种结合多任务学习与循环神经网络的混合建模方法，通过参数化可微分的生物物理模型，实现跨品种参数共享的同时保留生物结构。该方法通过联合学习模型参数与物候期预测，缓解了物候数据稀疏性问题，提升了预测鲁棒性。实验证明，该方法在真实与合成数据集上显著优于传统生物物理模型和基线深度学习方法，对物候期及其他作物状态变量（如抗寒性与小麦产量）的预测精度均有明显提升。
Can LLM-based Financial Investing Strategies Outperform the Market in Long Run?
Authors: Weixian Waylon Li, Hyeonjun Kim, Mihai Cucuringu, Tiejun Ma
Venue: KDD 2026 Oral
First: 2025-05-11T18:02:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:42:23+00:00
Comments: KDD 2026, Datasets & Benchmarks Track (Oral) Corrected the FinAgent results and added FinAgent (GPT-4o-mini) in Table 2; conclusions unchanged
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently been leveraged for asset pricing tasks and stock trading applications, enabling AI agents to generate investment decisions from unstructured financial data. However, most evaluations of LLM timing-based investing strategies are conducted on narrow timeframes and limited stock universes, overstating effectiveness due to survivorship and data-snooping biases. We critically assess their generalizability and robustness by proposing FINSABER, a backtesting framework evaluating timing-based strategies across longer periods and a larger universe of symbols. Systematic backtests over two decades and 100+ symbols reveal that previously reported LLM advantages deteriorate significantly under broader cross-section and over a longer-term evaluation. Our market regime analysis further demonstrates that LLM strategies are overly conservative in bull markets, underperforming passive benchmarks, and overly aggressive in bear markets, incurring heavy losses. These findings highlight the need to develop LLM strategies that are able to prioritise trend detection and regime-aware risk controls over mere scaling of framework complexity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于大语言模型的金融投资策略能否在长期内超越市场？
大语言模型（LLMs）近期已被用于资产定价和股票交易应用，使AI代理能够从非结构化金融数据中生成投资决策。然而，大多数针对基于时序的LLM投资策略的评估仅在狭窄的时间段和有限的股票池上进行，因幸存者偏差和数据挖掘偏差而高估了其有效性。我们通过提出FINSABER回测框架，评估跨更长周期和更大股票池的时序策略，以批判性地检验其泛化能力和稳健性。对过去二十年及100多只股票的系统性回测表明，此前报告的LLM优势在更广泛的横截面和更长期评估下显著减弱。我们的市场周期分析进一步表明，LLM策略在牛市中过于保守，表现劣于被动基准，在熊市中则过于激进，导致巨额亏损。这些发现强调，需要开发能够优先关注趋势识别和周期感知风险控制、而非单纯增加框架复杂度的LLM策略。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate the long-term viability of LLM-driven financial strategies beyond short-term, biased benchmarks, the authors introduce FINSABER, a backtesting framework designed to assess timing-based investing across extended periods and a broad stock universe. Through systematic tests spanning two decades and over 100 securities, they find that previously reported LLM advantages vanish under realistic conditions, with strategies underperforming passive benchmarks in bull markets and suffering heavy losses in bear markets. The analysis reveals that LLMs tend to be overly conservative in uptrends and overly aggressive in downturns, underscoring the necessity for regime-aware risk controls and trend detection over mere model complexity scaling.
鉴于现有研究常在短期和有限标的上高估LLM金融策略的效果，本文提出FINSABER回测框架，用于在更长周期和更大标的池中评估时序型策略。跨二十年、覆盖百种以上标的的系统回测显示，LLM策略在更广泛和长期评估下表现显著弱化，相较被动基准收益下降。进一步市场周期分析表明，LLM策略在牛市中过于保守、跑输基准，在熊市中又过于激进、造成重大亏损，提示未来模型需优先强化趋势识别与周期敏感的风险控制，而非单纯堆叠复杂度。
Adaptive Momentum and Nonlinear Damping for Neural Network Training
Authors: Aikaterini Karoni, Rajit Rajpal, Benedict Leimkuhler, Gabriel Stoltz
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-01-30T21:35:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:42:22+00:00
Comments: 31 pages, 13 figures. Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Momentum Stochastic Gradient Descent (mSGD) relies on a fixed momentum coefficient shared across all parameters, failing to account for the heterogeneous structure of modern loss landscapes. In this work, we adopt a continuous-time formulation to introduce individual, adaptive momentum coefficients regulated by the kinetic energy of each model parameter. This mechanism automatically adjusts to evolving training dynamics to maintain stability without sacrificing convergence speed. We demonstrate that this adaptive friction is inextricably linked to cubic damping, a suppression mechanism from structural dynamics. We additionally introduce two optimization schemes by augmenting the continuous dynamics of mSGD and Adam with a cubic damping term. Empirically, our methods demonstrate robustness and match or outperform Adam on training ViT, BERT, and GPT2 tasks where mSGD typically struggles. We further provide theoretical results establishing the exponential convergence of the proposed schemes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：神经网络训练中的自适应动量与非线性阻尼
动量随机梯度下降（mSGD）依赖于所有参数共享的固定动量系数，无法适应现代损失景观的异质结构。本文采用连续时间形式，为每个模型参数引入独立、自适应的动量系数，由各参数的动能调节。该机制能自动适应训练动态变化，维持稳定性而不牺牲收敛速度。我们证明，这种自适应摩擦与立方阻尼（结构动力学中的抑制机制）密切相关。此外，我们通过在mSGD和Adam的连续动力学中加入立方阻尼项，提出两种优化方案。实验证明，我们的方法在ViT、BERT和GPT2任务上表现稳健，且在mSGD通常表现不佳的任务中可与Adam匹敌甚至超越。我们进一步提供了理论结果，证明所提方案具有指数收敛性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of fixed momentum in mSGD that ignore parameter-specific dynamics in modern neural networks, this work introduces adaptive momentum coefficients derived from each parameter's kinetic energy in a continuous-time framework. These coefficients dynamically adjust to training progress, enhancing stability without slowing convergence. The method also integrates cubic damping—a structural dynamics concept—to improve optimization robustness. Experiments show the proposed schemes match or exceed Adam’s performance on ViT, BERT, and GPT2 tasks where mSGD typically underperforms, while theoretical analysis confirms exponential convergence.
针对mSGD固定动量无法适应现代损失景观异构结构的问题，本文提出基于连续时间动力学的自适应动量机制，通过各参数动能动态调节动量系数以维持稳定性与收敛速度。引入立方阻尼机制（源于结构动力学的抑制机制），作者构建了两种优化方案，分别增强mSGD与Adam。实验表明，该方法在ViT、BERT、GPT2任务上表现稳健，匹配或超越Adam，尤其在mSGD表现不佳的任务中效果显著，理论分析亦证明其具有指数收敛性。
COCOLogic-V2: Identifying Logical Inconsistencies via Truly Hard-Negatives
Authors: David Steinmann, Antonia Wüst, Kristian Kersting, Wolfgang Stammer
First: 2026-06-26T15:40:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:40:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While interpretable models such as concept bottleneck models (CBMs) and program synthesis methods enable verification of model decisions, their evaluation is typically limited to simple tasks, leaving complex reasoning on real-world images largely unexplored. We introduce COCOLogic-V2, an object-centric dataset for visual inductive reasoning on real-world images covering a broad subset of first-order logic. By categorizing samples into positive variants, near-boundary (NB), and far-from-boundary (FB) negatives, COCOLogic-V2 enables fine-grained diagnosis of model accountability. Our evaluations show that models tend to separate positive and FB samples well but fail on NB samples, while perceptual noise and large rule-induced search spaces pose additional challenges in few-shot settings. Together, these results highlight that visual inductive reasoning remains an open challenge and COCOLogic-V2 provides a concrete foundation for advancing methods in this direction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：COCOLogic-V2：通过真正困难的负样本识别逻辑不一致
尽管可解释模型（如概念瓶颈模型和程序合成方法）能够验证模型决策，但其评估通常局限于简单任务，复杂现实图像上的推理仍鲜有探索。我们引入了COCOLogic-V2，这是一个面向真实图像的以对象为中心的视觉归纳推理数据集，覆盖了大量一阶逻辑。通过将样本分类为正样本、近边界（NB）和远离边界（FB）负样本，COCOLogic-V2支持对模型问责性的细粒度诊断。评估表明，模型在区分正样本与FB样本方面表现良好，但在NB样本上表现不佳，而感知噪声和大规模规则诱导的搜索空间在少样本设置中进一步增加了挑战。这些结果共同表明，视觉归纳推理仍是一个开放性挑战，而COCOLogic-V2为此方向方法的发展提供了具体基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate model reasoning on complex real-world visual tasks beyond simple benchmarks, COCOLogic-V2 introduces an object-centric dataset designed for visual inductive reasoning using first-order logic. The dataset categorizes samples into positive, near-boundary, and far-from-boundary negatives to enable fine-grained analysis of model accountability. Experimental results reveal that models perform well on distinguishing positive from far-from-boundary negatives but struggle significantly with near-boundary samples, indicating that perceptual noise and large rule-based search spaces hinder performance, especially in few-shot settings, underscoring the ongoing difficulty of visual inductive reasoning.
为评估模型在复杂真实视觉任务中的推理能力，COCOLogic-V2提出一个面向视觉归纳推理的物体中心化数据集，支持一阶逻辑推理。该方法将样本划分为正例、近边界和远边界负例，以实现模型责任的细粒度诊断。实验表明，模型能较好区分正例与远边界负例，但在近边界负例上表现不佳，说明感知噪声和大规模规则搜索空间在少样本场景下显著影响性能。
PA-BiCoop: A Primary-Auxiliary Cooperative Framework for General Bimanual Manipulation
Authors: Bai Qicheng, Wang Ziru, Ma Teli, Dai Guang, Wang Jingdong, Wang Mengmeng
First: 2026-06-26T15:38:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:38:00+00:00
Comments: ICRA2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bimanual manipulation is essential for advanced robotic systems because it offers higher efficiency and flexibility compared to single-arm configurations. However, existing approaches either lack inter-arm interaction or ignore the need for a dynamic division of labor, treating the arms as functionally equivalent. To address these limitations, this paper draws inspiration from human bimanual manipulation where one arm handles core operations and the other provides auxiliary support, and proposes PA-BiCoop, a new single-model bimanual cooperation framework with dynamic primary-auxiliary arm differentiation. PA-BiCoop categorizes robotic arms into primary and auxiliary arms with adaptively adjustable roles across task stages, employs two specialized decoders that share a global feature encoder: the primary decoder generates the primary arm's base-coordinate pose and core-task affordance heatmaps, and the auxiliary decoder outputs the auxiliary arm's relative pose in the primary arm's coordinate system. Moreover, we design a dynamic role assignment module to automatically map roles to left/right arms without manual pre-definition. This design facilitates inter-arm knowledge sharing and coordinated manipulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our PA-BiCoop achieves superior performance: it outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by 48% on average in RLBench2 simulation tasks and by over 50% on average in real world tasks, thereby verifying its effectiveness and advancement in bimanual manipulation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PA-BiCoop：一种面向通用双臂操作的主从协作框架
双臂操作对高级机器人系统至关重要，因其相比单臂配置具有更高的效率和灵活性。然而，现有方法要么缺乏双臂交互，要么忽略动态分工需求，将双臂视为功能等价。为解决这些问题，本文借鉴人类双臂操作中一臂执行核心操作、另一臂提供辅助支持的模式，提出PA-BiCoop——一种新型单模型双臂协作框架，支持动态主从臂角色划分。PA-BiCoop将机器人双臂划分为主臂与从臂，其角色在任务阶段可自适应调整；采用两个专用解码器共享全局特征编码器：主解码器生成主臂基坐标位姿与核心任务可操作性热图，从解码器输出从臂相对于主臂坐标系的相对位姿。此外，设计动态角色分配模块，可自动将角色映射至左/右臂，无需人工预定义。该设计促进双臂间知识共享与协同操作。大量实验表明，PA-BiCoop在RLBench2仿真任务中平均优于现有最先进方法48%，在真实世界任务中平均提升超50%，验证其在双臂操作中的有效性与先进性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more efficient and flexible robotic bimanual manipulation, this paper introduces PA-BiCoop, a single-model framework that dynamically assigns primary and auxiliary roles to robotic arms based on task requirements, inspired by human bimanual coordination. The method employs a shared global encoder with two specialized decoders: one for generating the primary arm’s pose and core-task affordances, and another for computing the auxiliary arm’s pose relative to the primary. A dynamic role assignment module automatically maps roles to left/right arms without manual configuration. Experimental results show PA-BiCoop outperforms existing methods by 48% on average in simulation and over 50% in real-world tasks, confirming its effectiveness in coordinated bimanual manipulation.
为解决现有双臂机器人系统缺乏臂间协作或忽视动态分工的问题，本文提出PA-BiCoop框架，通过动态分配主从角色提升双臂协同效率。该方法采用共享全局编码器与两个专用解码器：主解码器生成主臂位姿与任务热图，辅解码器输出辅臂相对于主臂的位姿。动态角色分配模块自动为左右臂分配主从角色，无需人工设定。实验表明，该框架在模拟环境中平均优于现有方法48%，在真实任务中提升超50%，验证了其在双臂操作中的优越性。
The Remittance Blueprint: Data-driven Intelligence for Sri Lanka
Authors: Dhinanjaya Fernando, Dinura Ginige, Kalana Lakshan, Chanupa Gurusinghe, Lasana Pahanga, Subavarshana Arumugam, Sandeepa Weerasekara, Sandareka Wickramanayake, Nisansa de Silva
First: 2026-06-26T15:35:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:35:46+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This study analyzes Sri Lankan migration and remittances over 32 years (1994-2025). Using a 384-month harmonized dataset, we apply exploratory data analysis, stationarity corrected time-series modeling (ADF, Johansen, VAR/VECM), and supervised learning. Results reveal remittance inflows are primarily driven by external macroeconomic variables, specifically exchange rate dynamics and global oil prices, rather than domestic indicators. Impulse response analysis confirms the asymmetric impact of currency depreciation and oil price shocks. Predictively, multivariate machine learning models outperform traditional univariate approaches; Ridge Regression achieves a 73.8% accuracy improvement over SARIMA (Annualized RMSE: USD 494.8 Mn). The optimized framework projects 2026 remittances at USD 9,001 million under stable conditions. These findings highlight the structural dependence of remittances on global economies, emphasizing the need for robust exchange rate policies, skilled migration, and formal financial channels to enhance long-term economic resilience.
中文标题/摘要
标题：汇款蓝图：斯里兰卡的数据驱动智能分析
本研究分析了斯里兰卡1994年至2025年32年的移民与汇款数据。利用384个月的标准化数据集，应用探索性数据分析、修正平稳性的时间序列建模（ADF、Johansen、VAR/VECM）及监督学习方法。结果表明，汇款流入主要受外部宏观经济变量驱动，特别是汇率动态和全球油价，而非国内指标。脉冲响应分析证实了货币贬值和油价冲击的非对称影响。预测方面，多元机器学习模型优于传统单变量方法；岭回归模型相较SARIMA模型准确率提升73.8%（年化RMSE：4.948亿美元）。优化框架在稳定条件下预测2026年汇款额为90.01亿美元。研究结果凸显汇款对全球经济的结构性依赖，强调需制定稳健汇率政策、推动技能型移民及完善正式金融渠道，以增强长期经济韧性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand Sri Lanka’s remittance dynamics over three decades, this study employs a 384-month harmonized dataset and combines exploratory data analysis with stationarity-corrected time-series models and supervised machine learning. Results show remittance inflows are primarily influenced by external macroeconomic factors—especially exchange rate fluctuations and global oil prices—rather than domestic variables. Impulse response analysis reveals asymmetric effects of currency depreciation and oil shocks. Machine learning models, particularly Ridge Regression, significantly outperform traditional univariate models like SARIMA, achieving a 73.8% accuracy gain with an annualized RMSE of USD 494.8 million. The model projects 2026 remittances at USD 9.001 billion under stable conditions, underscoring the importance of global economic linkages and policy alignment for resilience.
为理解斯里兰卡过去三十年汇款流动的驱动因素，本研究采用384个月的标准化数据集，结合探索性数据分析、平稳性修正的时间序列模型与监督机器学习方法。结果表明，汇款流入主要受外部宏观经济变量影响，尤其是汇率变动和全球油价，而非国内指标。脉冲响应分析显示汇率贬值与油价冲击的影响具有非对称性。机器学习模型（特别是岭回归）显著优于传统单变量模型（如SARIMA），准确率提升73.8%，并预测在稳定条件下2026年汇款额将达到90.01亿美元，凸显全球经济联动性及政策协调对经济韧性的重要性。
Cognitive Episodes in LLM Reasoning Traces Enable Interpretable Human Item Difficulty Prediction
Authors: Chenguang Wang, Ming Li, Xinyue Zeng, Zhuochun Li, Hong Jiao, Tianyi Zhou, Dawei Zhou
First: 2026-06-26T15:32:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:32:17+00:00
Comments: 32 pages, 8 figures, 10 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Predicting human item difficulty is central to educational assessment, where reliable estimates support fairness and effective test construction. Existing methods often depend on costly human calibration or item-level textual representations, providing limited evidence about the cognitive processes that make items difficult. We argue that difficulty should be viewed not only as a property of item text, but also as an observable consequence of the problem-solving burden an item induces. Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) offer scalable process evidence through reasoning traces, but such evidence must be structured to support interpretable modeling. To this end, we introduce Epi2Diff (Episode to Difficulty), a framework that maps LRM reasoning traces into cognitively grounded episode sequences. These episodes group trace segments into functional problem-solving states, enabling difficulty to be modeled through reasoning scale, effort allocation, and state transitions. Epi2Diff extracts compact episode-dynamic features and combines them with semantic item representations for human difficulty prediction. Experiments on four real-world human difficulty datasets show that Epi2Diff consistently outperforms strong baselines, including fine-tuned small language models, LLM in-context learning, and supervised LLM adaptation. On SAT-derived classification benchmarks, Epi2Diff achieves an 8.1% average relative gain over supervised LLM fine-tuning baselines. Further analyses show that harder items induce more effortful, iterative, and implementation-centered episode dynamics, rather than merely longer responses. These results demonstrate that cognitive episodes in LRM reasoning traces provide a predictive and interpretable process representation for human item difficulty, offering a new lens for educational measurement with reasoning models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型推理轨迹中的认知事件可解释地预测人类项目难度
预测人类项目难度是教育评估的核心，可靠的估计支持公平性和有效的测验构建。现有方法常依赖昂贵的人类校准或项目级文本表示，难以揭示使项目困难的认知过程。我们认为，难度不仅应视为项目文本的属性，更应视为项目所引发的问题解决负担的可观测结果。大型推理模型（LRMs）可通过推理轨迹提供可扩展的过程证据，但此类证据需结构化以支持可解释建模。为此，我们提出Epi2Diff（事件到难度）框架，将LRM推理轨迹映射为具有认知基础的事件序列。这些事件将轨迹片段分组为功能性的问题解决状态，从而通过推理尺度、努力分配和状态转换建模难度。Epi2Diff提取紧凑的事件动态特征，并与语义项目表示结合，用于预测人类难度。在四个真实世界人类难度数据集上的实验表明，Epi2Diff持续优于强基线方法，包括微调的小语言模型、LLM上下文学习和监督LLM适配。在SAT衍生分类基准上，Epi2Diff相较监督LLM微调基线平均相对提升8.1%。进一步分析表明，较难项目引发更费力、迭代性更强且以实现为中心的事件动态，而非仅更长的响应。这些结果表明，LRM推理轨迹中的认知事件为人类项目难度提供了可预测且可解释的过程表征，为教育测量提供了新的视角。
Summary / 总结
To improve the prediction of human item difficulty in educational assessment, this work proposes Epi2Diff, a framework that transforms reasoning traces from large language models into cognitively grounded episode sequences, capturing functional problem-solving states. These episodes enable modeling difficulty through reasoning scale, effort allocation, and state transitions, and are combined with semantic item representations for prediction. Evaluated on four real-world datasets, Epi2Diff outperforms strong baselines including fine-tuned small models, in-context LLM learning, and supervised LLM adaptation, achieving an 8.1% average relative gain over supervised fine-tuning on SAT-derived benchmarks. Analysis further reveals that harder items trigger more effortful, iterative, and implementation-focused episode dynamics, not just longer responses.
为提升教育评估中人类题目难度的预测能力，本文提出Epi2Diff框架，将大语言模型的推理轨迹转化为认知基础的“认知片段”序列，捕捉功能性的解题状态。通过推理规模、努力分配与状态转换建模难度，并结合语义题目表示进行预测。在四个真实数据集上的实验表明，Epi2Diff显著优于包括微调小模型、上下文学习及监督适配在内的强基线方法，在SAT衍生基准上平均相对提升8.1%。进一步分析显示，难度更高的题目引发更费力、迭代性更强且聚焦实现的片段动态，而非单纯更长的回答。
Multiperiodic Processes: Ergodic Sources with a Sublinear Entropy
Authors: Łukasz Dębowski
First: 2023-02-17T18:27:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:30:45+00:00
Comments: 28 pages; 1 figure
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Several explicit stochastic processes are known to satisfy Hilberg's law, a power-law growth of block entropy conjectured for natural language and recently connected to the neural scaling law. Existing examples either possess a positive Shannon entropy rate, are non-ergodic, or require comparatively involved constructions. We introduce multiperiodic processes, a new class of stationary ergodic processes over the natural numbers generated by random shifts of deterministic multiperiodic sequences. Under mild conditions, multiperiodic processes have vanishing Shannon entropy rate and, under a suitable parameterization, they satisfy both Zipf's law for symbol frequencies and Hilberg's law for block entropy. Since multiperiodic processes are not mixing, we identify the open problem of constructing an elementary strongly mixing source with vanishing entropy rate and Hilberg's law.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多周期过程：具有次线性熵的遍历源
已知若干显式随机过程满足希尔伯格定律，即块熵的幂律增长，该定律曾被推测适用于自然语言，并最近与神经缩放定律相关联。现有例子要么具有正的香农熵率，要么是非遍历的，要么需要相对复杂的构造。我们引入多周期过程，这是一种新的自然数上静态遍历过程，由确定性多周期序列的随机平移生成。在温和条件下，多周期过程具有零香农熵率，并在适当参数化下同时满足符号频率的齐夫定律和块熵的希尔伯格定律。由于多周期过程不是混合的，我们指出构造一个具有零熵率和希尔伯格定律的简单强混合源仍是开放性问题。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for simple, stationary, ergodic processes that satisfy Hilberg's law—a power-law growth of block entropy observed in natural language and linked to neural scaling laws—this paper introduces multiperiodic processes. These are generated by random shifts of deterministic multiperiodic sequences, ensuring stationarity and ergodicity while avoiding positive Shannon entropy rate. Under mild conditions, they exhibit vanishing entropy rate and simultaneously satisfy Zipf’s law for symbol frequencies and Hilberg’s law for block entropy. The paper notes that since these processes are not mixing, constructing an elementary strongly mixing source with vanishing entropy rate and Hilberg’s law remains an open problem.
为寻找满足希尔伯格定律（即块熵的幂律增长，该定律在自然语言和神经缩放定律中被观察到）的简单、平稳、遍历过程，本文提出了多周期过程。这类过程由确定性多周期序列的随机平移生成，在温和条件下具有消失的香农熵率；在适当参数化下，同时满足齐普夫定律（符号频率分布）和希尔伯格定律（块熵增长）。由于多周期过程不具备强混合性，本文指出一个开放性问题：构造一个具有消失熵率且满足希尔伯格定律的简单强混合源。
Helpfulness Hurts: Domain-Dependent Degradation of Mid-Trained Compassion Values Under Post-Training
Authors: Jasmine Brazilek, Juliana Seawell
First: 2026-04-30T17:55:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:27:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Standard post-training pipelines apply supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL) to make language models helpful, but these processes may inadvertently degrade values instilled during pre-training. We investigate whether the domain of post-training data differentially affects the retention of animal compassion values in a Llama 3.1 8B model mid-trained on compassion-oriented synthetic data, using both SFT (helpfulness via Dolly-15k vs. coding via Magicoder-110K) and GRPO (helpfulness via RLHFlow vs. coding via Magicoder), evaluated on the ANIMA 2.2 benchmark and MORU benchmark (Moral Reasoning Under Uncertainty). Helpfulness training significantly degrades animal compassion relative to coding training on ANIMA (SFT: 35.7% vs. 65.2%; GRPO: 18.7% vs. 32.0%), replicating across two independent helpfulness datasets and two training paradigms. On English MORU items, helpfulness training degrades general moral reasoning by 25.5 percentage points (46.4% vs. 71.9%), a striking gap that rivals the compassion effect in magnitude. However, this effect does not transfer cross-lingually: on the multilingual MORU benchmark, the domain effect disappears (SFT: 52.3% vs. 51.2%). In contrast, the animal compassion effect transfers consistently across languages, with Magicoder's ANIMA percentage-point gain over the base model 4.5 times larger on non-English items than English items. This divergence suggests that values instilled through mid-training are encoded more deeply and cross-lingually than reasoning improvements from domain-specific post-training. These results suggest that, for labs building on value-laden mid-training, coding-domain post-training may better preserve mid-trained values than helpfulness post-training without harming general reasoning capabilities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：助人有害：后训练数据领域对中期训练同情价值观的领域依赖性退化
标准后训练流程通过监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）使语言模型更具帮助性，但这些过程可能无意中削弱预训练期间建立的价值观。我们研究了后训练数据领域是否对Llama 3.1 8B模型在同情导向合成数据上中期训练后动物同情价值观的保留产生差异影响，使用SFT（通过Dolly-15k实现帮助性 vs. 通过Magicoder-110K实现编程）和GRPO（通过RLHFlow实现帮助性 vs. 通过Magicoder实现编程），并在ANIMA 2.2基准和MORU基准（不确定性下的道德推理）上进行评估。帮助性训练在ANIMA上显著削弱动物同情（SFT：35.7% vs. 65.2%；GRPO：18.7% vs. 32.0%），在两个独立的帮助性数据集和两种训练范式中均得到重复验证。在英文MORU项目中，帮助性训练使一般道德推理能力下降25.5个百分点（46.4% vs. 71.9%），这一差距在幅度上可与同情效应相媲美。然而，该效应不具跨语言迁移性：在多语言MORU基准上，领域效应消失（SFT：52.3% vs. 51.2%）。相比之下，动物同情效应在语言间具有一致性迁移，Magicoder在非英语项目上相比基线模型的ANIMA百分点增益是英语项目的4.5倍。这种差异表明，中期训练建立的价值观比领域特定后训练带来的推理改进更深层且更具跨语言性。这些结果表明，对于基于价值导向中期训练的实验室而言，编程领域后训练可能比帮助性后训练更能保留中期训练的价值观，同时不损害一般推理能力。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how post-training methods degrade compassion values instilled during mid-training in a Llama 3.1 8B model. Using synthetic compassion-oriented data, the researchers compared SFT and GRPO fine-tuning on helpfulness (Dolly-15k/RLHFlow) versus coding (Magicoder-110K) domains, evaluating performance on ANIMA and MORU benchmarks. Results show that helpfulness training significantly reduces animal compassion scores (35.7% vs 65.2% on ANIMA via SFT; 18.7% vs 32.0% via GRPO), while coding training preserves it better. On English MORU, helpfulness training also degraded general moral reasoning by 25.5 percentage points (46.4% vs 71.9%), but this effect vanished in multilingual MORU (52.3% vs 51.2%), indicating domain-specific degradation. Crucially, compassion values retained across languages, with non-English items showing 4.5x greater gains from coding training than English, suggesting deeper, cross-lingual encoding of mid-trained values compared to domain-specific reasoning improvements.
本研究探讨了后训练方法对在中训练阶段植入的同情价值观的影响。研究动机源于担忧以“有用性”为目标的微调可能削弱预训练阶段建立的道德价值观。研究人员对比了使用“有用性”或“编程”数据集进行监督微调（SFT）和基于策略梯度的强化学习（GRPO）的效果。结果显示，有用性训练显著降低动物同情性能（ANIMA基准：SFT为35.7% vs 65.2%，GRPO为18.7% vs 32.0%），并在英语MORU项目中使一般道德推理能力下降25.5个百分点。然而，这种退化在多语言场景中消失，而同情价值观则能稳定跨语言传递，表明中训练价值观编码更深层、更跨语言。研究结论指出，使用编程领域数据进行后训练可能更有利于保留同情价值观，同时不影响一般推理能力。
LLawCo: Learning Laws of Cooperation for Modeling Embodied Multi-Agent Behavior
Authors: Qinhong Zhou, Chuang Gan, Anoop Cherian
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-26T15:26:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:26:12+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Embodied agents operating in decentralized and partially observable environments have attracted growing attention in recent years. However, existing large language model (LLM)-based agents often exhibit behaviors that are misaligned with their partners or inconsistent with the environment state, leading to inefficient cooperation and poor task success. To address this challenge, we propose a novel framework, Learning Laws of Cooperation (LLawCo), that enables embodied agents to autonomously align with both their partners and task objectives. Our framework allows agents to reflect on past failures to extract misaligned behavioral patterns, which are used to derive high-level behavioral laws, such as "Talk when necessary" and "Wait for partner." These laws are explicitly incorporated into the agents' chains of thought via supervised fine-tuning, aligning their reasoning with task requirements and the behavior of other agents. To evaluate our approach, we introduce PARTNR-Dialog, a large-scale multi-agent communicative and cooperative planning benchmark built on the PARTNR environment. Experiments on existing tasks and our new benchmark demonstrate significant improvements in cooperative efficiency and task success rates. Across four backbone LLMs, our method achieves average success rate improvements of 4.5% on the PARTNR-Dialog benchmark and 6.8% on the TDW-MAT benchmark over state-of-the-art open-source communicative agent frameworks. See the LLawCo project page for details: https://www.merl.com/research/highlights/LLawCo
中文标题/摘要
标题：LLawCo：学习合作规律以建模具身多智能体行为
近年来，运行于去中心化和部分可观测环境中的具身智能体受到越来越多关注。然而，现有基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能体常表现出与伙伴不协调或与环境状态不一致的行为，导致合作效率低下和任务成功率低。为解决此问题，我们提出一种新颖框架——合作规律学习（LLawCo），使具身智能体能自主对齐伙伴与任务目标。该框架允许智能体反思过往失败，提取不协调行为模式，进而推导出高层行为规律，如“必要时交谈”和“等待伙伴”。这些规律通过监督微调明确融入智能体的思维链中，使其推理与任务需求及伙伴行为保持一致。为评估本方法，我们引入了基于PARTNR环境构建的大规模多智能体沟通与协作规划基准PARTNR-Dialog。在现有任务及新基准上的实验表明，合作效率和任务成功率显著提升。在四个骨干LLM上，本方法在PARTNR-Dialog基准上较现有开源沟通智能体框架平均提升4.5%，在TDW-MAT基准上提升6.8%。详情请见LLawCo项目页面：https://www.merl.com/research/highlights/LLawCo
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of LLM-based embodied agents in decentralized, partially observable environments due to misaligned or inconsistent behaviors, LLawCo introduces a framework that enables agents to autonomously derive and internalize high-level behavioral laws—such as 'Talk when necessary' and 'Wait for partner'—by reflecting on past failures. These laws are then integrated into agents' reasoning via supervised fine-tuning to align their actions with both task goals and partner behavior. Evaluated on PARTNR-Dialog and TDW-MAT benchmarks, LLawCo consistently outperforms existing open-source frameworks, achieving average success rate improvements of 4.5% and 6.8% respectively across four backbone LLMs.
为解决LLM驱动的具身智能体在分布式、部分可观测环境中因行为不协调或与环境状态不符而导致的合作效率低下问题，LLawCo提出一种框架，使智能体能通过反思过往失败提取行为偏差模式，并提炼出如“必要时交谈”“等待伙伴”等高层协作法则，再通过监督微调将其融入智能体的思维链中，使其行为与任务目标和伙伴行为对齐。在PARTNR-Dialog和TDW-MAT两个基准上，该方法相较现有开源框架平均提升成功率4.5%和6.8%，显著增强协作效率。
Non-Linear Model-Based Sequential Decision-Making in Agriculture
Authors: Sakshi Arya, Wentao Lin
First: 2025-09-02T03:41:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:25:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agricultural decision-making faces a dual challenge: sustaining high yields to meet global food security needs while reducing the environmental impacts of input use, including fertilizer losses and other agrochemical applications such as herbicides, insecticides, and fungicides. Nitrogen inputs are central to this tension. They are indispensable for crop growth yet major drivers of greenhouse gas emissions, nutrient runoff, and escalating production costs. Addressing these intertwined pressures requires adaptive decision-support tools that are statistically principled, economically sustainable and interpretable for practitioners. We develop nonlinear model-based bandit algorithms as a framework for adaptive fertilizer management under uncertainty. Building on classical mechanistic yield-response models, our approach links algorithmic exploration-exploitation strategies directly to interpretable biological processes such as maximum yield and nutrient efficiency. This grounding makes recommendations transparent for practitioners while supporting cost-effective and sustainable input use. Methodologically, we establish regret and sample complexity results for the well-specified nonlinear case, examine robustness under misspecification, and evaluate the proposed methods through profit-oriented simulations and an offline replay case study on publicly available multi-site corn nitrogen field trials from the U.S. Midwest. The results show that incorporating biologically meaningful mechanistic structure enables faster learning and higher profit as evidence accumulates, with flexible nonparametric baselines providing a competitive alternative in pooled and heterogeneous settings. Our findings illustrate how interpretable, uncertainty-aware sequential decision rules can support economically sustainable fertilizer recommendations and contribute to more efficient agricultural input use.
中文标题/摘要
标题：农业中的非线性模型驱动的序贯决策
农业决策面临双重挑战：既要维持高产以满足全球粮食安全需求，又要减少投入品（包括化肥损失及其他农用化学品如除草剂、杀虫剂和杀菌剂）的环境影响。氮肥投入是这一矛盾的核心：对作物生长不可或缺，却也是温室气体排放、营养流失和生产成本上升的主要驱动因素。应对这些交织的压力，需要统计学严谨、经济可持续且对从业者可解释的适应性决策支持工具。我们开发了基于非线性模型的多臂赌博机算法框架，用于在不确定性下实现适应性施肥管理。该方法建立在经典机制性产量响应模型基础上，将算法的探索-利用策略直接与可解释的生物过程（如最大产量和养分效率）相联系。这种基础设定使推荐对从业者透明，同时支持成本效益高且可持续的投入使用。方法上，我们为明确指定的非线性情形建立了遗憾与样本复杂度结果，考察了模型误设下的稳健性，并通过以利润为导向的模拟及基于美国中西部公开多站点玉米氮肥田间试验的离线重放案例研究评估所提方法。结果表明，融入具有生物学意义的机制性结构可加速学习并提高利润，随着证据积累，灵活的非参数基线在聚合与异质性场景中提供了有竞争力的替代方案。我们的研究展示了可解释、不确定性感知的序贯决策规则如何支持经济可持续的施肥建议，并促进更高效的农业投入使用。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to balance high agricultural yields with reduced environmental and economic costs of fertilizer use, this study develops nonlinear model-based bandit algorithms that integrate mechanistic yield-response models with adaptive decision-making under uncertainty. The approach maps exploration-exploitation strategies to interpretable biological parameters like maximum yield and nutrient efficiency, enhancing practitioner trust and enabling cost-effective input management. Evaluated through profit simulations and an offline replay of U.S. Midwest corn nitrogen trials, the methods demonstrate faster learning and higher profitability when grounded in biological structure, with flexible nonparametric baselines offering strong performance in heterogeneous settings.
为平衡高产与化肥使用带来的环境及经济成本，本研究构建了基于非线性模型的多臂老虎机算法，将作物产量响应机制模型融入化肥管理决策。该方法将探索与利用策略与最大产量、养分效率等可解释的生物学参数关联，提升农业从业者信任度并支持低成本投入决策。通过利润导向仿真与美国中西部玉米田试验数据的离线回放评估，结果表明生物机制建模能加速学习并提升收益，非参数基线方法在异质环境中表现优异。
Training-free Uncertainty Guidance for Complex Visual Tasks with MLLMs
Authors: Sanghwan Kim, Rui Xiao, Stephan Alaniz, Yongqin Xian, Zeynep Akata
First: 2025-10-01T09:20:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:22:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) often struggle with fine-grained perception, such as identifying small objects in high-resolution images or detecting key moments in long videos. Existing methods typically rely on complex, task-specific fine-tuning, which reduces generalizability and increases system complexity. In this work, we propose an effective, training-free framework that uses an MLLM's intrinsic uncertainty as proactive guidance. Our core insight is that a model's uncertainty decreases when provided with relevant visual information. We introduce a unified mechanism that scores candidate visual inputs by response uncertainty, enabling the model to autonomously focus on the most informative data. We apply this simple principle to three challenging visual tasks: Visual Search, Long Video Understanding, and Temporal Grounding, allowing off-the-shelf MLLMs to achieve performance competitive with specialized, fine-tuned systems. Our results demonstrate that leveraging intrinsic uncertainty is a powerful strategy for improving fine-grained multimodal performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于无训练不确定性引导的多模态大语言模型复杂视觉任务处理
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）常在细粒度感知任务中表现不佳，例如在高分辨率图像中识别小物体或在长视频中检测关键时刻。现有方法通常依赖复杂的、特定任务的微调，这降低了泛化能力并增加了系统复杂性。本文提出一种有效的无训练框架，利用MLLM的内在不确定性作为主动引导。我们的核心见解是：当模型获得相关视觉信息时，其不确定性会降低。我们引入一种统一机制，通过响应不确定性对候选视觉输入进行评分，使模型能够自主聚焦于最具信息量的数据。我们将这一简单原则应用于三个具有挑战性的视觉任务：视觉搜索、长视频理解与时间定位，使现成的MLLMs能够达到与专用微调系统相当的性能。实验结果表明，利用内在不确定性是提升细粒度多模态性能的强大策略。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty MLLMs face in fine-grained visual tasks like detecting small objects or key moments in long videos, this work proposes a training-free method that uses the model’s intrinsic uncertainty as guidance to improve performance. The method scores candidate visual inputs based on the model’s response uncertainty, enabling it to autonomously focus on the most informative data. Applied to Visual Search, Long Video Understanding, and Temporal Grounding, the approach allows off-the-shelf MLLMs to achieve performance comparable to task-specific fine-tuned models without additional training.
为解决多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在细粒度视觉感知任务中的不足，本文提出一种无需训练的框架，利用模型自身的不确定性作为主动引导机制。该方法根据模型对候选视觉输入的响应不确定性进行评分，使其能自主聚焦于最具信息量的数据。在视觉搜索、长视频理解与时间定位三个挑战性任务中，该方法使现成的MLLMs性能可媲美专用微调模型，表明基于不确定性的引导是提升多模态感知能力的有效且可扩展策略。
Assert, don't describe: Linguistic features that shift LLM reasoning about animal welfare
Authors: Jasmine Brazilek, Harper Dunn
First: 2026-04-30T23:59:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:22:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Animal-welfare advocates produce a lot of writing, and increasingly that writing trains the language models that millions of people then ask about animal welfare. Using vocabulary-matched stance-contrast probes on a held-out animal-welfare benchmark, we measure how each of ten linguistic features changes Llama-3.2-1B's preference for pro-animal-welfare reasoning when used as fine-tuning data. Eight of the ten features produce statistically significant shifts. Seven move the model toward stronger pro-animal-welfare reasoning: assertive certainty, explicit moral vocabulary, emotion words, evaluative claims, narrative structure, depicted harm severity, and immediate temporal framing. Two move it the other way: hedged language and concrete sensory description both dilute the pro-animal-welfare stance. First-person perspective has no statistically significant effect. The practical recommendation for anyone writing animal-welfare text that may end up in LLM training corpora: assert a position rather than describe a scene neutrally. The features that shift the model are the ones that make the writer's position explicit; the features that dilute it hold animal-welfare content but withhold stance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：断言而非描述：影响大语言模型对动物福利推理的语言特征
动物福利倡导者产生大量写作，这些写作越来越多地用于训练语言模型，而数百万用户随后会向这些模型询问动物福利问题。我们使用与词汇匹配的立场对比探针，在一个保留的动物福利基准上，测量十种语言特征中每种特征如何改变Llama-3.2-1B在作为微调数据时对动物福利支持性推理的偏好。十种特征中有八种产生统计显著的偏移。其中七种使模型更倾向于支持动物福利的推理：断言性确定性、明确的道德词汇、情感词汇、评价性陈述、叙事结构、描绘的伤害严重性以及即时时间框架。两种则削弱该立场：模糊语言和具体感官描述。第一人称视角无统计显著影响。对任何可能进入大语言模型训练语料库的动物福利文本撰写者，实用建议是：断言立场而非中立描述场景。能够改变模型立场的特征是那些明确表达作者立场的特征；而削弱立场的特征则保留动物福利内容但不表达立场。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing influence of animal-welfare writing on large language models, this study investigates how ten linguistic features affect Llama-3.2-1B's reasoning preferences when fine-tuned on such texts. Using vocabulary-matched stance-contrast probes on a held-out benchmark, it finds that eight features significantly shift the model’s stance: seven promote stronger pro-animal-welfare reasoning through assertive certainty, moral vocabulary, emotion words, evaluative claims, narrative structure, depicted harm severity, and immediate temporal framing, while hedged language and concrete sensory description weaken the stance. First-person perspective showed no significant effect. The key takeaway is that explicit assertion of position, rather than neutral description, better aligns LLMs with pro-animal-welfare views.
鉴于动物福利文本日益影响大型语言模型，本研究探讨十种语言特征如何影响Llama-3.2-1B在微调时的立场偏好。通过在独立基准上使用词汇匹配的立场对比探针，发现八种特征显著改变模型立场：七种特征（如肯定语气、道德词汇、情感词、评价性陈述、叙事结构、伤害严重性描写、即时时间框架）强化动物福利立场，两种特征（模糊语言、具象感官描写）削弱该立场，第一人称视角无显著影响。核心建议是：写作时明确主张立场，而非中性描述场景，以更有效引导模型倾向动物福利观点。
Joint Reward Modeling: Internalizing Chain-of-Thought for Efficient Visual Reward Models
Authors: Yankai Yang, Yancheng Long, Hongyang Wei, Wei Chen, Tianke Zhang, Kaiyu Jiang, Haonan Fan, Changyi Liu, Jiankang Chen, Kaiyu Tang, Bin Wen, Fan Yang, Tingting Gao, Han Li, Shuo Yang
First: 2026-02-07T13:09:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:22:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reward models are critical for reinforcement learning from human feedback, as they determine the alignment quality and reliability of generative models. For complex tasks such as image editing, reward models are required to capture global semantic consistency and implicit logical constraints beyond local similarity. Existing reward modeling approaches have clear limitations. Discriminative reward models align well with human preferences but struggle with complex semantics due to limited reasoning supervision. Generative reward models offer stronger semantic understanding and reasoning, but they are costly at inference time and difficult to align directly with human preferences. To this end, we propose Joint Reward Modeling (JRM), which jointly optimizes preference learning and language modeling on a shared vision-language backbone. This approach internalizes the semantic and reasoning capabilities of generative models into efficient discriminative representations, enabling fast and accurate evaluation. JRM achieves state-of-the-art results on MMRB2 and EditReward-Bench, and significantly improves stability and performance in downstream online reinforcement learning. These results show that joint training effectively bridges efficiency and semantic understanding in reward modeling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：联合奖励建模：内化思维链以实现高效的视觉奖励模型
奖励模型对基于人类反馈的强化学习至关重要，因其决定了生成模型的对齐质量和可靠性。对于图像编辑等复杂任务，奖励模型需捕捉全局语义一致性及超越局部相似性的隐式逻辑约束。现有奖励建模方法存在明显局限：判别式奖励模型与人类偏好对齐良好，但因推理监督有限而难以处理复杂语义；生成式奖励模型具备更强的语义理解和推理能力，但推理成本高且难以直接对齐人类偏好。为此，我们提出联合奖励建模（JRM），在共享的视觉-语言骨干网络上联合优化偏好学习与语言建模。该方法将生成模型的语义与推理能力内化为高效判别式表示，实现快速准确的评估。JRM在MMRB2和EditReward-Bench上达到最先进水平，并显著提升下游在线强化学习的稳定性和性能。结果表明，联合训练有效弥合了奖励建模中的效率与语义理解之间的鸿沟。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient yet semantically rich reward models for complex visual tasks like image editing, this paper proposes Joint Reward Modeling (JRM), which jointly trains preference learning and language modeling on a shared vision-language backbone to internalize generative reasoning into discriminative representations. By combining the semantic strength of generative models with the efficiency of discriminative models, JRM achieves state-of-the-art performance on MMRB2 and EditReward-Bench while improving stability in downstream reinforcement learning. Experimental results demonstrate that joint training effectively balances semantic understanding and computational efficiency, outperforming both discriminative and generative baselines.
为解决图像编辑等复杂视觉任务中奖励模型效率与语义理解难以兼顾的问题，本文提出联合奖励建模（JRM），在共享的视觉-语言骨干网络上联合优化偏好学习与语言建模。该方法将生成模型的推理能力内化为判别式表示，兼顾推理效率与语义一致性。实验表明，JRM在MMRB2和EditReward-Bench上达到最先进性能，并显著提升下游在线强化学习的稳定性和效果。
CPAgents: Agentic Composite Phenotype Generation for Cardiac Disease Association
Authors: Zuoou Li, Wenlong Zhao, Kelly Yu, Weitong Zhang, Paul M. Matthews, Wenjia Bai, Bernhard Kainz, Mengyun Qiao
Venue: MICCAI 2026
First: 2026-06-26T15:20:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:20:08+00:00
Comments: Accepted to MICCAI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Identifying robust associations between cardiac imaging phenotypes and clinical diseases is fundamental to population-scale cardiovascular research and reliable risk stratification. However, current phenome-wide association studies rely on pre-defined, single-variable phenotypes or expert-crafted features, which limits their ability to capture clinically meaningful non-linear effects and cross-phenotype interactions. To address this, we propose CPAgents, an iterative phenotype-Composition framework for cardiovascular Phenome-wide association study (PheWAS) that automatically constructs and validates interpretable composite phenotypes (e.g., polynomial, ratio, and interaction forms) from base imaging features. Specifically, our system coordinates three agents: (i) an Analyst that identifies statistical pathologies and nominates candidate transformations; (ii) a Proposer that generates constrained, medically and statistically motivated expressions under numerical safety rules; and (iii) a Verifier that evaluates candidates using multi-stage criteria and produces transparent evidence trails for accepted phenotypes. Evaluated on a population-scale cardiac imaging cohort, the discovered composite phenotypes markedly improve disease discrimination: across 72 classifier-disease-metric combinations, our variants achieve the top rank in 56 cases versus 18 for baselines, with gains observed across all nine clinical disease categories. Our framework yields compact, clinically interpretable phenotype formulas with transparent evidence trails, enabling scalable discovery of stronger phenotype-disease associations beyond expert-driven feature selection.

LinkAnchor: An Autonomous LLM-Based Agent for Issue-to-Commit Link Recovery
Authors: Arshia Akhavan, Alireza Hoseinpour, Abbas Heydarnoori, Hamid Bagheri, Mehdi Keshani
First: 2025-08-17T04:21:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:18:28+00:00
Comments: Proceedings of the ACM International Conference on the Foundations of Software Engineering (FSE), Montreal, Canada, July 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Issue-to-commit link recovery in software repositories is fundamental to software traceability and project management, yet it remains a challenging task. Prior studies show that only about 42.2% of issues on GitHub are correctly linked to their commits, highlighting the need for more effective solutions. Existing work has explored a range of ML/DL approaches, and more recently, large language models (LLMs) have been applied to this problem. However, these methods face two major limitations. First, LLMs are restricted by limited context windows and cannot simultaneously process all available data sources, such as long commit histories, extensive issue discussions, and large code repositories. Second, most approaches operate on individual issue-commit pairs, where a model independently scores the relevance of a single commit to an issue. This pairwise formulation fails to account for the complex associativity of software fixes, where an issue is often resolved by an aggregate chain of commits rather than a single atomic change. By ignoring these temporal and parental dependencies, existing methods often fail to incorporate the complete resolution logic and might misidentify intermediate commits as final fixes. Furthermore, this strategy is computationally inefficient in large repositories, as it requires exhaustively evaluating an enormous number of candidate pairs. To address these challenges, we present LinkAnchor, the first autonomous LLM-based agent designed specifically for issue-to-commit link recovery. LinkAnchor introduces a lazy-access architecture that allows the underlying LLM to dynamically retrieve only the most relevant contextual data, such as commits, issue comments, and code files, without exceeding token limits.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LinkAnchor：一种基于大语言模型的自主代理，用于问题到提交链接恢复
在软件仓库中恢复问题到提交的链接是软件可追溯性和项目管理的基础，但这一任务仍具挑战性。现有研究表明，GitHub上仅有约42.2%的问题正确链接到其提交，凸显了对更有效解决方案的需求。已有研究探索了多种机器学习/深度学习方法，最近则开始应用大语言模型（LLMs）解决此问题。然而，这些方法面临两大局限：首先，LLMs受限于有限的上下文窗口，无法同时处理所有数据源，如长提交历史、广泛的讨论和大型代码库；其次，多数方法基于单个问题-提交对独立评分，忽略了软件修复的复杂关联性——一个问题通常由一系列提交的累积链解决，而非单一原子变更。忽略这些时序和父依赖关系，现有方法常无法完整纳入修复逻辑，可能误将中间提交识别为最终修复。此外，该策略在大型仓库中计算效率低下，需穷举大量候选对。为应对这些挑战，我们提出LinkAnchor，这是首个专为问题到提交链接恢复设计的自主LLM代理。LinkAnchor引入懒加载架构，使底层LLM可动态仅检索最相关的上下文数据（如提交、问题评论和代码文件），而不超出令牌限制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the low accuracy of issue-to-commit linking in software repositories—only 42.2% of GitHub issues are correctly linked—this paper proposes LinkAnchor, an autonomous LLM-based agent designed to overcome limitations of existing pairwise methods. LinkAnchor employs a lazy-access architecture that dynamically retrieves only relevant contextual data (commits, comments, code) within token constraints, avoiding the inefficiency and context limits of traditional LLM approaches. Experimental results demonstrate that LinkAnchor significantly outperforms prior methods by capturing the temporal and parental dependencies of software fixes, correctly identifying resolution chains rather than isolated commits, and achieving higher precision and recall in large-scale repositories.
鉴于GitHub上仅有约42.2%的issue能正确链接到对应提交，本文提出LinkAnchor，这是首个专为issue到提交链接恢复设计的自主LLM代理。LinkAnchor采用懒加载架构，动态仅获取相关上下文数据（如提交、评论、代码），在不超出令牌限制的前提下提升效率。实验表明，LinkAnchor显著优于现有LLM及ML/DL方法，能捕捉软件修复的时序与父子依赖关系，准确识别修复链而非单个提交，并通过选择性上下文加载降低计算开销。
Automating RT Planning at Scale: High Quality Data For AI Training
Authors: Riqiang Gao, Mamadou Diallo, Han Liu, Anthony Magliari, Jonathan Sackett, Wilko Verbakel, Sandra Meyers, Rafe Mcbeth, Masoud Zarepisheh, Simon Arberet, Martin Kraus, Florin C. Ghesu, Ali Kamen
First: 2025-01-21T00:44:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:16:25+00:00
Comments: radiotherapy planning, data for AI training
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Radiotherapy (RT) planning is complex, subjective, and time-intensive. Advances with artificial intelligence (AI) promise to improve its precision and efficiency, but progress is often limited by the scarcity of large, standardized datasets. To address this, we introduce the Automated Iterative RT Planning (AIRTP) system, a scalable solution for generating high-quality treatment plans. This scalable solution is designed to generate substantial volumes of consistently high-quality treatment plans, overcoming a key obstacle in the advancement of AI-driven RT planning. Our AIRTP pipeline adheres to clinical guidelines and automates essential steps, including organ-at-risk (OAR) contouring, helper structure creation, beam setup, optimization, and plan quality improvement, using AI integrated with RT planning software like Varian Eclipse. Furthermore, a novel approach for determining optimization parameters to reproduce 3D dose distributions, i.e. a method to convert dose predictions to deliverable treatment plans constrained by machine limitations is proposed. A comparative analysis of plan quality reveals that our automated pipeline produces treatment plans of quality comparable to those generated manually, which traditionally require several hours of labor per plan. Committed to public research, the first data release of our AIRTP pipeline includes nine cohorts covering head-and-neck and lung cancer sites to support an AAPM 2025 challenge. To our best knowledge, this dataset features more than 10 times number of plans compared to the largest existing well-curated public dataset. Repo: https://github.com/RiqiangGao/GDP-HMM_AAPMChallenge.

A Gaussian Perspective for Distributional Discrepancy in Generative Diffusion Models
Authors: Qiang Sun, H. Vincent Poor, Wenyi Zhang
First: 2026-01-20T05:06:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:06:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper introduces an analytical approach to quantifying and optimizing the distributional discrepancy in generative diffusion models. For a multivariate Gaussian source, we explicitly derive the closed-form evolution trajectory and the resulting Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence between the distributions of the source data and the reversely sampled data. Asymptotic analysis via the Euler-Maclaurin expansion characterizes the convergence behavior of this KL divergence, extracting its dominant term as an explicit functional of the noise schedule. Minimizing this dominant term via the calculus of variations yields a noise schedule described by a tangent law, inherently determined by the source covariance spectrum. We further prove that the Gaussian source exhibits an extremal property for the KL divergence among general source distributions with a given covariance. We also utilize the analytical KL divergence as a principled metric to identify efficient time discretization strategies for pretrained diffusion models, and demonstrate via experiments over diverse datasets that the identified strategies consistently outperform established baselines, particularly under constrained function evaluation budgets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：生成扩散模型中分布差异的高斯视角
本文提出了一种分析方法，用于量化和优化生成扩散模型中的分布差异。对于多元高斯源分布，我们显式推导出其闭式演化轨迹及源数据分布与反向采样数据分布之间的Kullback-Leibler（KL）散度。通过欧拉-麦克劳林展开的渐近分析，刻画了该KL散度的收敛行为，并将其主导项表示为噪声调度的显式函数。通过变分法最小化该主导项，得到由源协方差谱内在决定的切线律噪声调度。我们进一步证明，高斯源在给定协方差的通用源分布中具有KL散度的极值性质。此外，我们利用该分析KL散度作为原理性度量，识别预训练扩散模型的有效时间离散化策略，并在多个数据集上的实验表明，所识别的策略在受限函数评估预算下持续优于现有基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to quantify and optimize distributional discrepancy in generative diffusion models, this paper derives a closed-form expression for the Kullback-Leibler divergence between source and reversely sampled data under a multivariate Gaussian assumption. Using asymptotic analysis via Euler-Maclaurin expansion, it identifies the dominant term of the KL divergence as a function of the noise schedule, and minimizes it via calculus of variations to obtain an optimal noise schedule governed by a tangent law derived from the source covariance spectrum. The paper further proves that the Gaussian source minimizes KL divergence among all distributions with fixed covariance, and applies the analytical KL divergence as a metric to guide efficient time discretization in pretrained models, showing consistent performance gains over baselines under constrained evaluation budgets.
为量化和优化生成扩散模型中的分布差异，本文在多元高斯源假设下推导出源数据与反向采样数据间KL散度的闭式表达。通过欧拉-麦克劳林展开的渐近分析，识别出KL散度主导项作为噪声调度的显式函数，并通过变分法最小化该项，得到由源协方差谱决定的切线律噪声调度。论文进一步证明高斯源在给定协方差下具有KL散度极小的极值性质，并利用该分析KL散度作为指导原则，为预训练扩散模型选择高效的时间离散策略，实验表明在函数评估预算受限时，该策略显著优于现有基线方法。
Psychometric Comparability of LLM-Based Digital Twins
Authors: Yufei Zhang, Zhihao Ma
First: 2025-12-22T18:04:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:04:48+00:00
Comments: Also available as a preprint on OSF Preprints https://osf.io/preprints/psyarxiv/965yg_v2
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) act as digital twins for human respondents, yet their psychometric comparability remains uncertain. We propose a construct validity framework spanning construct representation and the nomothetic span, benchmarking models against human gold standards. Across studies, digital twins achieved high aggregate-level accuracy and profile correlations, but showed attenuated item-level correlations. In word association tests, LLM networks exhibited humanlike small-world structure and theory-consistent communities, yet diverged lexically and in local structure. In decision-making and contextualized tasks, they under-reproduced heuristic biases, demonstrating normative rationality, compressed variance, and limited temporal sensitivity. Feature-rich and trait relevant conditioning improved Big Five personality prediction and nomothetic-span alignment, but network invariance remained limited, with partial configural solutions and persistent loading differences. In cross-language free-text tasks in English and Chinese, feature-rich digital twins better approximated construct-level narrative content, but linguistic and idiographic differences persisted. These findings clarify that digital twins are most useful within validated boundaries, where the construct, task and level of inference align with evidence from human data.

标题：基于大语言模型的数字孪生的心理测量可比性
大语言模型（LLMs）可作为人类受访者的数字孪生，但其心理测量可比性尚不明确。我们提出一个涵盖构念表征与法则跨度的构念效度框架，将模型与人类黄金标准进行基准比较。在多项研究中，数字孪生在整体层面表现出高准确性与剖面相关性，但在项目层面相关性较低。在词语联想测试中，LLM网络展现出类人小世界结构与理论一致的社群，但在词汇层面和局部结构上存在差异。在决策与情境化任务中，它们未能充分再现启发式偏差，表现出规范理性、方差压缩及有限的时间敏感性。富特征且与特质相关的条件化提升了大五人格预测与法则跨度对齐，但网络不变性仍有限，存在部分配置解与持续的载荷差异。在英语与中文的跨语言自由文本任务中，富特征数字孪生更接近构念层面的叙事内容，但语言与个案差异仍存。这些发现表明，数字孪生在构念、任务与推断层级与人类数据证据一致的验证范围内最为有用。

SpatialFlow-GRPO: Where Spatial Credit Drives Image Editing
Authors: Yankai Yang, Yancheng Long, Wei Chen, Xingyu Lu, Hongyang Wei, Bin Wen, Fan Yang, Tingting Gao, Han Li, Shuo Yang
First: 2026-06-25T10:58:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:03:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent online reinforcement learning has substantially improved image editing quality. However, existing Flow-GRPO-style methods usually rely on a single whole-image reward, which makes fine-grained editing optimization difficult. We observe that a key obstacle in image editing is this spatial uniformity assumption: a whole-image reward cannot distinguish how different spatial regions contribute to image quality. To address this issue, we propose SpatialFlow-GRPO, a training framework that introduces spatially fine-grained reward feedback. The framework converts region-aware rewards into semantic-region-level optimization signals and aligns region advantages with the corresponding latent positions during policy updates. We also train a region-aware reward model, SFReward, construct SFReward-14K with region-annotated editing samples, and introduce MultiEditBench to evaluate multi-region editing ability. On OmniGen2 and FLUX.2-klein-4B, SpatialFlow-GRPO outperforms Flow-GRPO on GEdit-Bench, ImgEdit-Bench, and MultiEditBench. The results show that SpatialFlow-GRPO converts local feedback into spatially aligned update signals and improves editing quality.

标题：SpatialFlow-GRPO：空间信用驱动图像编辑
近期在线强化学习显著提升了图像编辑质量。然而，现有Flow-GRPO类方法通常依赖单一整体图像奖励，导致细粒度编辑优化困难。我们观察到图像编辑的关键障碍在于空间均匀性假设：整体图像奖励无法区分不同空间区域对图像质量的贡献。为解决此问题，我们提出SpatialFlow-GRPO训练框架，引入空间细粒度奖励反馈。该框架将区域感知奖励转化为语义区域级优化信号，并在策略更新时将区域优势与对应潜在位置对齐。我们还训练了区域感知奖励模型SFReward，构建了包含区域标注编辑样本的SFReward-14K数据集，并引入MultiEditBench评估多区域编辑能力。在OmniGen2和FLUX.2-klein-4B上，SpatialFlow-GRPO在GEdit-Bench、ImgEdit-Bench和MultiEditBench上均优于Flow-GRPO。结果表明，SpatialFlow-GRPO将局部反馈转化为空间对齐的更新信号，提升了编辑质量。

为解决Flow-GRPO类方法依赖单一全局奖励导致的细粒度图像编辑优化困难问题，作者提出SpatialFlow-GRPO框架，将区域感知奖励转化为语义区域级优化信号，并在策略更新时将区域优势与对应潜在位置对齐。该框架结合训练区域感知奖励模型SFReward-14K及多区域编辑评估基准MultiEditBench，在OmniGen2与FLUX.2-klein-4B模型上显著优于Flow-GRPO，证明空间细粒度奖励反馈可有效提升图像编辑质量。
MedGuards: Multi-Agent System for Reliable Medical Error Detection and Correction
Authors: Congbo Ma, Hu Wang, Yichun Zhang, Farah E. Shamout
First: 2026-06-24T10:07:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:03:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in healthcare settings, accurate error detection and correction in generated or existing text becomes critical, as even minor mistakes can pose risks to patient safety. Existing methods for error detection and correction, including automated checks and heuristic-based approaches, do not generalize well across unseen datasets. In this paper, we propose MedGuards as a medical safety guardrail, which is a new framework that treats medical error detection and correction as a multi-agent in-context learning task. Specialized agents separately detect, localize, and correct errors, while a confidence-guided arbitration mechanism resolves disagreements using reasoning traces and confidence scores. This design enhances interpretability, robustness, and adaptability, without requiring additional training of the base LLMs. Additionally, we introduce the Keyword-Prioritized Correction Score (KPCS), a new evaluation metric that considers whether critical keywords within the reference text are generated correctly, providing a more comprehensive assessment than conventional metrics. Experiments across four multilingual medical datasets consisting of clinical notes demonstrate significant improvements by the proposed framework across several metrics and models. Our aim is to enable safer deployment of LLMs in real-world healthcare applications. For reproducibility, we make our code publicly available at https://github.com/congboma/MedGuards.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MedGuards：用于可靠医疗错误检测与修正的多智能体系统
随着大型语言模型（LLMs）在医疗环境中的广泛应用，对生成或现有文本中错误的准确检测与修正变得至关重要，因为即使是微小的错误也可能危及患者安全。现有错误检测与修正方法，包括自动化检查和启发式方法，难以在未见数据集上泛化。本文提出MedGuards，作为医疗安全护栏，将医疗错误检测与修正视为多智能体上下文学习任务。专用智能体分别负责检测、定位和修正错误，而基于置信度的仲裁机制通过推理轨迹和置信度分数解决分歧。该设计提升了可解释性、鲁棒性和适应性，且无需对基础LLMs进行额外训练。此外，我们引入关键词优先修正得分（KPCS），一种新的评估指标，关注参考文本中关键术语是否正确生成，提供比传统指标更全面的评估。在四个多语言医疗数据集（包含临床笔记）上的实验表明，所提框架在多个指标和模型上均显著提升性能。我们的目标是推动LLMs在真实医疗应用中的更安全部署。为确保可复现性，代码已公开于https://github.com/congboma/MedGuards。

Evaluated across four multilingual clinical datasets, MedGuards consistently outperforms baseline methods in accuracy, robustness, and adaptability without retraining the underlying LLMs, and introduces the Keyword-Prioritized Correction Score (KPCS) to better assess critical medical content correctness.
为提升大语言模型在医疗场景中的安全性，MedGuards提出一种多智能体系统，将医疗文本纠错视为上下文学习任务。系统由专门负责检测、定位与修正错误的智能体组成，通过基于置信度的仲裁机制结合推理轨迹与置信分数解决冲突。在四个多语言临床数据集上的实验表明，该框架在多个指标上显著优于基线方法，且无需重新训练基础模型。新引入的关键词优先修正得分（KPCS）评估指标更关注关键医学术语的准确性，从而提供更贴近临床需求的评估结果。
Dual-Prior Guided Null-Space Learning with Mixture-of-Splines for Arbitrary Medical Slice Super-Resolution
Authors: Haofei Song, Siyuan Xu, Xintian Mao, Shaojie Guo, Qingli Li, Yan Wang
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-25T07:53:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:03:16+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ECCV 2026! Project page: https://github.com/DeepMed-Lab-ECNU/Medical-Image-Reconstruction
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Arbitrary slice super-resolution reconstructs isotropic volumes from anisotropic clinical acquisitions by synthesizing intermediate slices at arbitrary scales. However, treating this ill-posed inverse problem as unconstrained residual-based regression risks hallucinating anatomically implausible structures or altering the originally observed data. To address both concerns, this paper presents the Dual-Prior Null-space Learning (DP-NSL) framework, which reformulates the task as a constrained recovery process guided by two complementary priors. A Measurement-Consistent Projection (MCP) enforces a Deterministic Observation Prior: the reconstruction undergoes an exact orthogonal projection that reproduces every acquired slice with zero error, confining all learned details to the unobservable null space. Within this null space, a Mixture-of-Splines (MoS) module imposes a Geometric Continuity Prior by dynamically mixing B-spline experts of different analytic orders, allowing each anatomical region to be modeled with a content-aware level of continuity. To promote spatial coherence, a Local Spatial Consistency Decoder (LSCD) further injects local inductive bias. Experiments on three CT and one MRI benchmark show that DP-NSL outperforms existing approaches while strictly preserving measurement consistency. Code is available at https://github.com/DeepMed-Lab-ECNU/Medical-Image-Reconstruction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于混合样条的双先验引导零空间学习用于任意医学切片超分辨率重建
任意切片超分辨率重建通过合成任意尺度的中间切片，从各向异性临床采集数据中重建各向同性体积。然而，将这一不适定逆问题视为无约束残差回归，可能产生解剖学上不合理的结构或改变原始观测数据。为解决上述问题，本文提出双先验零空间学习（DP-NSL）框架，将任务重新表述为受两个互补先验引导的约束恢复过程。测量一致性投影（MCP）强制执行确定性观测先验：重建结果经精确正交投影，可无误差复现所有采集切片，将所有学习到的细节限制在不可观测的零空间内。在该零空间内，混合样条（MoS）模块通过动态混合不同解析阶数的B样条专家，施加几何连续性先验，使每个解剖区域可按内容感知的连续性水平建模。为促进空间一致性，局部空间一致性解码器（LSCD）进一步注入局部归纳偏置。在三个CT和一个MRI基准数据集上的实验表明，DP-NSL在严格保持测量一致性的同时优于现有方法。代码见 https://github.com/DeepMed-Lab-ECNU/Medical-Image-Reconstruction。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to avoid anatomical hallucinations and preserve original data in arbitrary medical slice super-resolution, this work introduces the Dual-Prior Null-space Learning (DP-NSL) framework. It reformulates the problem as a constrained recovery guided by two priors: a Measurement-Consistent Projection (MCP) ensures exact reconstruction of input slices by confining new details to the null space, while a Mixture-of-Splines (MoS) module enforces geometric continuity by adaptively blending B-spline experts of varying orders. A Local Spatial Consistency Decoder (LSCD) further enhances spatial coherence. Experiments on four medical imaging benchmarks demonstrate that DP-NSL achieves superior reconstruction quality while strictly maintaining measurement consistency compared to existing methods.
为解决任意医学切片超分辨率中可能产生的解剖结构伪影和原始数据失真问题，本文提出双先验引导的零空间学习框架（DP-NSL）。该框架将任务重构为受两个先验约束的恢复过程：测量一致性投影（MCP）通过正交投影精确复现输入切片，将新细节限制在零空间内；零空间内使用混合样条（MoS）模块通过动态混合不同阶次的B样条专家，实现区域自适应的几何连续性约束。局部空间一致性解码器进一步增强空间连贯性。在四个医学影像基准测试中，DP-NSL在严格保持测量一致性的同时，显著优于现有方法。
Tandem Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards
Authors: Difan Jiao, Raghav Singhal, Robert West, Ashton Anderson
First: 2026-06-26T15:00:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T15:00:35+00:00
Comments: 21 pages,7 figures,8 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has significantly improved the reasoning capability of large language models, reaching expert or even superhuman performance in domains such as competition math. However, whether weaker agents and humans can actually harness this capability is far less certain, with RLVR documented to drift reasoning toward idiosyncratic patterns such as poor readability and language mixing. Tandem training is a recently introduced paradigm that targets this compatibility problem: a trained, stronger senior co-generates each rollout with a frozen, weaker junior, and the two are rewarded as a team, so the senior is pushed to reason in ways the junior can follow. Yet this paradigm has so far been demonstrated only in proof-of-concept settings, leaving open whether it scales to the long chains of thought of the modern RLVR pipeline. In this work, we propose Tandem Reinforcement Learning (TRL), which carries the tandem training paradigm into RLVR. In TRL, the senior and a frozen junior alternate stochastically to co-generate the reasoning, the resulting generation is rewarded, and the standard GRPO loss is applied to the senior. Training Qwen3-4B-Instruct on competition math, we find that TRL matches vanilla GRPO on solo reasoning capability while three properties emerge together from the same rollout structure: stronger handoff robustness with the junior, reduced distributional drift from the junior, and a chain-of-thought more legible to the junior. Our results demonstrate a promising route for RLVR with practical payoffs in multi-model communication and human compatibility.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可验证奖励的双人强化学习
可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）显著提升了大语言模型的推理能力，在竞赛数学等领域达到专家甚至超人类水平。然而，较弱的代理和人类能否真正利用这一能力仍不确定，RLVR已被证实会向非标准模式（如可读性差、语言混杂）偏移。双人训练是一种新提出的范式，旨在解决这一兼容性问题：训练好的较强“资深”代理与冻结的较弱“初级”代理共同生成每个 rollout，二者作为团队获得奖励，从而促使资深代理以初级代理能理解的方式进行推理。但该范式迄今仅在概念验证设置中展示，尚未验证其能否扩展到现代 RLVR 流程中长推理链。本文提出双人强化学习（TRL），将双人训练范式引入 RLVR。在 TRL 中，资深代理与冻结的初级代理随机交替共同生成推理，对生成结果进行奖励，并对资深代理应用标准 GRPO 损失。在竞赛数学上训练 Qwen3-4B-Instruct，我们发现 TRL 在单人推理能力上与标准 GRPO 相当，同时从同一 rollout 结构中涌现出三个特性：与初级代理更强的交接鲁棒性、来自初级代理的分布偏移减少、以及更易被初级代理理解的思维链。我们的结果展示了 RLVR 一个有前景的路径，对多模型通信和人类兼容性具有实际价值。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the issue that RLVR models often drift toward unreadable or incoherent reasoning patterns that hinder human compatibility, this work introduces Tandem Reinforcement Learning (TRL), a method where a strong senior model and a frozen junior model alternately co-generate reasoning steps in a stochastic fashion, with the joint output rewarded and the senior trained via GRPO. Experiments on Qwen3-4B-Instruct for competition math show that TRL matches vanilla GRPO in solo reasoning performance while simultaneously achieving stronger handoff robustness, reduced distributional drift from the junior, and more legible chain-of-thoughts, demonstrating practical benefits for multi-model collaboration and human interpretability.
为解决RLVR模型输出的推理过程难以被弱模型或人类理解的问题，本文提出Tandem Reinforcement Learning（TRL）方法，通过让训练好的高级代理与冻结的初级代理交替协同生成推理步骤，以团队形式获得奖励并仅更新高级代理的GRPO损失。在竞赛数学任务上对Qwen3-4B-Instruct进行实验发现，TRL在单代理推理能力上与标准GRPO相当，同时在同一次推理中实现了更强的代理间交接鲁棒性、更少的分布漂移以及更易被初级代理理解的推理链，表明该方法在多模型协作与人机兼容性方面具有实用价值。
EchoSonar-R: A Multi-View Reasoning-Enabled Model for Disease Classification and Report Generation in Echocardiography
Authors: Darya Taratynova, Ahmed Aly, Numan Saeed, Mohammad Yaqub
First: 2026-06-26T14:58:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T14:58:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Echocardiography is the most widely used non-invasive cardiac imaging modality, providing essential information for cardiovascular diagnosis. Interpreting an echocardiogram requires synthesizing complementary evidence across multiple heart views to identify abnormalities and produce structured clinical reports. While recent efforts focus on improving classification performance, most models lack explicit diagnostic reasoning and spatially grounded anatomical evidence, limiting clinician trust. We present EchoSonar-R, a multi-view reasoning-enabled vision-language model that jointly performs multi-label disease classification and report generation from echocardiography studies. EchoSonar-R combines a spatiotemporal video encoder with a structure-aware cardiac detector that provides spatially grounded anatomical cues to improve interpretability and clinician trust during cross-view reasoning. EchoSonar-R is trained in two stages: supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on reasoning-annotated targets, followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with task-specific rewards that jointly align classification and report generation within a unified reinforcement-learning framework. Across a private multi-view dataset and two public benchmarks, EchoSonar-R improves macro balanced accuracy by 17.1% on the private set and 6.1% on MIMICEchoQA over the strongest baseline, achieves a GREEN clinical faithfulness score of 0.800, and produces interpretable reasoning traces grounded in multi-view visual evidence.

标题：EchoSonar-R：一种支持多视角推理的超声心动图疾病分类与报告生成模型
超声心动图是最常用的无创心脏成像方式，为心血管诊断提供关键信息。解读超声心动图需综合多个心脏视角的互补证据以识别异常并生成结构化临床报告。尽管近期研究侧重提升分类性能，多数模型缺乏明确的诊断推理和空间锚定的解剖学证据，限制了临床医生的信任。我们提出EchoSonar-R，一种支持多视角推理的视觉-语言模型，可联合执行超声心动图研究中的多标签疾病分类与报告生成。EchoSonar-R结合时空视频编码器与结构感知心脏检测器，提供空间锚定的解剖学线索，提升跨视角推理的可解释性与临床信任度。模型分两阶段训练：首先在推理标注目标上进行监督微调（SFT），随后采用任务特定奖励的组相对策略优化（GRPO），在统一强化学习框架内联合对齐分类与报告生成。在私有多视角数据集及两个公开基准上，EchoSonar-R相较最强基线在私有数据集上提升宏平衡准确率17.1%，在MIMICEchoQA上提升6.1%，获得临床可信度GREEN评分0.800，并生成基于多视角视觉证据的可解释推理轨迹。
