为提升超声心动图诊断的可解释性和临床可信度，EchoSonar-R 构建了一个多视角推理模型，联合进行心脏疾病分类与报告生成。该模型结合时空视频编码器与结构感知心脏检测器，提取空间锚定的解剖线索以增强推理透明性。通过两阶段训练——先在标注推理目标上进行监督微调，再采用任务特定奖励的组相对策略优化——模型在私有数据集上宏观平衡准确率提升17.1%，在MIMICEchoQA上提升6.1%，并获得0.800的GREEN临床忠实度评分，表明其推理过程与多视角视觉证据高度一致，符合临床需求。
Enhanced Neural Video Representation Compression across Extreme Complexity and Quality Scales
Authors: Ho Man Kwan, Tianhao Peng, Fan Zhang, Mike Nilsson, Andrew Gower, David Bull
First: 2026-06-26T14:58:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T14:58:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Implicit neural representations (INRs) have recently emerged as a promising approach to video compression, delivering competitive rate-distortion performance alongside rapid decoding. However, existing neural video codecs struggle to balance complexity and scalability. Lightweight models often suffer from degraded compression performance when scaled to different bitrate/quality levels, whereas high-performance models exhibit limited scalability, as their model complexity typically increases with quality. This lack of a unified architecture capable of maintaining consistent complexity across a wide range of bitrates severely limits their diverse real-world deployment. To address these challenges, we introduce NVRC++, a novel INR-based video codec that utilizes a lightweight INR with multiple high-resolution feature grids, providing high scalability at any given complexity level. This is paired with an optimization framework that enables efficient overfitting on high-resolution grids for long video sequences, thereby exploiting spatio-temporal redundancies without prohibitive computational or memory overhead. Additionally, an advanced entropy model is designed for efficiently compressing the high-dimensional grid parameters. As a result, NVRC++ provides four complexity levels (from 7kMACs/pixel to 360kMACs/pixel), each spanning wide bitrate and quality ranges while supporting real-time decoding. The experimental results show that NVRC++ offers a much faster decoding speed (up to 7.6x) compared to the SOTA INR-based video codec, NVRC, while delivering comparable performance.

标题：跨极端复杂度与质量尺度的增强型神经视频表示压缩
隐式神经表示（INR）近期作为视频压缩的有前景方法出现，可提供具有竞争力的码率-失真性能并实现快速解码。然而，现有神经视频编解码器难以平衡复杂度与可扩展性：轻量级模型在不同码率/质量级别扩展时压缩性能下降，高性能模型则因复杂度随质量提升而缺乏可扩展性。缺乏统一架构以在宽范围码率下保持一致复杂度，严重限制其多样化的实际部署。为解决这些问题，我们提出NVRC++，一种基于INR的新型视频编解码器，采用轻量级INR配合多个高分辨率特征网格，可在任意复杂度水平提供高可扩展性。该架构结合优化框架，可高效对高分辨率网格进行过拟合以处理长视频序列，从而利用时空冗余而不产生过高的计算或内存开销。此外，设计了先进的熵模型以高效压缩高维网格参数。结果表明，NVRC++提供四个复杂度级别（从7kMACs/像素至360kMACs/像素），每个级别均覆盖宽码率与质量范围并支持实时解码。实验结果表明，NVRC++相比当前最优INR视频编解码器NVRC解码速度提升高达7.6倍，同时保持相当的性能。

Experimental results demonstrate that NVRC++ achieves up to 7.6x faster decoding than the prior state-of-the-art NVRC codec while maintaining comparable rate-distortion performance.
为解决现有神经视频编码器在复杂度与质量尺度间难以平衡的问题，本文提出NVRC++，一种基于隐式神经表示的视频编码器，通过轻量级神经网络搭配多分辨率特征网格，在任意复杂度水平下实现高可扩展性。该方法结合优化框架，支持在高分辨率网格上高效过拟合长视频序列，利用时空冗余而不增加计算与内存负担，并设计高级熵模型压缩高维网格参数。实验表明，NVRC++支持四个复杂度层级（7k至360k MACs/像素），每层覆盖宽泛码率与质量范围并支持实时解码，解码速度较当前最优的NVRC提升最高达7.6倍，性能相当。
Recovering Sharp Conductivity Features in the Finite-Data Calderón Problem with Physics-Informed Neural Networks
Authors: Ali AlHadi Kalout, Pablo Tejerina-Pérez, Konstantin Karchev, Pedro Tarancón-Álvarez, Leonid Sarieddine, Raul Jimenez, Max Engelstein, Guy David
First: 2026-06-26T14:54:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T14:54:32+00:00
Comments: 41 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have recently emerged as a promising framework for addressing the Calderón inverse problem from limited boundary data. In this work, we revisit neural Calderón inversion by introducing multiscale boundary excitations based on randomized wavelet functions and investigating the role of Fourier-feature encoding (FFE) for representing sharp conductivity variations. We propose a physics-informed reconstruction framework that represents the unknown conductivity and the associated family of electric potentials with separate neural networks conditioned on the applied boundary excitations. The governing elliptic PDE is enforced through physics-informed residuals, while finite Dirichlet-to-Neumann (DtN) data are incorporated through boundary losses. Using synthetic data from a finite-difference forward solver, we evaluate the method on conductivity fields with inclusions, sharp interfaces, smooth profiles, and heterogeneous media. Results show that the framework recovers dominant conductivity structures from finite boundary measurements with relative errors between $3\%-12\%$ approximately. We show that FFE improves the reconstruction of localized sharp features, particularly for inclusions and interfaces, but are not universally optimal, with raw-coordinate networks performing competitively for smoother fields. These results highlight coordinate representations and boundary excitation design as key factors in neural Calderón inversion.

标题：利用物理信息神经网络从有限数据中恢复卡勒龙问题中的尖锐导电特性
物理信息神经网络（PINNs）最近被提出作为解决从有限边界数据出发的卡勒龙反问题的一种有前景的框架。本文重新审视神经卡勒龙反演，引入基于随机小波函数的多尺度边界激励，并研究傅里叶特征编码（FFE）在表示尖锐导电变化中的作用。我们提出一种物理信息重建框架，通过独立的神经网络分别表示未知导电率及其相关的电势族，这些网络以施加的边界激励为条件。通过物理信息残差强制执行控制椭圆偏微分方程，同时通过边界损失引入有限狄利克雷-诺伊曼（DtN）数据。我们使用有限差分正向求解器生成的合成数据，在包含、尖锐界面、平滑轮廓和非均匀介质的导电场中评估该方法。结果表明，该框架可从有限边界测量中恢复主导导电结构，相对误差约为3%-12%。我们发现FFE能改善局部尖锐特征的重建，特别是对包含物和界面，但并非普遍最优，原始坐标网络在平滑场中表现具有竞争力。这些结果突出了坐标表示和边界激励设计在神经卡勒龙反演中的关键作用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of recovering sharp conductivity features from limited boundary measurements in the finite-data Calderón problem, this work employs physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) enhanced with multiscale boundary excitations via randomized wavelet functions and Fourier-feature encoding (FFE) to represent conductivity variations. Experiments on synthetic conductivity fields—including inclusions, sharp interfaces, smooth profiles, and heterogeneous media—demonstrate that the framework achieves reconstruction accuracy with relative errors between 3% and 12%. 为解决Calderón反问题中从有限边界数据恢复尖锐电导率特征的难题，本文提出一种物理信息神经网络框架，采用基于随机小波的边界激励和傅里叶特征编码来表征电导率变化。该方法使用独立神经网络分别建模电导率与电势，通过物理信息残差强制满足椭圆偏微分方程，并通过边界损失引入有限Dirichlet-to-Neumann数据。在包含夹杂物、界面和异质介质等合成电导率场的实验中，该框架相对误差控制在3–12%之间，傅里叶特征编码对局部尖锐特征恢复效果更优，但在平滑场中，原始坐标网络表现更具竞争力。
Robust Harmful Features Under Jailbreak Attacks: Mechanistic Evidence from Attention Head Specialization in Large Language Models
Authors: Yanchen Yin, Dongqi Han, Linghui Li
Venue: ICML 2026 Oral presentation
First: 2026-06-26T14:51:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T14:51:16+00:00
Comments: 323 pages, 19 figures. Accepted at ICML 2026 as a Oral presentation
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Jailbreak attacks bypass LLM safety alignment, yet their mechanisms remain poorly understood. We provide evidence that attacks do not comprehensively eliminate safety features, but instead selectively suppress specific attention heads. We identify two functionally differentiated types: Adversarially Compromised Heads (ACHs) concentrated in early layers, which are suppressed under attacks, and Safety-Aligned Heads (SAHs) in mid-layers, which maintain robust activations even when attacks succeed. Ablation studies support the causal role of ACHs and the contribution of SAHs to robust activations: suppressing a small number of ACHs is sufficient to induce jailbreak-like behavior on normally refused inputs, while removing SAHs substantially weakens mid-layer safety activations. Token-level attribution further shows that ACH suppression is driven specifically by attack-template tokens, providing a mechanistic account of why attacks can bypass refusal decisions through ACH suppression while leaving internal safety signals sustained by SAHs -- a phenomenon we term Robust Harmful Features. To validate the practical significance of this robustness, we show that simply reading these persistent activations -- without any training -- yields competitive aggregate detection performance with strong adversarial robustness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：越狱攻击下的鲁棒有害特征：来自大语言模型注意力头特化的机制证据
越狱攻击可绕过大语言模型的安全对齐，但其机制尚不明确。我们提供证据表明，攻击并未全面消除安全特征，而是选择性抑制特定的注意力头。我们识别出两种功能上不同的类型：集中在早期层、在攻击下被抑制的对抗性受损头（ACHs），以及位于中层、即使攻击成功仍保持稳健激活的安全对齐头（SAHs）。消融研究支持ACHs的因果作用及SAHs对稳健激活的贡献：抑制少量ACHs即可诱导正常被拒绝输入的越狱式行为，而移除SAHs则显著削弱中层安全激活。词级归因进一步表明，ACH抑制由攻击模板词驱动，从而解释了为何攻击可通过抑制ACHs绕过拒绝决策，同时保留由SAHs维持的内部安全信号——我们称之为鲁棒有害特征。为验证该鲁棒性的实际意义，我们表明，仅读取这些持续激活（无需任何训练），即可获得具有强对抗鲁棒性的竞争性聚合检测性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how jailbreak attacks bypass LLM safety mechanisms, this study investigates the internal attention dynamics of large language models under such attacks. It identifies two distinct types of attention heads: Adversarially Compromised Heads (ACHs) in early layers that are suppressed by attacks, and Safety-Aligned Heads (SAHs) in mid-layers that remain active even when attacks succeed. Experimental ablation shows that suppressing a small number of ACHs can induce jailbreak-like behavior, while removing SAHs weakens safety activations significantly. Token-level attribution confirms that ACH suppression is triggered specifically by attack-template tokens, revealing a mechanism termed Robust Harmful Features — where safety signals persist via SAHs while harmful behavior emerges from ACH suppression. The study further demonstrates that detecting these persistent SAH activations alone yields competitive, robust detection performance without training.
为理解越狱攻击如何绕过大语言模型的安全机制，本研究通过分析注意力头的分化发现，攻击会针对性地抑制早期层的对抗性受损头（ACHs），而中层的安全对齐头（SAHs）则保持激活，从而实现有害输出而不完全破坏安全机制。消融实验表明，抑制少量ACHs即可诱导拒绝输入的越狱行为，而移除SAHs则显著削弱中层安全激活。词级归因显示，ACH抑制由攻击模板词驱动，揭示了“鲁棒有害特征”机制：即使ACHs被抑制，SAHs仍维持安全信号。研究进一步证明，仅检测这些持续存在的SAH激活，无需训练即可获得具有强对抗鲁棒性的竞争性检测性能。
Regularized Reward-Punishment Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Jiexin Wang, Eiji Uchibe
First: 2026-06-26T14:50:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T14:50:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose KL-Coupled Policy Regularization (KCPR), a policy coordination framework for Reward-Punishment Reinforcement Learning (RPRL). Based on KCPR, we derive KL-Coupled Soft Optimality (KCSO) and develop its deep realization, klDMP. Unlike existing RPRL approaches that optimize reward-seeking and punishment-related policies largely independently, KCPR enables direct interactions between companion policies by treating each as a dynamically learned prior for the other. KCSO yields coupled soft-optimal policies and KL-regularized Bellman operators, allowing reward and punishment information to jointly influence value propagation. To improve learning stability, we introduce a companion-prior softening mechanism and evaluate separate replay-buffer designs for balancing reward- and punishment-related experience. Experiments in grid-world and Gazebo robotic navigation tasks demonstrate that klDMP improves safety and learning stability while maintaining competitive task performance compared with DQN, SQL and softDMP. These results suggest that policy-level coordination provides an effective mechanism for integrating multiple behavioral objectives and may serve as a useful design principle for reinforcement learning systems with interacting motivational processes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：正则化奖励-惩罚强化学习
我们提出KL耦合策略正则化（KCPR），一种用于奖励-惩罚强化学习（RPRL）的策略协调框架。基于KCPR，我们推导出KL耦合软最优性（KCSO）并开发其深度实现klDMP。与现有RPRL方法大多独立优化奖励追求和惩罚相关策略不同，KCPR通过将每个策略视为对方的动态学习先验，实现伴随策略间的直接交互。KCSO生成耦合软最优策略和KL正则化贝尔曼算子，使奖励与惩罚信息能共同影响价值传播。为提升学习稳定性，我们引入伴随先验软化机制，并评估不同经验回放缓冲区设计以平衡奖励与惩罚相关经验。在网格世界和Gazebo机器人导航任务中的实验表明，klDMP相比DQN、SQL和softDMP，在保持竞争力任务表现的同时提升了安全性和学习稳定性。这些结果表明，策略层面的协调为整合多重行为目标提供了一种有效机制，或可作为具有交互动机过程的强化学习系统的设计原则。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to coordinate reward-seeking and punishment-related policies in reinforcement learning, this work introduces KL-Coupled Policy Regularization (KCPR), a framework that treats each policy as a dynamically learned prior for the other, enabling direct interaction. From KCPR, it derives KL-Coupled Soft Optimality (KCSO) and its deep implementation, klDMP, which jointly propagate reward and punishment signals via KL-regularized Bellman operators. Experiments on grid-world and robotic navigation tasks show that klDMP enhances safety and learning stability while maintaining performance comparable to DQN, SQL, and softDMP, suggesting policy-level coordination is effective for integrating multiple motivational objectives.
为解决强化学习中奖励追求与惩罚相关策略难以协调的问题，本文提出KL耦合策略正则化（KCPR）框架，将每个策略视为对方的动态先验，实现直接交互。由此推导出KL耦合软最优性（KCSO）及其深度实现klDMP，通过KL正则化贝尔曼算子联合传播奖励与惩罚信息。在网格世界和Gazebo机器人导航任务中的实验表明，klDMP在提升安全性与学习稳定性的同时，保持与DQN、SQL和softDMP相当的任务表现，表明策略层面的协调是整合多重行为目标的有效机制。
Toward Robust In-Context Segmentation via Concept Guidance
Authors: Zhigang Chen, Xiawu Zheng, Rongrong Ji
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-26T14:46:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T14:46:38+00:00
Comments: ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In-context segmentation (ICS) requires a model to segment target regions in a query image using only a few reference images and their corresponding masks, without updating any parameters. Despite recent progress, prior ICS studies have largely overlooked a critical aspect: system robustness, ie, whether the model can produce stable segmentation results for the same query under different references. In this work, we revisit ICS from the robustness perspective and introduce a novel paradigm, Concept-Guided In-Context Segmentation (CG-ICS), which performs segmentation by extracting high-level semantic concepts from references rather than relying solely on low-level visual matching. Specifically, CG-ICS introduces a concept reasoning module that uses an MLLM to propose candidates and a SAM3-driven scoring function with tree-search refinement to select reliable textual concepts, together with a parallel visual exemplar route that provides query-side spatial grounding via a simple context construction. Both the textual concept and the visual exemplar are then used to activate the segmentation capability of a frozen SAM3 backbone. Extensive experiments on standard ICS benchmarks demonstrate that CG-ICS not only achieves state-of-the-art accuracy but also substantially improves robustness, yielding a more reliable ICS system with significantly reduced variance across diverse reference choices.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过概念引导实现鲁棒的上下文内分割
上下文内分割（ICS）要求模型仅使用少量参考图像及其对应掩码，在不更新任何参数的情况下对查询图像中的目标区域进行分割。尽管近期取得了进展，但先前的ICS研究大多忽略了关键方面：系统鲁棒性，即模型是否能在不同参考图像下对同一查询图像产生稳定分割结果。本文从鲁棒性角度重新审视ICS，提出了一种新范式——概念引导上下文内分割（CG-ICS），该方法通过从参考图像中提取高层语义概念进行分割，而非仅依赖低层视觉匹配。具体而言，CG-ICS引入了一个概念推理模块，利用MLLM提出候选概念，并结合由SAM3驱动的评分函数与树搜索优化选择可靠文本概念；同时，一个并行的视觉范例路径通过简单上下文构建为查询图像提供空间定位。最终，文本概念与视觉范例共同激活冻结的SAM3主干网络的分割能力。在标准ICS基准上的大量实验表明，CG-ICS不仅实现了最先进精度，还显著提升了鲁棒性，从而构建出更可靠的ICS系统，且在不同参考选择下结果方差显著降低。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of robustness in existing in-context segmentation methods, this work introduces Concept-Guided In-Context Segmentation (CG-ICS), which improves segmentation stability by extracting high-level semantic concepts from reference images rather than relying on low-level visual matching. CG-ICS employs a concept reasoning module powered by an MLLM to generate concept candidates, refines them via a SAM3-driven scoring function with tree search, and simultaneously uses a visual exemplar route for spatial grounding. The selected textual concepts and visual exemplars jointly activate a frozen SAM3 backbone to perform segmentation. Experiments show CG-ICS achieves state-of-the-art accuracy while significantly reducing segmentation variance across different reference inputs, thereby enhancing overall system reliability.
为解决现有上下文分割方法缺乏鲁棒性的问题，本文提出概念引导式上下文分割（CG-ICS），通过从参考图像中提取高层语义概念而非依赖低层视觉匹配来提升分割稳定性。CG-ICS采用由MLLM驱动的概念推理模块生成候选概念，结合SAM3驱动的评分函数与树搜索进行优化，并辅以视觉范例路径实现查询图像的空间定位。最终，文本概念与视觉范例共同激活冻结的SAM3主干网络进行分割。实验表明，CG-ICS在保持最先进精度的同时显著降低了不同参考输入带来的结果方差，从而大幅提升系统可靠性。
Does My Embedding Reflect That $A = B$? Evaluating Mathematical Equivalence in Embedding Models
Authors: Jiaying Ye, Samarth Rao, Leo Carlin, Kedar Chintalapati, Saharsh Bhargava, Rachit Jaiswal, Michael Zhou, Jared Darlington, Jiahe Lu, Jarod Alper, Vasily Ilin, Henry Kvinge
First: 2026-06-22T21:37:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T14:43:06+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, comments welcome
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Because mathematics is highly abstract, a single statement can take very different forms depending on what subfield it is framed in. There are many examples where breakthroughs occurred after researchers discovered that a question had already been answered in a different field. At the same time, the growth of new resources related to formalization has increased the need for tools that enable efficient and reliable navigation between mathematical 'languages' (e.g., from Lean to natural language). In this paper, we investigate whether current embedding models capture mathematical equivalence. To do this, we introduce the Mathematically Equivalent but Lexically Different Pairs (MELD) Dataset, a collection of mathematically equivalent statements that are expressed in very different language. We show that current state-of-the-art embedding models tend to group statements by the terminology used to make them instead of the underlying math. Motivated by this, we propose a contrastive approach to learning embeddings of mathematical text that focuses on aligning informal statements with different formalizations. Our experiments demonstrate that this leads to improvements not only on informal-formal retrieval tasks but also on MELD, which only contains natural language statements.
中文标题/摘要
标题：我的嵌入是否反映了 $A = B$？评估嵌入模型中的数学等价性
由于数学高度抽象，同一陈述在不同子领域中可能呈现非常不同的形式。许多突破性成果源于研究人员发现某个问题已在其他领域得到解答。同时，随着形式化相关新资源的增长，亟需工具以高效可靠地在数学‘语言’（如Lean与自然语言）之间导航。本文探讨当前嵌入模型是否能捕捉数学等价性。为此，我们引入数学等价但词汇不同的配对（MELD）数据集，其中包含语义等价但表达形式迥异的数学陈述。我们发现当前最先进的嵌入模型倾向于按所用术语分组，而非按其底层数学结构。为此，我们提出一种对比学习方法，专注于将不同形式化表述的非正式陈述对齐。实验表明，该方法不仅提升了非正式-形式化检索任务的表现，也显著改善了仅含自然语言陈述的MELD数据集的表现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to bridge mathematical expressions across different formalisms and languages, this paper evaluates whether current embedding models capture mathematical equivalence. The authors introduce the MELD dataset, containing mathematically equivalent statements with vastly different lexical forms, to test model performance. Results show that state-of-the-art models group statements by surface terminology rather than underlying mathematical meaning. To address this, the authors propose a contrastive learning method that aligns informal statements with their formal counterparts, leading to improved retrieval performance on both informal-formal tasks and the MELD dataset.
为解决跨形式数学语言间的语义对齐问题，本文评估当前嵌入模型是否能捕捉数学等价性。作者构建了MELD数据集，包含语义等价但语言表达迥异的数学命题，用于测试模型表现。实验表明，现有顶尖嵌入模型倾向于按表面术语分组而非数学本质。为此，作者提出一种对比学习方法，通过将非正式表述与形式化表述对齐，显著提升了非正式-形式化检索任务及MELD数据集上的性能。
Temporal Convolutional Autoencoder for Interference Mitigation in FMCW Radar Altimeters
Authors: Charles E. Thornton, Jamie Sloop, Samuel Brown, Aaron Orndorff, William C. Headley, Stephen Young
First: 2025-05-28T18:52:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T14:42:11+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reliable altitude estimation with frequency-modulated continuous wave (FMCW) radar altimeters is increasingly a challenge due to in-band interference from modern communication systems. In this paper, we present a temporal convolutional autoencoder (TCAE) that directly processes in-phase and quadrature (IQ) samples to suppress structured interference while preserving signal phase and frequency content for range estimation. The model is trained and initially evaluated within a full radar altimeter simulation chain, then further validated via over-the-air (OTA) experiments using a universal software radio peripheral (USRP)-based testbed. Results show that the TCAE reduces altitude estimation error by more than 85% compared to least mean squares (LMS) adaptive filtering under severe interference conditions, including low signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) and full temporal overlap between interfering and radar signals. Unlike conventional methods, the TCAE maintains phase fidelity and beat structure, enabling accurate range estimation even when interferers occupy more than one-quarter of the radar bandwidth. The implemented TCAE performs mitigation directly on fixed-length IQ windows using a single feed-forward pass and was integrated into the MATLAB/ONNX-based evaluation chain used for both simulation and OTA testing. These findings demonstrate that learned IQ-domain interference mitigation can enhance radar-altimeter resilience under a range of tested interference conditions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于FMCW雷达高度计干扰抑制的时序卷积自编码器
由于现代通信系统产生的带内干扰，基于频率调制连续波（FMCW）的雷达高度计实现可靠的高程估计正面临日益严峻的挑战。本文提出了一种时序卷积自编码器（TCAE），该模型直接处理同相与正交（IQ）采样数据，在抑制结构化干扰的同时保留信号相位和频率成分以支持测距。该模型首先在完整的雷达高度计仿真链中进行训练和初步评估，随后通过基于通用软件无线电外设（USRP）的空中（OTA）实验进一步验证。结果表明，在严重干扰条件下（包括低信干噪比SINR和干扰信号与雷达信号在时间上完全重叠），TCAE相比最小均方（LMS）自适应滤波方法可将高程估计误差降低85%以上。与传统方法不同，TCAE保持相位保真度和拍频结构，即使干扰信号占据雷达带宽的四分之一以上，仍能实现精确测距。所实现的TCAE通过单次前向传递直接对固定长度的IQ窗口进行干扰抑制，并集成至用于仿真和OTA测试的MATLAB/ONNX评估链中。这些结果表明，基于IQ域的深度学习干扰抑制方法可显著提升雷达高度计在多种干扰条件下的鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of reliable altitude estimation in FMCW radar altimeters under modern in-band interference, this paper introduces a temporal convolutional autoencoder (TCAE) that processes raw IQ samples to suppress structured interference while preserving phase and frequency content critical for range measurement. Trained and evaluated in a simulated radar chain and validated via over-the-air experiments using a USRP testbed, the TCAE significantly outperforms traditional LMS filtering, reducing altitude error by over 85% under severe interference including low SINR and full temporal overlap. The model operates on fixed-length IQ windows with a single feed-forward pass, maintaining phase fidelity even when interferers occupy more than a quarter of the radar bandwidth, thereby enabling accurate range estimation in previously problematic scenarios.
为解决FMCW雷达高度计在现代通信系统干扰下的可靠高度估计问题，本文提出一种时域卷积自编码器（TCAE），直接处理IQ原始数据以抑制结构化干扰，同时保留用于测距的关键相位和频率信息。该模型在仿真雷达链路中训练并评估，随后通过基于USRP的OTA实验验证。结果表明，在严重干扰条件下（如低SINR和完全时域重叠），TCAE相比LMS滤波器将高度估计误差降低超过85%，且在干扰占据雷达带宽四分之一以上时仍能保持相位保真度。模型对固定长度IQ窗口进行单次前向传递，可无缝集成至MATLAB/ONNX评估链路中。
Autoencoder Architectures for Athlete Performance Scoring from Wearable Telemetry
Authors: Mateusz Kubita, Jan Zubalewicz, Krzysztof Siwek
First: 2026-06-26T14:41:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T14:41:24+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 3 figures, submitted to SPA 2026 Conference https://spaconference.org.pl/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Wearable devices produce large, high dimensional training logs for everyday runners, and interpretation rather than data collection is now the limiting step. This paper evaluates five dimensionality reduction models, three autoencoder variants, PCA, and a Variational Autoencoder, on their ability to compress nine sensor runner profiles into a single scalar performance indicator, the latent score. Because the setting is fully unsupervised, model quality is assessed along two complementary axes: reconstruction error (Mean Squared Error) and latent score interpretability, measured via Spearman and Kendall rank correlations, Mutual Information, and Permutation Importance. These are combined into a composite selection criterion that prevents selecting models on reconstruction accuracy alone. Feature rankings from the four metrics are aggregated via a modified Borda count, and their stability is confirmed by bootstrap validation. A two feature linear baseline is included to anchor the comparison. Deep autoencoder achieved the lowest reconstruction error and the highest composite score. Once the PCA hidden layers were widened, the deeper variants became closely competitive with Deep AE on the composite criterion, indicating that the limiting factor was hidden layer capacity rather than the one dimensional bottleneck. Running pace, aerobic decoupling, and average heart rate emerged as the dominant latent score drivers across all models and resampling runs, consistent with established physiology.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于可穿戴遥测数据的运动员表现评分的自编码器架构
可穿戴设备为日常跑步者生成大量高维训练日志，当前限制因素已从数据收集转向数据解读。本文评估了五种降维模型——三种自编码器变体、PCA和变分自编码器——在将九种传感器跑步数据压缩为单一标量表现指标（潜在得分）的能力。由于该场景完全无监督，模型质量从两个互补维度评估：重建误差（均方误差）和潜在得分可解释性，通过斯皮尔曼和肯德尔秩相关、互信息及排列重要性衡量。这些指标组合成综合选择标准，避免仅依据重建精度选模型。四类指标的特征排名通过改进的博达计数法聚合，并通过自助法验证其稳定性。包含一个两特征线性基线以锚定比较。深度自编码器重建误差最低、综合得分最高。当PCA隐藏层宽度增加后，深度变体在综合标准上与深度自编码器接近，表明限制因素是隐藏层容量而非一维瓶颈。跑步配速、有氧解耦和平均心率在所有模型和重采样运行中均成为主导潜在得分驱动因素，与已建立的生理学一致。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to interpret high-dimensional wearable sensor data for runners without relying on labeled outcomes, this study evaluates five dimensionality reduction methods—including three autoencoder variants, PCA, and a Variational Autoencoder—to distill nine sensor streams into a single scalar performance score. Model quality is assessed via reconstruction error and latent score interpretability using multiple metrics, combined into a composite criterion to avoid over-reliance on reconstruction accuracy alone. Feature importance rankings from these metrics are aggregated using a modified Borda count and validated via bootstrap. The deep autoencoder achieved the lowest reconstruction error and highest composite score, while widened PCA layers made deeper models nearly competitive, suggesting hidden layer capacity—not bottleneck dimensionality—is the key constraint. Across all models and resampling runs, running pace, aerobic decoupling, and average heart rate consistently ranked as the most influential latent drivers.
为了解决穿戴设备产生的高维跑步传感器数据难以解释的问题，本研究评估了五种降维模型（包括三种自编码器变体、PCA和变分自编码器），旨在将九个传感器数据流压缩为一个单一的性能评分。模型质量通过重建误差和潜在评分可解释性（如秩相关、互信息和排列重要性）进行评估，并结合成一个综合选择标准，避免仅依据重建精度做决策。各指标的特征排名通过改进的博达计数法聚合，并经自助法验证稳定性。深度自编码器在重建误差和综合评分上表现最优，而扩大PCA隐藏层容量后，深度自编码器与之几乎持平，表明限制因素是隐藏层容量而非一维瓶颈。所有模型和重采样运行中，跑步配速、有氧解耦和平均心率始终是影响潜在评分的主要驱动因素。
Monocular Avatar Reconstruction via Cascaded Diffusion Priors and UV-Space Differentiable Shading
Authors: Hong Li, Minqi Meng, Yanjun Liang, Chongjie Ye, Houyuan Chen, Weiqing Xiao, Xianda Guo, Guojun Lei, Xuhui Liu, Chaojie Yang, Yanlun Peng, Hao Zhao, Baochang Zhang
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-26T14:41:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T14:41:13+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ECCV 2026. Project page: https://luh1124.github.io/MARCUS-Avatar-Projectpage/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Reconstructing high-fidelity, relightable 3D avatars from a single in-the-wild image is a challenging ill-posed problem, primarily hindered by the scarcity of high-quality PBR data and the complexity of disentangling illumination from intrinsic materials. In this paper, we present a data-efficient framework that leverages the robust priors of a unified pre-trained diffusion backbone to sequentially address texture completion, delighting, and material decomposition. Unlike existing methods that rely on fragmented pipelines or extensive proprietary datasets, we utilize cascaded Low-Rank Adaptations (LoRAs) to adapt the strong generative prior of the diffusion model for each sub-task in UV space. Specifically, we first employ an Inpainting LoRA to complete missing UV textures caused by occlusion, leveraging the model's semantic understanding to generate semantically and photometrically coherent details. Subsequently, a Light-Homogenization LoRA and a novel Cross-Intrinsic Attention mechanism are introduced to remove baked-in lighting and collaboratively synthesize pixel-aligned PBR maps (Albedo, Normal, Roughness, Specular, and Displacement). To ensure physical plausibility, we impose a UV-space differentiable BRDF shading loss during the decomposition stage, forcing the generative process to adhere to the rendering equation without the artifacts typical of rasterization-based supervision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method, trained on fewer than 100 real 3D scans, generates comprehensive, 4K-resolution PBR assets with superior realism and generalization compared to state-of-the-art methods, and all training code and model weights will be released upon acceptance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过级联扩散先验与UV空间可微分着色实现单目头像重建
从单张野外图像重建高保真、可重光照的3D头像是一项具有挑战性的不适定问题，主要受限于高质量PBR数据的稀缺性以及光照与内在材质分离的复杂性。本文提出一种数据高效的框架，利用统一预训练扩散主干的强先验，依次解决纹理补全、光照统一和材质分解任务。与现有依赖碎片化流程或大量专有数据集的方法不同，我们采用级联低秩适配（LoRAs）在UV空间中为每个子任务适配扩散模型的强生成先验。具体而言，首先使用Inpainting LoRA完成因遮挡导致的缺失UV纹理，利用模型语义理解生成语义与光度一致的细节；随后引入Light-Homogenization LoRA与新颖的Cross-Intrinsic Attention机制，去除烘焙光照并协同合成像素对齐的PBR贴图（基础色、法线、粗糙度、高光、位移）。为确保物理合理性，我们在分解阶段引入UV空间可微分BRDF着色损失，迫使生成过程遵循渲染方程，避免基于光栅化监督的典型伪影。大量实验表明，仅用少于100个真实3D扫描数据训练，本方法生成的4K分辨率PBR资产在真实感与泛化能力上优于现有最先进方法，所有训练代码与模型权重将在录用后公开。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of reconstructing high-fidelity, relightable 3D avatars from a single image due to limited PBR data and illumination-material ambiguity, this work proposes a data-efficient framework using cascaded LoRAs adapted in UV space to sequentially handle texture completion, lighting removal, and material decomposition. It first uses an inpainting LoRA to fill occluded UV textures, then applies a light-homogenization LoRA with a novel cross-intrinsic attention module to generate aligned PBR maps. A differentiable BRDF shading loss enforces physical plausibility during decomposition. Experiments show that trained on fewer than 100 real scans, the method produces 4K-resolution PBR assets with superior realism and generalization compared to prior approaches.
为了解决单张图像重建高保真可重光照3D虚拟形象时因高质量PBR数据稀缺和光照与材质耦合导致的难题，本文提出一种数据高效的框架，通过在UV空间中使用级联LoRA逐步完成纹理补全、光照去除与材质分解。首先用Inpainting LoRA填补遮挡导致的UV纹理缺失，再结合Light-Homogenization LoRA与新型Cross-Intrinsic Attention生成对齐的PBR贴图，同时通过可微分BRDF着色损失确保物理合理性。实验表明，仅用不到100个真实3D扫描数据训练，该方法即可生成4K分辨率的PBR资产，其真实感与泛化能力优于现有方法。
Deep Residual Networks Learn the Geodesic Curve in the Wasserstein Space
Authors: Kuo Gai, Shihua Zhang
First: 2021-02-18T09:37:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T14:41:00+00:00
Comments: 35 pages, 16 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent studies revealed the mathematical connection between deep neural networks (DNNs) and dynamic systems. However, the specific dynamics that DNNs, especially deep residual networks (ResNets), tend to learn during training remain insufficiently characterized. To this end, we model the forward propagation of deep residual networks using continuity equations, in which the measure is conserved and infinite curves in the measure space connect the input distribution to the output one of a ResNet. We find ResNets with $L_2$ regularization attempt to learn the geodesic curve in the Wasserstein space, induced by the optimal transport map. Compared with plain networks, ResNets can better approximate the geodesic curve, which explains why ResNets can be optimized and generalize better. Numerical experiments show that the data tracks of a ResNet tend to be line-shaped in terms of the line-shape score, and the map learned by a ResNet is closer to the optimal transport map in terms of the optimal transport score. In a word, we conclude that ResNets learn the geodesic curve in the Wasserstein space and discretely engineer the data transformation in high-dimensional spaces.
中文标题/摘要
标题：深度残差网络在Wasserstein空间中学习测地线曲线
近期研究表明深度神经网络（DNN）与动态系统之间存在数学联系。然而，DNN（尤其是深度残差网络ResNets）在训练过程中所学习的具体动力学仍缺乏充分刻画。为此，我们利用连续性方程建模深度残差网络的前向传播，其中测度守恒，测度空间中的无限曲线连接输入分布与ResNet输出分布。我们发现带有L2正则化的ResNets倾向于学习由最优传输映射诱导的Wasserstein空间中的测地线曲线。与普通网络相比，ResNets能更好地逼近测地线曲线，这解释了为何ResNets更容易优化并具有更好的泛化能力。数值实验表明，ResNet的数据轨迹在“线性形状得分”上更趋近于直线，且其学习到的映射在“最优传输得分”上更接近最优传输映射。总之，我们得出结论：ResNets在Wasserstein空间中学习测地线曲线，并在高维空间中离散化地工程化数据变换。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand the implicit dynamics of deep residual networks (ResNets), this study models their forward propagation using continuity equations in the Wasserstein space, where measure conservation links input and output distributions. The authors find that ResNets with L2 regularization approximate the geodesic curve induced by optimal transport, outperforming plain networks in this task. Experimental results confirm that ResNet data trajectories exhibit line-like shapes and their learned mappings closely align with optimal transport maps, supporting the conclusion that ResNets implicitly learn Wasserstein geodesics, enabling better optimization and generalization.
为理解深度残差网络的隐式动态，该研究通过Wasserstein空间中的连续性方程建模其前向传播，其中测度守恒且路径连接输入与输出分布。研究发现，带有L2正则化的残差网络倾向于逼近由最优传输诱导的测地线，优于普通网络。实验表明，残差网络的数据轨迹呈现线性形状，其学习到的映射更接近最优传输映射，证实残差网络在高维空间中离散化实现了测地线变换。
MixTTA: Low-Rank Cross-Channel Mixing for Reliable Test-Time Adaptation
Authors: Mansoo Jung, Youngwook Kim, Jungwoo Lee
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-26T14:40:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T14:40:36+00:00
Comments: To be published in the 19th European Conference on Computer Vision -- ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) methods commonly update the affine parameters of normalization layers to adapt deployed models under distribution shifts. However, per-channel affine parameters perform axis-aligned scaling and shifting, making them geometrically incapable of correcting cross-channel structural changes induced by distribution shift. To address this limitation, we propose MixTTA, a lightweight plug-in module that equips normalization layers with a low-rank cross-channel transformation, enabling inter-channel mixing at each layer. To ensure that the low-rank branch captures only cross-channel interactions, we also propose Decoupling Projection that enforces strict separation from the diagonal affine path, along with Spectral Projection that prevents rank-1 collapse under non-stationary test streams. MixTTA can be seamlessly integrated into any existing normalization-based TTA method. Experiments in both standard and wild TTA settings show consistent improvements over strong baselines while mitigating adaptation failure under challenging conditions. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/delta6189/MixTTA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MixTTA：低秩跨通道混合用于可靠的测试时自适应
测试时自适应（TTA）方法通常更新归一化层的仿射参数，以适应部署模型在分布偏移下的表现。然而，每通道仿射参数仅能进行轴对齐的缩放和偏移，几何上无法纠正分布偏移引起的跨通道结构变化。为解决此局限，我们提出MixTTA，一种轻量级插件模块，为归一化层配备低秩跨通道变换，使每层均可实现通道间混合。为确保低秩分支仅捕获跨通道交互，我们还提出解耦投影，强制其与对角仿射路径严格分离，以及谱投影，防止在非平稳测试流下发生秩1坍缩。MixTTA可无缝集成至任何现有基于归一化的TTA方法。在标准与野外TTA设置下的实验均显示其优于强基线方法，同时在挑战性条件下缓解自适应失败。源代码公开于https://github.com/delta6189/MixTTA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the geometric limitations of per-channel affine updates in Test-Time Adaptation, which fail to correct cross-channel structural shifts, the paper introduces MixTTA: a lightweight module that injects low-rank cross-channel transformations into normalization layers to enable inter-channel mixing. It employs Decoupling Projection to isolate cross-channel interactions from diagonal affine updates and Spectral Projection to prevent rank-1 collapse under non-stationary test streams. Experiments across standard and wild TTA settings demonstrate consistent performance gains over strong baselines and improved robustness against adaptation failure in challenging conditions.
为解决测试时适应中单通道仿射更新无法处理跨通道结构变化的问题，本文提出MixTTA：一种轻量级模块，通过在归一化层中引入低秩跨通道变换实现通道间混合。该方法采用解耦投影确保跨通道交互与对角仿射路径分离，并用谱投影防止非平稳测试流下的秩一坍塌。在标准与野外TTA设置下，MixTTA稳定优于强基线方法，有效降低复杂分布偏移下的适应失败率，且可无缝集成现有归一化基线TTA框架。
